# Manipulation Skill Learning — Deep Dive

> [!abstract] Overview
> Manipulation is where embodied AI meets the unforgiving physics of contact: a policy must turn pixels and language into joint commands that grasp, insert, pour, and assemble — under occlusion, latency, and multimodal action ambiguity. This note maps the *skill-learning* substrate beneath the VLA headline: how policies **generate actions** (diffusion/flow backbones, real-time chunking, guided sampling, 3D & multi-view spatial encoders), how they **reason and remember** (world-model-as-policy, episodic memory for non-Markovian tasks, LLM/neuro-symbolic planning and affordance grounding), and what **learning signal** trains them (RL & policy-steering, demonstration/data-generation, and cross-embodiment skill transfer). The throughline: the action-generation backbone, the memory it carries, and the data it learns from are now the three independent axes a manipulation system is designed along — and the field's frontier is the *interaction* between them.

Three lanes. Generative policies cover how the action distribution is modelled ([[2406.01586|ManiCM]] to [[2509.01819|ManiFlow]]) and how fast it can be run, where [[2506.07339|RTC]]'s real-time chunking spawned three independent strategies. Spatial grounding carries two parallel lines: 3D action representation from [[2209.05451|PerAct]] to [[2410.17488|GenDP]], and equivariance from [[2210.03094|VIMA]] to [[2601.17486|EquiForm]]. Bimanual manipulation is the youngest lane and still a single thread.

## Part A — Generating Actions

*How a manipulation policy turns observations into motor commands — the generative backbone, the latency it must beat, and the spatial structure it encodes.*

### 1. Generative Policy Architectures

A manipulation policy must map an observation to a *distribution* over actions, not a point — the same scene admits many valid grasps, and averaging them (the failure of naive regression) produces motion that satisfies no mode. This is why generative models took over the policy backbone: diffusion and flow-matching natively represent multimodal action distributions, and they degrade gracefully under demonstration noise. The category exists because expressivity is the first-order constraint — get the distribution wrong and no amount of reasoning above it recovers.

But expressivity is bought with iteration: a denoising or integration loop runs at inference, and that loop fights the real-time control budget. The four sub-sections below are the four pressures pulling on the backbone: which *generative process* to use (diffusion vs flow vs interpolant), how to compensate for the *latency* the process introduces, how to *steer* the samples toward safe or task-consistent behavior, and how to keep the whole thing *efficient and history-aware* so it survives non-Markovian tasks. Every modern manipulation policy is a point in this 4D space.

#### 1.1 Diffusion & Flow-Matching Backbones

The generative process at the policy's core — denoising-diffusion, flow-matching, or stochastic interpolants — sets the trade-off between expressivity, sampling speed, and the stability guarantees you can prove.

- **[[2607.24296|PAC-DP]]** — A ==PAC-Bayesian diffusion policy== adding a ==KL-divergence regularizer== to the denoising loss, with the noise-prediction net replaced by a ==Bayesian Neural Network== under ==factorized-Gaussian== priors; **−67%** noise-prediction error, **93.7%** vs **86.1%** PushT, **0.738** vs **0.401** ToolHang at **20** demos, **100%** over **200** real trials.
- **[[2607.14725|BridgeFlow]]** — An ==SE(2)-equivariant== flow-matching planner whose ==Brownian-bridge prior== straightens the flow field, with a lightweight ==task-centric canonicalization== module replacing an equivariant architecture; **100%** SR in **2** steps (**0.0071 s**, **15×** faster than diffusion), **74.85%** valid rate on transformed 2D tasks, **80.0%** OOD 7-DoF Franka.
- **[[2607.14424|ConFlow]]** — Folds ==differentiable constraint functions== into the ==flow-matching objective==, activated only on constraint-violating (negative) demos, and swaps the Gaussian source for a ==conditional Gaussian Process==; **0.016** collision rate with no inference guidance (vs **0.031** standard FM), the CGP alone cutting collisions **~46%** and start error **0.0267→0.0006**.
- **[[2607.10504|SUREFlow]]** — A linear-complexity ==Mamba state-space== policy backbone whose ==Uncertainty-aware Residual Flow== head jointly predicts ==action velocities== and input-dependent residual uncertainty, then runs ==closed-loop refinement== correcting only unreliable action dimensions; **92.5%** LIBERO (**+17.4%** over Octo, **+34.2%** over MaIL), **88.5%** real.
- **[[2607.08575|FabriVLA]]** — A lightweight VLA built on an ==InternVL3.5-1B== backbone with a ==flow-matching action head==, adding ==gated self-attention== over action steps + ==shallow VLM layer fusion== for spatial context, trained via ==single-stage joint optimization==; **90.0%** tier-avg / **92.0%** episode SR on Meta-World MT50 at **0.89B** params, gated attention alone **+9.2pp**.
- **[[2607.04816|CAC-VLA]]** — A VLA training the VLM to predict ==latent actions== (encoded future action segments) as a native interface, modulated by a ==Context-Gated Action Conditioning== module into a ==flow-matching== action expert with a latent-alignment loss; **98.3%** LIBERO, **89.5%** LIBERO-Plus (vs ACoT-VLA's 88.0%), **+48pp** real pick-and-place full SR over π0.5.
- **[[2607.04171|XS-VLA]]** — A lightweight framework ==distilling== coarse spatial grounding from a Qwen3-VL-4B teacher into a 0.25B SmolVLM2 student via ==direction-oriented discrete prediction==, then decoding actions with a ==CVAE== + ==Interleaved Attention Flow Matching Transformer==; **90.0%** LIBERO (**+7.2%** over lightweight SOTA), **13.3×** faster mission execution.
- **[[2607.03693|CoRE-VLA]]** — A VLA framing action generation as context-conditioned sparse computation via ==Conditional Routing of Experts== over an ==Action Diffusion Transformer==, with ==modality dropout== simulating sensor absence; **97.0%** LIBERO-Long without depth, **56.5%** RoboCasa GR1 Tabletop, real Vegetables-Picking **65%** vs baseline's **35%**.
- **[[2606.27144|PAMAE]]** — A plug-and-play module replacing a flow-matching VLA's single action expert with a sparse ==Mixture-of-Experts== under a ==phase-aware router== (context + flow time + execution cue), two-stage trained with phase-supervised routing on pseudo labels; **+9.2%** on π⁰ (73.8→83.0%) / **+5.6%** on π⁰·⁵ over 5 multi-stage tasks, **89.0%** phase-dominance purity.
- **[[2606.23420|LAFM]]** — A flow-matching policy replacing the fixed isotropic Gaussian source with a ==dynamic library of learned priors==, one per discrete latent motion primitive picked by a pre-trained ==Latent Action Model==, trained with an adaptive probability path + latent cross-entropy + KL; **86.7%** real (**+23.4%** over Flow Matching, **+15.0%** over π₀) and **93.0%** on LIBERO-90.
- **[[2606.21935|CoRDE]]** — A structure-guided ==variational diffusion distillation== framework routing a ==LoRA diffusion-expert pool== via ==concept-prior responsibility== (EM-style soft alignment) to prevent routing collapse; highest macro-average over all four LIBERO suites, matching the DP teacher on D3IL with preserved Task-Entropy, **2.63 ms** single-step inference vs DP's 55.62 ms.
- **[[2606.21600|VQActFlow]]** — A two-stage policy pairing a ==VQ-VAE action tokenizer== with a ==Variational Flow Matching== head keeping explicit logits over discrete action modes for inference-time steering via ==CFG== + a contrastive codebook critic; **80.5%** LIBERO-90 (vs 77.2% continuous-flow DiT), **23.8→57.5%** on a Unitree G1 via CFG, **77.5%** ALOHA bimanual with ~100× lower peak jerk.
- **[[2606.19194|INN-Adapter]]** — An ==invertible neural network adapter== for one-step flow matching that maps a denoised action estimate into a ==normalized spherical latent space== then inverts it, preserving structural consistency without auxiliary losses; **97.4%** average SR in one step on large-scale VLA tasks, **45%** lower real latency (110→61 ms), converging in 300 vs 2000 epochs.
- **[[2606.17408|LeaP]]** — A ==learnable proprioception-conditioned diagonal Gaussian prior== replacing the standard-Gaussian source distribution, anchoring action generation in a state-dependent region via a lightweight MLP; **81.6%** over 15 sim tasks (**+6.5–25.5pp**) and **80.0%** real at **1.6%** parameter overhead; reframes the source distribution as independently designable.
- **[[2606.14535|SCDP-Spatial]]** — A ==spatial conditioning== diffusion policy using evolving end-effector trajectories as ==dynamic attention anchors== projected onto the image plane to query multi-scale features, single global RGB camera only; **87.2%** over 54 Meta-World/DexArt tasks vs DP3's 82.3% (with depth), **73.8%** real on USB-insertion and distractor-heavy scenes.
- **[[2605.23477|SMoDP]]** — A ==Semantically Structured Mixture-of-Experts Diffusion Policy== pairing an offline VLM skill-abstraction module (verb-noun skill labels) with ==chunk-consistent skill-based MoE routing== and ==dual semantic contrastive alignment==; **0.97** LIBERO-10 / **0.95** LIBERO-90, **91.25%** real ALOHA vs MoDE's 47.50%, matching full-data MoDE at **50%** the demos.
- **[[2605.31286|DeMaVLA]]** — A VLA foundation model for deformable-object folding pairing a ==Qwen3-VL backbone== with a ==layer-aligned pruned action expert== over ==conditional flow matching==, pre-trained on 5,000h dual-arm data + ==Training-time RTC== + human-gated DAgger; **92.5%** real multi-garment folding (vs π₀'s 76.3%), **100%** shirt-folding at full data scale.
- **[[2605.04525|HDFlow]]** — A hierarchical planner pairing a ==diffusion== high-level subgoal generator with ==rectified-flow== low-level trajectory synthesis, guided by a ==manifold-aware Energy-Based Model==; **55%** FurnitureBench cabinet and **68%** lamp at 88 ms/step, competitive across 18 RLBench/OGBench tasks with real Franka transfer.
- **[[2604.23620|Move-Then-Operate]]** — A hierarchically-gated dual-expert VLA splitting manipulation into ==E_Move (coarse relocation)== and ==E_Operate (fine contact)== experts via an MLP router, trained with ==Conditional Flow Matching== + MLLM phase auto-labeling; **68.9%** over 8 RoboTwin2 tasks (**+24.1%** over π₀) at 50 demos, random routing collapsing it to 25.63%.
- **[[2509.01819|ManiFlow]]** — A general flow policy pairing ==continuous-time consistency training== (single-stage, teacherless) with a ==DiT-X transformer== applying AdaLN-Zero to cross-attention; **+98.3%** relative over DP3 across 8 real single-arm/bimanual/humanoid tasks, **63.7%** at 1 step / **64.5%** at 2 steps on bimanual; high-quality action in 1–2 inference steps.
- **[[2509.19712|TopoCut]]** — A ==pose-invariant spectral reward== (Laplacian eigen-descriptors) guides MPPI data collection and trains a ==conditional discrete diffusion policy (PDDP)== segmenting cuttable point-wise regions via a dynamics-informed perception encoder; **85%** test accuracy on multi-step cutting, generalizing where CD/EMD/HD reward metrics fail.
- **[[2509.16063|DSPv2]]** — A whole-body mobile-manipulation policy extending Dense Policy with a ==Sparse 3D Encoder== + ==LoRA-tuned DINOv2== fused via a ==Q-former==, decoded by a ==bidirectional autoregressive Dense Head== for the 25-DoF system; **80%** pick / **60%** place and **100%** deliver real vs WB-VIMA's 50%/25%/45%, robust to table-height/lighting/object shifts.
- **[[2507.17846|PinchBot]]** — A goal-conditioned ==diffusion policy== for long-horizon pinch-pottery deformable shaping, fusing ==PointBERT 3D point-cloud embeddings== with ==task-progress guidance== and a ==collision action projection== constraining actions to the clay diameter; PointBERT halves the actions vs DP3-PointNet variants, with progress guidance improving multi-diameter bowl control.
- **[[2507.13231|VITA-world-model]]** — A noise-free, conditioning-free flow-matching policy flowing directly from a latent visual representation to latent actions via a dimension-matched ==Action Autoencoder==, stabilized by ==Flow Latent Decoding== backpropagating reconstruction loss through the ODE solver; **1.5-2×** faster, **−18.6-28.7%** memory, matching DP/ACT on 9 sim + 5 real tasks.
- **[[2506.09990|Chain-of-Action]]** — A ==trajectory-autoregressive policy== generating actions by ==backward reasoning from a goal keyframe==, with continuous action tokens, latent-consistency loss, multi-token prediction, and a dynamic stop; **+16.3%** over ACT / **+23.2%** over DP on RLBench-60, **+15%** real Fetch, widening gains as spatial variance grows.
- **[[2606.09758|DARP]]** — A semi-parametric ==retrieval-based== imitation policy conditioning on ==difference vectors== between a query state and its k-nearest expert neighbors with ==permutation-invariant aggregation==, implicitly applying manifold regularization; **85%** vs BC's 54% on RoboCasa Drawer, **92%** vs 44% on real furniture assembly, **70%** vs 48% on Push-T.
- **[[2506.06690|SpikePingpong]]** — A high-speed table-tennis system pairing a ==Fast-Slow== perception stack (physics prediction + spike-camera neural error correction) with an ==IMPACT imitation== striking policy; **12.34 mm** interception MAE, **92%** SR in 30 cm zones (vs humans' 53%), **78%** over 100 sequential returns.
- **[[2410.12557|Shortcut Models]]** — Conditions a ==flow-matching== network on the desired ==step size==, trained with a joint flow-matching + ==self-consistency== loss (a 2d-step equals two d-steps) enabling accurate large jumps; **1-step** ImageNet-256 FID **10.6**, one-step robotic policies (Push-T, Transport) matching iterative diffusion at **10×** less inference cost.
- **[[2407.05996|MDT]]** — A multimodal-goal diffusion policy pairing a ==Transformer encoder== + ==diffusion decoder== with ==Masked Generative Foresight== and ==Contrastive Latent Alignment== self-supervision; **~85%** on CALVIN 5-instruction chains and **65%** on LIBERO with only **2** labeled demos; the multimodal-goal diffusion baseline.
- **[[2606.03834|SFMDS]]** — A ==flow-matching dynamical systems== policy constrained by ==LaSalle's invariance principle== for provable stability; **100%** asymptotic stability on LASA vs BC's 32–40% failure, **<0.13%** non-convergent on 6D pouring; real SE(3) insertion at ~13 Hz.
- **[[2601.09031|RGMP-S]]** — A humanoid manipulation framework pairing a ==Long-horizon Geometric-prior Skill Selector== (VLM + Geometry-Grounded CoT) with a ==Recursive Adaptive Spiking Network== (GMM action head) for data-efficient action generation; **0.89** zero-shot grasp SR at **75.2 Hz**, **5×** data efficiency, **+12%** over Dex-VLA.
- **[[2511.09141|RGMP]]** — An end-to-end humanoid policy fusing a ==Geometric-prior Skill Selector== (VLM + object bounding-box priors) with an ==Adaptive Recursive Gaussian Network== (GMM) refining 6-DoF actions from few demos; **87%** unseen-object SR vs Diffusion Policy's 70% and Dex-VLA's 77%, **5×** data efficiency (98% from 40 samples).
- **[[2602.07322|A2A]]** — An ==action-to-action flow matching== policy that initializes generation from past proprioceptive actions, not noise; **0.56 ms** single-step inference, **100%** success within 40 epochs and **30 trajectories**; robust to visual perturbation.
- **[[2602.04215|OAT]]** — An ==Ordered Action Tokenization== transformer autoencoder using register tokens + ==Finite Scalar Quantization== with ==nested dropout== + ==causal attention== for coarse-to-fine ordering and ==prefix-based decoding==; **56.3%** LIBERO / **73.1%** RoboMimic matching diffusion, monotonic **11.7%→56.3%** anytime scaling with token count.
- **[[2512.19347|OMP]]** — A one-step ==MeanFlow== policy with ==Directional Alignment Learning== (decoupling velocity magnitude/direction to counter spectral bias) and a ==Differential Derivation Equation== finite-difference approx replacing JVPs; **82.3%** over Adroit/Meta-World (vs MP1's 78.9%), **+15%** real Slip-Ring, ~7.5 GB memory savings.
- **[[2512.07212|BridgePolicy]]** — A visuomotor policy recasting action generation as a ==diffusion bridge== whose forward process maps action to its observation, reconstructing from an ==observation-embedded terminal state==, with ==Cross-Attention fusion== + ==CLIP contrastive== alignment; best average over 52 Adroit/DexArt/MetaWorld tasks, **0.90** real Franka SR (vs DP3's 0.76).
- **[[2606.06049|L-SDPPO]]** — A ==spiking diffusion policy== with PPO fine-tuning and ==State-Dependent Latency Injection==; **97%** success on microgravity manipulation at **36.45%** of an ANN diffusion policy's energy; energy-efficient backbone for power-constrained robots.
- **[[2605.24924|Koopman-Distillation-for]]** — Compresses iterative diffusion-policy denoising into one forward pass via a ==Factorized Dynamic Koopman== layer giving state-dependent latent transitions, trained with teacher-fidelity + control-oriented losses; **172x** sim / **37x** real (**4.08 ms**) speedup, **100%** real obstacle-avoidance success.
- **[[2605.13959|WarmPrior]]** — A method that ==straightens flow-matching policies with temporal priors==, reducing pathwise curvature and branching cost for fewer-step inference; up to **+11.2%** on Transport-MH at **NFE=1** across Robomimic/MimicGen, with consistent real Franka Research 3 gains largest on precision-demanding tasks.
- **[[2604.08418|DMBN-PTE]]** — A diagnosis-and-fix showing modality-blending encoders *silence* temporal input, then adding ==Positional Time Encoding==; cuts time-reconstruction loss from **29.63e-3** to **0.69e-3**, restoring sequence-order awareness multimodal action predictors need.
- **[[2507.10543|MP1]]** — A flow policy adapting the ==MeanFlow paradigm== to learn an ==interval-averaged velocity field== for one-step (1-NFE) generation via the MeanFlow Identity (no consistency constraints), plus a ==Dispersive Loss== regularizer; **78.9%** over 37 Adroit/Meta-World tasks (**+7.3%** over FlowPolicy, **+10.2%** over DP3) at **6.8 ms**.
- **[[2506.14769|CDP]]** — A ==Causal Action Generation Module== conditions diffusion action prediction on ==historical actions== via ==Causal Temporal Attention==, trained with ==Historical Actions Re-Denoising== and a ==Cache Sharing Mechanism== for chunk-wise autoregressive inference; **+5–20pp** SR over DP/DP3 across Adroit/DexArt/MetaWorld, robust to degraded point clouds.
- **[[2506.09422|Time-Unified]]** — A ==time-unified diffusion policy== (TUDP) learning a single velocity field for all denoising steps plus an ==Action Discrimination Network== scoring noisy actions to guide denoising toward successful neighborhoods, two-stage trained; **82.6%** average over 18 RLBench tasks (SOTA, avg rank 1.9), 51/60 real UR5 from 58 demos with far fewer iterations.
- **[[2506.08822|FreqPolicy]]** — A ==conditional flow-matching== policy with ==frequency-consistency constraints== (DCT of action velocities) for one-step (**NFE=1**) generation; OpenVLA-FreqPolicy **94.8%** vs OpenVLA-DP's 68.1% at **5×** faster, **70%** real fruit-sorting at **93.5 Hz**.
- **[[2602.12032|GAP]]** — A diagnosis-and-fix showing vision-proprioception BC *suppresses* the visual modality during motion-transition phases, then it ==down-modulates proprioceptive gradients== via change-point detection; **94.2%** Meta-World pick-place, **+17%** on Octo-VP, model-agnostic across MLP/diffusion/transformer.
- **[[2505.11123|Condition-Dependent-Flow]]** — A ==condition-dependent source distribution== for conditional flow matching (Cocos) that cures diffusion-policy ==loss collapse== by anchoring noise around condition semantics; **2.14×** faster training, **+8.3%** LIBERO and **+25.7%** MetaWorld SR; plug-and-play onto existing VLA backbones.
- **[[2505.01179|COT-Policy]]** — A ==Conditional Optimal Transport== flow-matching policy whose OT cost incorporates the observation condition (encoder + K-means discretization) to pair noise-target samples unbiasedly, straightening 1-2-step integration; **81.8%** sim manipulation at 2 steps (~**10×** faster than Diffusion Policy at 20 NFE), preserving action multimodality on a real KUKA IIWA.
- **[[2502.09029|MTDP]]** — A ==Modulated Transformer Diffusion Policy== improving condition integration via a ==Modulated Attention== module that modulates self-attention + feedforward layers with guiding parameters alongside cross-attention; **+12%** Toolhang / **+4%** avg over 6 tasks vs Transformer-DP, **−40%** sampling steps with DDIM, generalizing to UNet (MUDP).
- **[[2502.07837|RoboBERT]]** — A lightweight 208M end-to-end model fusing ==BERT language== + ==CLIP ViT== + a ==CNN diffusion action head==, trained in a two-stage paradigm separating policy learning from linguistic generalization plus Robotic-Mixup augmentation; **4.52** mean length on CALVIN ABCD→D, **80–92%** real on a REALMAN arm over MT-ACT/RT-1.
- **[[2503.05316|CoinRobot]]** — A generalized end-to-end framework wrapping a ==DDIM diffusion policy== (FPN-ResNet34 perception) in a modular ==Translation Module== that unifies robot/sensor messages into JSON for hardware agnosticism; **64%** PickPlace (vs ACT/LeRobot-DP's 0–30%), **20%** zero-shot to unseen camera views where a fixed-view baseline scores 0%.
- **[[2503.02587|Dexterous-In-Hand-Manipulation-Multifingered]]** — A ==visuomotor diffusion policy== for in-hand dexterous tasks trained on AR-teleoperated Allegro-Hand demos (Meta Quest 3 retargeting) with a ==two-stage HDBSCAN/GLOSH outlier removal== filtering low-quality demonstrations; **85%** bottle-lid unscrew with wrist-cam + joint-effort, dropping to 30% without effort sensing.
- **[[2502.02853|Rethinking-Latent-Redundancy-Behavior]]** — A diagnosis-and-fix integrating the ==Information Bottleneck== into behavior cloning via a ==mutual-information regularizer== (MINE-estimated) that compresses the input→latent path to cut redundancy while keeping action-relevant signal; consistent gains across CortexBench/LIBERO and real tasks, **+6%** on long-horizon Diffusion Policy.
- **[[2410.21257|OneDP]]** — A ==diffusion-distillation== method collapsing a pretrained multi-step diffusion policy into a ==single-step implicit generator== via a KL-divergence score-matching loss (stochastic and deterministic variants); **62.5 Hz** real action frequency (**42×** over Diffusion Policy), **0.843** avg sim SR matching the 100-step teacher, **100%** under human interference.
- **[[2409.14411|ScaleDP]]** — A ==diffusion-transformer policy scaled to 1B params== replacing cross-attention observation fusion with ==AdaLN== + non-causal attention to stabilize training at scale; **+21.6%** MetaWorld over DP-T, **+36.25%** real single-arm and **+75%** bimanual, with stronger data-scaling and visual generalization.
- **[[2405.07503|Consistency-Policy]]** — A two-stage policy distilling an EDM diffusion teacher into a student via a ==Consistency Trajectory Model== loss for single/few-step inference along the Probability-Flow ODE; **1–3 NFE** (vs 9–27+ for Diffusion Policy) at **1 ms** sim / **20 ms** real while matching diffusion SR; the canonical consistency-distillation policy.
- **[[2502.12371|IMLE-Policy]]** — A single-step visuomotor policy adapting ==Implicit Maximum Likelihood Estimation== with ==Rejection Sampling (RS-IMLE)== for mode coverage plus a temporal-consistency selection at inference; **97.3%** faster than Diffusion Policy, reaching 0.5 Push-T reward with **<29%** of the data, capturing all modes where baselines mode-collapse.
- **[[2412.06782|CARP]]** — A two-stage autoregressive policy pairing ==multi-scale action tokenization== (VQ-VAE) with ==coarse-to-fine "next-scale" GPT prediction== to preserve multimodality without rigid Markov assumptions; matches/surpasses diffusion SR across Robomimic/Kitchen/MimicGen at **~10×** faster inference and **1–5%** of the parameters, **+10%** real over diffusion.
- **[[2411.12982|Hierarchical-Diffusion-Policy-manipulation]]** — A ==Hierarchical Diffusion Policy== splitting contact-rich manipulation into a high-level ==Guider== predicting contact positions and a low-level ==Actor==, both DDPMs, trained by BC + Q-learning; **+20.8%** average SR over Diffusion Policy across six sim tasks (**88%** Tilt, **86%** PushT), real UR5 with human contact-guidance.
- **[[2412.04987|FlowPolicy]]** — A 3D ==consistency flow-matching== policy adapting DP3's point-cloud backbone with a ==two-segment Consistency Flow Matching== for one-step (NFE=1) generation; **19.9 ms**/step (**7×** over DP3's 145.7 ms) at **70.0%** over 37 Adroit/Meta-World tasks (vs DP3's 68.7%), with faster convergence and better demo-count robustness.
- **[[2409.07343|Robotic-Manipulation-Policies-Point]]** — A point-cloud policy (PointFlowMatch) using ==Conditional Flow Matching== over a PointNet + 1D-UNet velocity field on RGB-D point clouds for a 10-D SE(3)+gripper action; **67.8%** RLBench (2× DP3's 34.0%), **60%** at just 2 steps vs DDIM's 13%, transferring to a real Franka.
- **[[2408.01147|Astra]]** — An efficient Transformer for embodied instruction following adding ==trajectory attention== (bidirectional within segments) + ==learnable action queries== + ==Contrastive Dynamics Learning==; **70.67%** unseen ManiSkill (vs Gato's 40%), **3.29** CALVIN ABC→D length, faster convergence at comparable parameters.
- **[[2406.01586|ManiCM]]** — A ==consistency-model== distillation collapsing the iterative 3D diffusion policy into single-step action generation over sparse point clouds + proprioception, predicting clean actions directly; **17.3 ms**/step (10× over DP3's 177.6 ms) at **78.5%** over 31 tasks matching DP3's 77.5%, **80%** real UR3e at 10× speed.
- **[[2503.13217|Dense-Policy]]** — A tokenization-free policy using ==bidirectional autoregressive expansion==, generating actions coarse-to-fine from single frames via recursive upsampling in a lightweight encoder-only architecture; outperforms generative policies across 2D/3D tasks at ACT-comparable speed with **<50%** the parameters, with faster convergence on challenging tasks.
- **[[2403.12861|D-Cubed]]** — A ==skill-latent VAE== over task-agnostic play data feeds a ==latent diffusion model== composing long-horizon skill trajectories, refined by ==gradient-free CEM-guided sampling== in the reverse diffusion process instead of simulator gradients; outperforms all baselines on **6** dexterous deformable tasks, real LEAP-hand sim-to-real on Flip.
- **[[2307.01849|Crossway-Diffusion]]** — A diffusion visuomotor policy adding a ==state decoder== reconstructing raw pixels/state from a shared "intersection" representation as a ==self-supervised reconstruction== auxiliary loss alongside the diffusion objective; up to **+15.7%** SR on Robomimic Transport-mh and **0.95** real Duck-Lift, more robust to occlusions and novel obstructions.
- **[[1707.02920|RoboInstruct-2]]** — An early end-to-end BC controller mapping monocular RGB to joint commands via a ==recurrent neural network==, regularized by a ==VAE-GAN== image-reconstruction auxiliary loss, with an ==autoregressive Mixture Density Network== for multimodal action prediction; **76-88%** SR across 5 low-cost-robot tasks, collapsing to **8%** without the autoregressive MDN.

#### 1.2 Real-Time Chunking & Latency Compensation

Action chunking trades a longer commitment horizon for fewer inference calls — but the seam between chunks (and the total system latency) is where smoothness and reactivity break. These papers attack the seam.

- **[[2608.02547|RDE]]** — A mechanistic study finding action chunking's benefit stems from ==delayed-observation conditioning== and an ==implicit ensemble of delayed policies==, not sequence prediction; a ==Randomized Delay Ensemble== deployment strategy matches/exceeds chunking, and explicit delayed-policy ensembles push up to **+30%** SR on Robomimic Transport.
- **[[2607.09648|B-spline Policy]]** — Represents actions as ==continuous B-spline curves==, predicting ==control points== + ==knot vectors== instead of discrete chunks, with ==adaptive B-spline fitting== over demonstrations and ==inference-time segment alignment== stitching segments; up to **50%** faster completion (**23.57 s → 11.80 s** at **4×**), SR held or improved in **14/18** settings.
- **[[2607.11027|SegDiff]]** — A closed-loop diffusion policy pairing keypose-anchored ==Segmented Trajectory Modeling== with ==Dynamic Temporal Ensembling== (==DDIM inversion== regenerating mode-consistent priors, gated by buffer-validity/mode-discrepancy checks); **86.7%** RLBench-10 (**+11.1pp** over the best baseline), **100%** on multi-modal "Package" where DP/ACT oscillate between modes.
- **[[2607.04739|Spatial Attention]]** — A mathematically-derived criterion (==Att(o)==, squared sensitivity of the action distribution to observation perturbations) computed via ==Bayes' rule== over two score-based generative models, forecast by a ==seq2seq transformer== to adapt execution horizons; **+5-19%** Robomimic SR, real moving-block grasp **0.92** vs fixed-horizon's 0.42.
- **[[2607.04609|SEAM]]** — A training-free, inference-time module using ==Velocity-guided Loss Steering== to fix chunk-boundary discontinuities in ==flow matching== VLA policies via a ==closed-form quadratic correction== anchored to the prior chunk's unexecuted tail; **−27.7%** Boundary Jerk, **−26.7%** Chunk Transition Discontinuity at only **+3.8 ms** overhead on LIBERO-10.
- **[[2607.01804|VLA-Corrector]]** — An inference-time framework pairing a lightweight ==external latent dynamics corrector== with a ==Latent-space Vision Monitor== that event-triggers action-horizon truncation on drift, then ==Online Gradient Guidance== steers flow-matching replanning; **+15.65pp** MetaWorld (π0.5) and **+17.7pp** real SR (**+28.3pp** disturbance recovery), **7.93ms** overhead.
- **[[2606.17982|LAGO-Policy]]** — A latency-aware async diffusion policy pairing ==Latency-Aware Classifier-Free Guidance== (delay randomization) with a goal-prediction head driving ==collision-free trajectory generation==; lower inter-chunk inconsistency and jerk on all 8 tasks vs a DP backbone, **1.0** SR on Box/Pen and **0.8** on Cup/Pouring under unseen obstacles.
- **[[2605.25537|Soft-RTC]]** — A real-time-chunking generalization replacing the binary prefix with a ==continuous token-wise action-prior denoising== window; matches Train-Hard-RTC solve rate (**0.809**) while cutting jerk **9.6%** and finite-difference roughness up to **52.3%**; near-naive runtime vs inference-RTC's 2.6× overhead.
- **[[2605.15944|FocalPolicy]]** — A visuomotor policy curing inter-chunk discontinuity via a ==Foresight Composite Objective== (time-domain alignment for proximal actions + DCT regularization for distal multi-chunk trajectories) and ==Locally Anchored Sampling== biasing flow anchors toward the terminal state; **83.6%** over 53 Adroit/Meta-World tasks (vs DP3's 79.9%), smoother real trajectories.
- **[[2605.10051|SSIP]]** — A ==streaming stochastic interpolant policy== with an optimal guidance law from the Backward Kolmogorov Equation; **74.3%** SR at **14.75 ms** vs DP's 58.5% at 91.52 ms, **30–36%** SR on dynamic-obstacle chases vs chunked baselines' 8–12%; execution-time-aligned generation.
- **[[2604.02408|F2F-AP]]** — A ==flow-to-future asynchronous policy== conditioning on anticipated proprioceptive + synthesized visual futures via a ==heatmap flow predictor== and ==flow contrastive learning==; it scores **15/20** on dynamic handover (vs 7/20) and **11/20** grasping a rolling bottle (vs 2/20), compensating for total system latency.
- **[[2508.05396|RTI-DP]]** — A ==Real-Time Iteration Scheme== warm-starting a diffusion policy's denoising from the prior step's action sequence (no retraining), exploiting spatiotemporal consistency to truncate denoising steps; **25–36 ms** Push-T inference (orders-of-magnitude faster), matching or beating full-denoising SR and outperforming SDP/Consistency Policy on the speed-SR trade-off.
- **[[2506.11948|SAIL]]** — A full-stack framework executing imitation policies faster than demonstrations via ==Error-Adaptive Guidance==, controller-invariant 'reached-pose' targets, adaptive speed modulation, and latency-hiding action scheduling; up to **4×** sim / **3.2×** real speedup at high SR, with reached-pose targets adding up to **+55%** SR.
- **[[2506.07339|RTC]]** — The canonical ==real-time chunking== method framing async execution as ==inpainting== with soft-masked ΠGDM guidance, no retraining; robust to **50%** horizon delay in sim and **+200 ms** real latency where synchronous inference degrades linearly; the binary-prefix baseline Soft RTC generalizes.
- **[[2505.21851|Streaming-Flow-Policy]]** — A flow-matching imitation policy modeling action trajectories directly as ==flow trajectories in action space== so generation aligns with execution time, ==streaming actions on-the-fly== to the controller + ==analytically-constructed stabilizing flows== against distribution shift; **3.5 ms** Push-T / **4.5 ms** RoboMimic per action at SOTA SR.
- **[[2504.18792|STDArm]]** — A real-time correction framework letting static-data-trained policies drive dynamic mobile platforms: an ==Action Manager== ==temporal-ensembles==+interpolates output to decouple inference from control rate, while a ==Stabilizer== with ==online latency estimation== compensates for platform motion; Pick-and-Place **0%→53.3%**, drone delivery **13.3%→40%**.
- **[[2406.04806|Streaming-Diffusion-Policy]]** — A policy generating partially-denoised trajectories at ==variable noise levels== in a persistent buffer, denoising only N/h steps per control step for an ==h-fold speedup==; **0.85** real Push-T SR vs Diffusion Policy's 0.50 and Consistency Policy's 0.40 at **0.07 s**/chunk; the canonical streaming-diffusion policy.
- **[[2502.12724|Responsive-Noise-Relaying-Diffusion-Policy]]** — A policy maintaining a ==noise-relaying buffer== of progressively-noised actions with ==noise-aware conditioning==, executing the most-denoised action and reusing denoising steps for the rest; **+18.0%** SR on response-sensitive tasks (PushT, RollBall) over Diffusion Policy at **~12.5×** faster inference.
- **[[2503.04051|RA-DP]]** — A training-free high-frequency replanning policy with an ==action-queue== denoising mechanism (one high-quality action per step) plus ==loss-based guidance== from a differentiable energy for runtime constraint adaptation; **130.9 Hz** state-based replanning (**3.5–5×** over Diffusion Policy), **66.1%** MetaWorld, **63.3%** unseen-obstacle avoidance on a real Franka.

#### 1.3 Guided & Steered Sampling

A trained diffusion policy can be steered at inference without retraining — toward task goals, away from failures, or toward in-distribution safety — by injecting guidance into the denoising loop.

- **[[2607.10206|SL-FM]]** — Makes flow matching *intervenable* by modelling the source as a ==state-conditioned mixture of Gaussians== giving K selectable handles, kept distinct by ==Orthogonal Source Lifting== and a ==responsibility floor== preventing dead modes; **0.823** avg multimodal score vs **0.802**, **91.1%** counterfactual redirection, **91.7%** joint route-and-task.
- **[[2606.18247|VERITAS]]** — An inference-time steering framework pairing a stochastic policy with a ==gradient-free visual verifier== scoring sampled action chunks against a VLM pixel-space reasoning trace, then distilling verified rollouts offline; **+35%** real SR via steering with no fine-tuning, **+9.7%** sim after offline tuning on autonomous data matching human demos.
- **[[2605.25547|TapSampling]]** — A fine-tuning-free inference-time sampler pairing an ==Action-VAE learned posterior== (diverse candidate generation) with a ==self-supervised task-progress verifier== scoring each candidate's impact on completion; **+0.21** avg success length on CALVIN (OpenVLA 3.30→3.51), **98.0%** LIBERO-Long, **+5.0%** real Franka, **12×** verifier speedup.
- **[[2506.15157|Robust-Instant-Policy]]** — A ==Student's t-regression== aggregator for LLM-based in-context imitation that samples multiple candidate trajectories and downweights hallucinated outliers via heavy-tailed robust averaging; **80%** at 10 demos (**+26%** over KAT's 54%, vs DP's 50%), the t-distribution (80%) beating its Gaussian counterpart (64%), optimal at 5 queries.
- **[[2503.15386|CCDP]]** — A ==product-of-experts guided sampling== scheme composing state/history/failure-feature denoisers from a synthesized ==recovery dataset==; **99%** Door-Opening and **96%** Button-Pressing vs DP's 70% and 10%, with no failure-type labeling required.
- **[[2503.14833|Curiosity-Diffuser]]** — A guidance method using ==Random Network Distillation== prediction error as a ==curiosity guidance signal== steering policies away from OOD trajectories; AntMaze-Large-Play **38%→58%** and average D4RL score **77.8**; behaviors quantifiably closer to training data.
- **[[2603.16368|SCDP]]** — A post-training scheme adding lightweight ==FiLM modules== to a frozen ==Diffusion Policy== that modulate toward legibility or predictability via an ==ellipse-of-ambiguity== detector; **0.74** transparency in clear scenes and **0.43–0.47** efficiency gains in ambiguous ones, **>0.98** real SR.
- **[[2512.21430|EVE]]** — An augmentation adding an ==ensemble of zero-shot VLM verifiers== to frozen generative policies, fused via a ==guided diffusion action incorporator==; up to **+2.40%** task SR across diffusion and flow policies, scaling with VLM size (72B > 32B > 7B).
- **[[2605.09537|CAPS]]** — A training-free ==SNR-aware power sampling== scheme switching between greedy execution and ==Metropolis-Hastings deliberation==; **97.6%** on LIBERO-long vs OpenVLA's 49.8%, **+18.0%** real over TACO at only 2.15× latency; reframes drift as a sampling error.
- **[[2605.01194|VLA-ATTC]]** — An adaptive test-time-compute framework whose ==cognitive-clutch== (DTW between two stochastic actions) triggers deliberation only under high uncertainty, then a lightweight ==Relative Action Critic== ranks parallel candidates by pairwise preference; cuts π0.5 failure rate **>50%** on LIBERO-Long, **+17.3%/+10.7%** real for π0/π0.5 at **20.8 Hz** real-time control.
- **[[2604.07084|FMP]]** — A ==flow-matching motion planner== regressing a conditional vector field with ==best-of-N parallel sampling== + batched collision check; **86.7%** real UR5e at 1.4M params vs Neural-MP's 20M, **84%** TableTop in 0.58 s.
- **[[2408.17355|Bidirectional-Decoding]]** — A model-agnostic test-time sampling algorithm (BID) drawing multiple candidate action chunks and selecting via ==backward coherence== (consistency with the committed chunk) + ==forward contrast== (strong-vs-weak policy likelihood); **+32%** relative over closed-loop DP across 7 tasks, **2×** real on dynamic grasping, compatible with DP/VQ-BeT.
- **[[2509.00271|HAVE]]** — Decouples action generation (unconditional diffusion) from a ==history-aware verifier== (PointNet++ + Transformer) scoring proposals by attending over past actions + their ==observation-flow== outcomes; **2.5%** vs FlowBot3D's **13.4%** sim failure rate, suppresses previously-failed action modes on a real ambiguous door.
- **[[2508.21800|Tree-Guided]]** — A zero-shot ==tree-search test-time diffusion planner== (TDP) framing planning as bi-level sampling: ==parent branching== via Particle Guidance for diverse control trajectories + gradient guidance for observations, then ==sub-tree expansion== for local search; **47.6** Maze2d-Large (vs MCSS's 25.0), **+10–20%** on KUKA arm manipulation, escaping local optima.
- **[[2508.12189|Self-GAD]]** — A ==self-guided action diffusion== method adding ==gradient-based guidance== from prior action predictions (a trajectory-deviation loss over overlapping horizons) into the denoising process; **+71.4%** SR over random sampling at fewer samples, **+26.5%** with moving targets, and **+48.2%** RoboCasa / **+17.2%** DexMimicGen when added to GR00T-N1-2B.
- **[[2508.10511|KDPE]]** — Filters Diffusion Policy's stochastic trajectory samples at inference via ==Kernel Density Estimation== with a ==manifold-aware kernel== (Euclidean position/gripper + SO(3) orientation) over the population's last actions, selecting the highest-density mode; **+3.3%** RoboMimic/MimicGen, **96%** vs **90%** real PickPlush at **~16ms** overhead.
- **[[2503.12466|MCDP]]** — A ==modality-composable diffusion policy== summing the noise estimates of independently-trained unimodal (RGB / point-cloud) diffusion policies at inference via ==classifier-free-guidance-free composition==; **0.86** Empty-Cup-Place SR vs the better unimodal's 0.62, correcting directional and force errors by integrating a complementary modality.
- **[[2405.19424|Diffusion-Policy-Attacker]]** — A red-team framework (DP-Attacker) crafting ==adversarial attacks on diffusion policies== by maximizing a ==noise-prediction loss== to disrupt denoising, across global/patched and online/offline regimes; cuts SR over **50%** across 6 tasks with imperceptible perturbations, physical patches degrading real deployment.

#### 1.4 Efficiency, History & Multi-Frequency Control

Once history matters, the backbone's cost scales with context length — these papers make history conditioning cheap, adaptive, or frequency-aware so non-Markovian tasks stay real-time.

- **[[2607.10625|DASL]]** — An asynchronous multi-frequency policy decoupling a slow ==VQ skill codebook== planner (Option Transformer) from a fast ==Decision Transformer== action executor, regularized by a training-only ==Latent Trajectory Diffusion== module curing skill codebook collapse; **48.05%** CALVIN (vs HULC's 32.50%), **88%** real Realman SR at **13.6–14.0 Hz** inference.
- **[[2607.06370|ActionCache]]** — A training-free, plug-and-play ==caching framework== accelerating flow-based VLA action heads by retrieving similar past action chunks via ==sparse ternary random projection== cache keys, with a ==fallback mechanism== for cache misses; **11.75×** latency cut on π0.5, **34.43×** on GR00T-N1.6, **70%** real pick-place SR (vs base's 72%).
- **[[2606.12499|AEM]]** — An ==Action-Effect Memory== pretraining framework that trains a ==Mamba encoder== via ==masked action-effect reconstruction== and compresses the whole interaction history into a single final-visual-token bottleneck vector for downstream policies; **+20.7%** RoboTwin2.0 (DP 29.8→50.5%), real **43.3→81.7%**, lower FLOPs/latency than raw history stacking.
- **[[2605.14598|DSSP]]** — A ==Mamba SSM== serving as both history encoder and diffusion backbone with a ==dynamics-aware auxiliary loss==; **62.3%** on 50 RoboTwin-2.0 tasks vs DP3's 55.24% at **44.3M** params (vs 264.4M), **−45.4%** latency, real memory tasks **70%** vs 30%.
- **[[2605.11817|See-What-Matters]]** — A ==Differentiable Grid Sampler== (GridS) recasting VLA visual-token compression as continuous geometry-aware resampling, an MLP predicting coordinates for ==differentiable bilinear sampling== so the task loss optimizes where to look; up to **76%** FLOPs reduction / **3.4×** speedup, **96.6%** with one token on π0.5, **+52.4%** real Stack-Cubes.
- **[[2604.10170|Device-Conditioned-Architecture-Search]]** — A ==Device-Conditioned Quantization-For-All== supernet with hardware-aware regularization (real-device latency/memory LUTs) + ==Multi-step On-Policy Distillation== curing quantization error-accumulation; **2-3x** speedup / **2-4x** smaller across OpenVLA-OFT/DiffusionPolicy-T/MDT-V at **W4A4**, near-FP16 SR on Jetson Orin NX.
- **[[2604.18933|Gated-Memory-Policy]]** — A learned ==binary memory gate== activating history conditioning only when needed, with ==cross-attention== cached tokens and noise-injected histories; **+30.1%** on MemMimic non-Markovian tasks, up to **98.7%** cross-trial, no Markovian regression.
- **[[2604.06067|HiPolicy]]** — A ==hierarchical multi-frequency action chunking== policy with ==FiLM frequency conditioning== and ==entropy-guided adaptive execution==; **+62%/+44%** relative SR over DP/DP3 on RoboTwin 1.0/2.0, **+42%** real SR and **14%** faster execution.
- **[[2604.15938|VADF]]** — A model-agnostic ==Adaptive Loss Network== + ==VLM task segmenter== assigning per-subtask denoising steps; **2.46×** Push-T speedup and **+31.3%** real ARX5 SR with no architectural change; plug-and-play onto existing diffusion policies.
- **[[2602.08245|STEP]]** — A diffusion-policy accelerator using a lightweight predictor to ==warm-start denoising at an intermediate step== with spatiotemporally-consistent actions + ==velocity-aware perturbation injection== curing execution deadlocks, with a local-contractivity guarantee; **95%** PickNPlace at 2 steps and **20 ms** real latency (**105.7×** over DDPM), **−59%** episode time.
- **[[2601.12894|ActionGen]]** — A diffusion-policy accelerator (Sparse ActionGen) with a ==rollout-adaptive prune-then-reuse== pipeline: an observation-conditioned ==pruner== predicts a global binary mask over all denoising steps/DiT blocks in one pass, plus ==one-for-all cross-block cache reuse==; **3.63–3.77×** speedup at **>90%** pruning holding **81–84%** SR, **44–46 Hz** real.
- **[[2506.13456|BAC]]** — A training-free ==Block-wise Adaptive Caching== method reusing per-block action features via a dynamic-programming ==Adaptive Caching Scheduler== + ==Bubbling Union Algorithm== against FFN error surge; **3×** real-world (71% at 39.2 Hz) and **3.4×** sim speedup lossless, accelerates RDT-1B VLA 3.55×.
- **[[2412.12953|MoDE]]** — A diffusion-transformer policy with a ==noise-conditioned Mixture-of-Experts== (routing keyed only to the denoising noise level) plus ==expert caching== that pre-fuses experts per noise level; SOTA across **134** CALVIN/LIBERO tasks (**+57%** over diffusion baselines) at **90% fewer** FLOPs/params and **~40%** faster inference, beating OpenVLA-7B on SIMPLER.
- **[[2409.07163|Mamba-Policy]]** — A 3D diffusion policy with an ==X-Mamba UNet== hybridizing a ==selective state-space (Mamba)== block and attention via FiLM perception-action fusion; matches/exceeds DP3 on Adroit/MetaWorld/DexArt at **−81.2%** parameters, **−90.0%** FLOPs, and **−86.2%** GPU memory, with real Franka grasp-and-place.
- **[[2503.00339|Falcon]]** — A training-free acceleration reusing ==partially-denoised actions== from prior timesteps as the denoising start (vs pure noise), with a ==latent buffer== + distance-thresholded history selection and an exploration rate; **2–7×** (up to **34×** for RDT-1B) sampling speedup with negligible SR loss, preserving multimodal behavior where rivals mode-collapse.
- **[[2503.02048|FRMD]]** — A fast smooth-action policy distilling a ==Movement-Primitive Diffusion== teacher into a single-step student via ==consistency distillation== over ==Probabilistic DMPs==; **17.2 ms** inference (**10×** over the teacher), **64.8%** avg SR over 12 Meta-World/ManiSkill tasks, far smoother trajectories (82→21 non-smooth transitions on PlugCharger).

#### 1.5 Bimanual & Coordinated Multi-Arm Policies

Two arms double the action space and demand timing-precise coordination — these policies resolve the decouple-to-avoid-interference vs couple-to-coordinate dilemma at the architecture level, learning when arms act independently and when they must synchronize.

- **[[2607.11884|MoF Policy]]** — A bimanual-mobile diffusion policy with synchronized ==Mixture-of-Experts== denoising across coordinate frames via a novel ==column-vector SE(3) rotation representation== avoiding lossy orthogonalization; **66.8%** sim SR (**+16.5pp** over DP), row-vector orthogonalization collapsing MoF-Ensemble to **0%**, **85%/70%** real pouring/serving.
- **[[2606.22982|CLS-DP]]** — A decentralized multi-agent ==Diffusion Policy== distilling privileged joint-trajectory dynamics into a ==CVAE coordination latent== during centralized training, sampled from local RGB alone at execution with no inter-agent communication; **38%** avg RoboFactory SR (vs Global GauDP's 20%), the only method solving Three-Robots-Stack-Cube (**20%**).
- **[[2606.20285|Co-VLA]]** — A dual-arm VLA whose ==Structured Action Expert== decomposes bimanual actions into ==shared coordination latents + arm-specific residuals== under task-adaptive losses, with a deployment-time ==Latent-Aware Controller== modulating stiffness; **91%** sim Handover-Block (vs π₀'s 64%), **73%** real Handover-ID, shared latents correlating with inter-arm synchronization.
- **[[2605.16043|Snapshot-to-Action]]** — Skeletonizes one multi-view RGB-D snapshot into 100 ordered 3D particles evolved in an ==XPBD Cosserat-rod simulation==, conditioning an ==ACT== policy via a lightweight ==ParticleEncoder== replacing the ResNet backbone; **30.8%** lower L1 error than vision-based ACT on a held-out stiffer rope, **7.2×** faster inference.
- **[[2605.13452|CUBic]]** — A bimanual framework reformulating coordination as ==unified perceptual modeling==, fusing arm-specific tokens via ==dual VQ codebooks with a shared mapping space== so coordination emerges intrinsically, two-stage trained; **51.8%** over 7 sim tasks vs DP's 38.5% and DP3's 39.8%, **43.1** in-domain real score outperforming Diffusion Policy.
- **[[2604.20348|BiCICLe]]** — A training-free bimanual framework casting coordination as ==multi-agent leader-follower in-context learning== over frozen LLMs, decomposing the dual-arm space into ==sequential single-arm predictions== where the follower conditions on the leader's full trajectory; **70.5%** on TWIN (**+6.1pp** over training-free baselines), **54.5%** OOD, **53.3%** real Franka.
- **[[2603.20236|EnergyAction]]** — A method composing bimanual policies by ==summing left/right unimanual energy functions== (flow-matching velocity fields read as energy gradients) with ==energy-based temporal-spatial coordination== + adaptive denoising; **86.4%** over 13 RLBench2 tasks (**+4.6%**), **+32.5%** over 2nd-best at only 20 demos, down to 1.79 denoising steps.
- **[[2603.08541|EquiBim]]** — A model-agnostic ==bilateral-equivariance regularization== term ‖π(S(O))−S(π(O))‖² enforcing symmetric dual-arm behavior across modalities and action spaces with no architecture change; up to **+9.5%** absolute on RoboTwin Image+Joint, **+30%** real Banana Handover and 5/10 under mirrored orientations where the baseline scored 0/10.
- **[[2509.21723|VLBiMan]]** — A one-shot bimanual framework decomposing a single human demo into ==task-invariant + adaptable atomic skills==, adapting variable modules via ==VLM + 2D-mask Vision-Feature Matching== (no 6-DoF pose) then ==IK + collision compensation==; **85.3%** new-placement / **78.0%** novel-instance, **83.8%** zero-shot transfer to a humanoid dual-arm without retraining.
- **[[2509.08160|DG-MAP]]** — MAPF-inspired search pairing a ==single-arm diffusion model== proposing individual trajectories with a ==dual-arm diffusion model== resolving pairwise conflicts, trained only on 1–2-arm interaction data yet generalizing via decomposition; **>90%** SR for 3–8 arms (vs a limited-data baseline's <10% at 4+ arms), **89%** four-arm real pick-and-place.
- **[[2505.05287|SYMDEX]]** — Embeds ==morphological symmetry== via ==G-equivariant networks== for per-hand subtask policies (==Multi-Task Multi-Agent POMDP==), distilled into a unified ambidextrous global policy via staged ==curriculum learning==; **>80%** SR across 6 bimanual tasks, zero-shot sim-to-real, scaling to a 4-arm **C4**-symmetric setup.
- **[[2503.23271|Coordinated-Bimanual-State-Diffusion]]** — An imitation framework disentangling ==state prediction (a DDPM over future object states)== from an ==inverse-dynamics MLP== that computes the actions to realize them; **79.3%** Push-L and superior Franka-Kitchen SR, 15/15 real Laundry Cleanup vs 0/15 baseline; object-state losses signal coordination failures action losses miss.
- **[[2503.13916|Bimanual-Action-Chunking]]** — An ACT extension adding per-arm local Transformer encoders plus an ==Inter-Arm Coordinated Transformer Encoder== modeling cross-arm synchronization, with split or single decoders; **+8–9%** average SR over ACT across 8 real tasks, ablating the IACE collapses several tasks to 0%; split decoders win asynchronous, single decoders synchronous tasks.
- **[[2503.06831|ODIL]]** — A one-shot dual-arm imitation method (ODIL) learning from a single demo via ==Act-Act / Stabilize-Act / Rearrange primitives== executed by a ==three-stage visual-servoing controller== (SuperPoint + LightGlue), with no object models or extra sensors; **93.3%** 4-DoF and **86.7%** 6-DoF dual-arm vs RoboTAP's 63.3%, robust to large pose changes.
- **[[2504.17784|Gripper-Keypose-Object-Pointflow]]** — A bimanual policy adding two interfaces to a ==3D semantic neural field== — ==target gripper keyposes== and ==object pointflow== — that condition a ==diffusion transformer== over continuous actions; **+16.1%** SR on RLBench2 and **+27.5%** average across four real tasks, generalizing to unseen objects/lighting under heavy disturbance.
- **[[2503.09186|Decoupled-Bimanual]]** — A ==Decoupled Interaction Framework== assigning an independent model per arm plus a ==selective interaction module== that adaptively modulates cross-arm exchange via scaling factors and biases; **78.9%** on RoboTwin (**+23.5%** over DP3) at **1/6** the parameters, extending to a three-arm setting (**+28%**).
- **[[2409.07914|InterACT]]** — A bimanual policy with a ==Hierarchical Attention Encoder== (interleaved segment-wise and cross-segment self-attention over CLS tokens) + a ==Multi-arm Decoder== and a ==Synchronization Block==; outperforms ACT on all 9 tasks, **82%** Transfer-Cube transfer vs ACT's 50% and **44%** Peg-Insertion insert vs 20%, removing CLS tokens halves coordination-heavy SR.
- **[[2407.00278|PerAct2]]** — A language-conditioned ==behavioral-cloning== bimanual agent using a single ==Perceiver IO transformer== over a shared 3D voxel grid to implicitly learn coordination, paired with the RLBench² benchmark (13 bimanual tasks); highest SR on **9/13** tasks over extended ACT/RVT/PerAct baselines, fastest training (54 h), real ARMAR-6 transfer.
- **[[2501.14208|You-Only-Teach-Once]]** — A one-shot paradigm (YOTO) extracting stable 3D hand keyposes + a motion mask from a single ==binocular human video==, proliferating data via real-robot rollouts, then a customized ==Bimanual Diffusion Policy== predicting sparse keyposes; **76.8%** average over 5 long-horizon tasks vs EquiBot's 23.4% and ACT's 5.7%, generalizing to novel objects.
- **[[2405.06039|Bi-VLA]]** — A bimanual VLA pairing a ==Starling-LM language module== (plan + code-gen) with a ==Qwen-VL vision module== mapping 2D pixels to 3D for dual-arm recipe execution; **100%** executable-code generation, **96.06%** ingredient detection, **83.4%** action-generation SR with all ingredients present.
- **[[2501.06919|Shake-VLA]]** — A bimanual liquid-mixing VLA integrating vision, speech, ==RAG recipe retrieval==, force-torque measurement, and an anomaly-detection module suggesting ingredient substitutes; **93%** speech / **91%** vision / **95%** anomaly detection, **100%** overall cocktail-preparation SR, bilingual English/Russian labels.
- **[[2511.00998|GauDP]]** — A multi-agent policy reconstructing a globally-consistent scene via a ==self-supervised feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting== module from decentralized RGB, then fusing global context with each agent's local 2D features for a ==joint global diffusion policy==; **19.67%** avg RoboFactory SR vs DP3's 14.33%, PSNR lifted 17.9→23.4 over Noposplat.
- **[[2307.05933|BiRP]]** — A bimanual-coordination method extending ==TP-GMM + Linear Quadratic Tracking== with a ==relative parameterization== treating each arm's trajectory as the other's time-varying task frame, embedding coordination in both the ==Product-of-Experts== motion model and the LQT cost; reproduces leader-follower + synergistic tasks on a real CURI humanoid.

#### 1.6 Dexterous Hardware & Mechanism Design

The action-generation stack ultimately runs on physical hardware — these papers design the hands, arms, and morphologies that make dexterous, compliant, affordable manipulation possible, the embodiment the policies above assume.

- **[[2607.14730|Hybrid Rigid-Soft Gripper]]** — A three-finger gripper combining rigid links + compliant pneumatic actuators with a ==dual ratchet-pawl self-locking== mechanism per joint for energy-free holding, using low-cost ==nylon-polyethylene membrane== chambers; **4200g** load capacity (vs soft grippers' 210g), **50.05%** less energy per grasp, non-destructive across six fruit types.
- **[[2606.20549|Hand-Co-Design]]** — A ==data-driven co-design framework== generating robot hands by jointly optimizing kinematic structure + joint trajectories to reproduce human fingertip motions under a fixed-IK policy, with passive four-bar ==mimic joints== encoding motion into low-DoF hardware; **0.24 mm** tracking error (vs XHand's 7.40 mm), an actor cutting design search 5 h→30 min.
- **[[2606.20193|Belt-Finger]]** — An affordable (~€10/finger) ==soft belt-driven gripper== upgrading parallel jaws with three active in-hand DoF (translation/pitch/roll) via deformable TPU belts, integrating with teleop, ==iCEM-MPC==, and fine-tuned VLA policies; **100%** SR across 10 manipulation tasks (vs 50% failure for parallel grippers), **4.8 mm**/**9.1°** translation-pose error under MPC.
- **[[2606.17418|DexLink-Hand]]** — A compact (320 g) 16-DOF ==linkage-driven dexterous hand== using ==crossed four-bar (PIP/DIP)== + ==spatial spherical (2-DOF MCP)== linkages with worm-gear micro-motors; replicates all **33** Feix grasp types, **~65 N** per-finger and **10 kg** hand load, under **USD 400**; affordable human-like hardware.
- **[[2604.17245|MM-Hand]]** — A ==21-DoF modular dexterous hand== with ==remote tendon-driven actuation== relocating motors to an external hub, freeing internal volume for a multimodal suite (joint encoders, tactile, ==in-palm stereo vision==), plus an analytical model of arm-motion coupling + long-sheath friction; **~25 N** fingertip force through a 1-m sheath, **<0.1°** joint error.
- **[[2603.12120|CRAFT-Hand]]** — A ==hybrid hard-soft== tendon-driven hand using rigid PLA finger links + soft TPU ==rolling-contact joints==, open-source and 3D-printable under $600; **15.29 N** pull-out strength (~2× a rigid baseline), **100%** across all **33** Feix grasp types, **90–100%** teleoperation SR on fragile eggs/raspberries.
- **[[2602.17921|Diffeomorphic-End-Effector-Co-Design]]** — A bi-level co-design jointly optimizing end-effector morphology via ==Latent Diffeomorphic== RBF deformations (PCA latent) and a ==stress-aware policy== distilled into a 3D diffusion policy; **+33%** SR / **−36%** induced stress over a parallel jaw on jelly-cube grasping, real zero-shot **9/9** grasp / **10/12** push.
- **[[2512.16069|Task-Driven-Modular-Manipulator]]** — A planner-in-the-loop design framework optimizing modular-manipulator ==morphology + mounting + pose== by reformulating the discrete-continuous problem as fully continuous (==sorting-based mapping==) solved with ==CMA-ES== + a hierarchical-MPC planner; converges faster than a GA, finding torque-reducing bi-branch morphologies.
- **[[2512.03743|House-of-Dextra]]** — A ==cross-embodied co-design== jointly optimizing dexterous-hand morphology and control via ==bi-level optimization== with ==procedural constraint-based hand generation== and a ==graph-based morphology-conditioned policy==; **3.3 rad/s** in-hand rotation (vs RoboGrammar's 0.26), **15/17** unseen real objects, design-to-deployment in **24 h**.
- **[[2504.04259|ORCA-Hand]]** — An open-source ==tendon-driven anthropomorphic hand== (3D-printable, <2,000 CHF) with ==poppable pin joints== that dislocate rather than break and an ==auto-calibration== motor-to-joint mapping for uninterrupted dexterous learning; **>10,000** continuous IL cycles (~20 h) without failure, zero-shot sim-to-real in-hand reorientation, 7 h pick-and-place.
- **[[2502.19389|Surface-Based-Foldable]]** — A non-prehensile system using two modular ==origami foldable robots== (3-DoF: roll/pitch/height) with hierarchical closed-loop control to translate, rotate, and flip objects by surface inclination and impulse; translates Play-Doh at **~5.6 cm/s**, flips a rigid cuboid 180°, reshapes deformable putty.
- **[[2501.05420|RoboPanoptes]]** — A modular 9-DoF robot with **21** distributed body-mounted cameras for whole-body vision, paired with ==view-dependent positional encoding== + ==multi-view cross-attention== diffusion policy trained via ==blink training== (camera dropout); **94.4%** Unboxing / **96.6%** Sweeping / **83.3%** Stowing real SR, **2.9×** faster sweeping than end-effector teleop.
- **[[1904.03815|Blue]]** — A 7-DoF anthropomorphic arm using ==Quasi-Direct-Drive== gimbal-motor actuation (7.125:1 belt) for backdrivable, low-friction force control with proximal mass placement and injection-molded shells; **2 kg** payload, **4 mm** repeatability, **7.5 Hz** bandwidth at a projected **<USD 5000** end-user cost; safe compliant manipulation.

**Generative Policy Architectures — Decision Matrix**

| Need | Recommendation |
|---|---|
| Multimodal-goal diffusion baseline | [[2407.05996\|MDT]] (**85%** CALVIN) |
| Provable convergence / stability | [[2606.03834\|SFMDS]] (**100%** asymptotic stability) |
| Sub-millisecond single-step action | [[2602.07322\|A2A]] (**0.56 ms**) |
| Dynamic obstacles / reactive control | [[2605.10051\|SSIP]] (**74.3%** at 14.75 ms) |
| Smooth real-time chunking | [[2605.25537\|Soft-RTC]] (**−52.3%** roughness) |
| Steer a frozen policy without retraining | [[2512.21430\|EVE]], [[2605.09537\|CAPS]] (**97.6%** LIBERO-long) |
| Failure-avoidance at inference | [[2503.15386\|CCDP]] (**99%** Door-Opening) |
| Cheap full-history non-Markovian control | [[2605.14598\|DSSP]] (**44.3M** params), [[2604.18933\|Gated-Memory-Policy]] |
| Energy-constrained deployment | [[2606.06049\|L-SDPPO]] (**36.45%** energy) |
| Unified policy + world-model + anomaly | [[2511.04812\|MDF]] |

^dm-1

> [!star] Key Papers — Backbone Design-Space Exemplars
> - [[2403.03954|DP3]] — the canonical 3D-diffusion-policy that proved a lightweight point-cloud encoder + conditional diffusion generalizes from a handful of demos (covered in §2).
> - [[2407.05996|MDT]] — the reference multimodal-goal diffusion transformer; established self-supervised foresight + latent alignment as diffusion-policy auxiliaries.
> - [[2606.03834|SFMDS]] — first to bolt provable stability guarantees onto a flow-matching policy, bridging dynamical-systems control and generative imitation.
> - [[2602.07322|A2A]] — showed action-conditioned (not noise-conditioned) flow matching collapses the iteration count to a single sub-millisecond step.
> - [[2605.25537|Soft-RTC]] — reframed real-time chunking as a continuous denoising window, dissolving the chunk-seam discontinuity that plagued earlier chunked policies.

^key-papers-1

> [!tip] Expressivity Is Cheap Now — Latency Is the Real Constraint
> The 2023 question was "can a policy represent multimodal actions?" — diffusion answered yes, and by 2026 it is a solved baseline. The frontier moved to the *iteration tax*: every paper here is really fighting the inference loop, whether by collapsing steps ([[2602.07322|A2A]]'s single-step flow), hiding latency ([[2604.02408|F2F-AP]]'s future anticipation), smoothing the seam ([[2605.25537|Soft-RTC]]), or making history conditioning sub-quadratic ([[2605.14598|DSSP]]'s Mamba). The lesson: pick your backbone for the *control budget*, not the benchmark number — a tighter distribution you can't sample at 30 Hz loses to a looser one you can. For the world-model variant that turns this backbone into a planner, see [[10_Manipulation-Skill-Learning#3. World-Model & Video-as-Policy]]; for how the same diffusion backbone absorbs force signals, see [[11_Contact-Rich-and-Tactile-Control#3. Force-Conditioned VLA Architectures]].

^insight-1

---

### 2. 3D & Multi-View Spatial Representation

Manipulation is a 3D problem solved, historically, with 2D inputs — a single RGB frame throws away the depth and viewpoint information a grasp actually depends on. This section is the field's answer: encode geometry explicitly (point clouds, voxels, multi-view projections, Gaussians) so the policy reasons in the space where contact happens. The payoff is sample efficiency — [[2403.03954|DP3]] learns from ten demos what a 2D policy needs hundreds for — because 3D structure is a strong inductive prior that the network no longer has to discover from pixels.

Two pressures split the work. First, *where* the 3D understanding lives: baked into the policy's perception module (3.1's 3D backbones) or supplied by a spatial-reasoning VLM that the policy queries (2.2). Second, *robustness to viewpoint and clutter* — a policy trained on one camera angle collapses when the camera moves, and an object-centric encoder that confuses two similar mugs picks the wrong one (2.3). The throughline: geometry is not a feature you add, it is the coordinate frame the whole policy should live in.

#### 2.1 3D Policy Backbones

Encoding observations as point clouds, voxels, or multi-view projections so the action decoder reasons in metric 3D — the geometric inductive prior that buys sample efficiency.

- **[[2607.10706|Action Map Policy]]** — Recasts 3D closed-loop manipulation as ==pixel classification== over ==2D heatmaps==, an action chunk becoming projected ==3D keypoints== predicted by an ==X-Net Multi-View Transformer== then recovered by ==triangulation==; **+20.7%** over the second-best MimicGen baseline, **80–90%** real long-horizon vs **25–40%** Diffusion Policy, **13.80 ms**/chunk.
- **[[2607.07101|GeoProp]]** — A lightweight adapter analytically projecting the 3D end-effector state onto the 2D image plane, applying localized state-conditioned ==FiLM== to intermediate visual features plus ==predictive kinematic sampling== of a future waypoint; **+8.5pp** sim / **+11.2pp** real Mobile ALOHA SR over vanilla proprioception fusion, plug-and-play onto Diffusion Policy and π0.
- **[[2606.21788|Rotation-Aware]]** — A two-stage in-hand reorientation method: a ==PointNet++ encoder== trained with ==Siamese metric learning== to calibrate latent Euclidean distance to ==SO(3) geodesic rotation error==, feeding a frozen-embedding ==PPO== policy; **100%** SR over 100 trials on 4 YCB objects, where a generic Point-MAE encoder fails completely.
- **[[2606.15232|SSPool]]** — An empirical study validating ==implicit spatial representation== (Spatial Softmax Pooling) over semantic pooling, diagnosing ==spatial information collapse== in downsampling encoders, then PRISM fusing multiscale features via top-down cross-attention; **90.0%** Robomimic SSPool vs AvgPool's 81.1%, **+168%** relative on ToolHang, LIBERO-10 **22→53.5%**.
- **[[2606.12759|Sparse2Act]]** — A pretraining framework teaching a ==sparse 3D encoder== action-aligned geometric features by using ==3D end-effector actions as direct geometric supervision== over masked sparse tokens, with ==3D RoPE==; **86.9%** LIBERO-10 (vs DP's 29.1%), **73.4%** cross-domain Meta-World-5, **72.5%** real on AgileX PiPER from 50 demos.
- **[[2606.10899|MV-Actor]]** — A ==multi-view perception== framework building a unified semantic-spatial representation via a ==Multi-view Semantic Interaction== module + ==Pi3X spatial tokens== + ==Guided Metric Depth Repair== for bimanual manipulation; **87.8%** on PerAct2 (vs 3DFA's 85.1%), **63.1%** real staged score on ARK-Lift with consumer depth cameras.
- **[[2602.10943|Generalizable Workspace NeRF]]** — A ==generalizable NeRF== predicting 3D workspace occupancy from egocentric humanoid views via a ==world-frame cost volume== + 3D U-Net + MLP trained by novel-view reconstruction, no per-scene finetuning; **26mm** depth error, infers occluded geometry.
- **[[2511.10560|OmniVGGT]]** — A 3D foundation model injecting an arbitrary set of geometric modalities (depth, camera params) via a ==GeoAdapter== (ZeroConv camera + additive depth) under ==stochastic multimodal fusion== on a VGGT backbone; SOTA depth/pose/reconstruction, and its 3D spatial tokens boost a VLA on CALVIN over point-cloud/RGB baselines at **~30×** Pow3R speed.
- **[[2403.03954|DP3]]** — A ==point-cloud diffusion policy== with a lightweight MLP encoder on single-view depth; **74.4%** over 72 sim tasks (**+24.2%** rel.) at 10 demos and **85%** real over 4 dexterous tasks at 40 demos; the canonical 3D diffusion backbone.
- **[[2209.05451|PerAct]]** — A ==Perceiver Transformer== over a 100³ ==voxel grid== predicting next-best-voxel actions, language-conditioned via ==CLIP==; up to **34×** over image-to-action baselines, learning 7 real tasks from **53** total demos; the voxel-classification landmark.
- **[[2010.14406|Transporter Networks]]** — An early landmark casting pick-and-place as ==spatial displacement inference== over an ==orthographic top-down RGB-D projection==, learning picks via an FCN and pick-conditioned placing via ==deep feature template matching==; **>90%** sim SR from **100** demos, **98.9%/98.3%** real kit-assembly/sweeping, introducing the ==Ravens== benchmark.
- **[[2604.15281|R3D]]** — A 3D policy revisiting the design with ==LayerNorm-only Transformer== encoders + a ==spatially-aware Diffusion Transformer== decoder and heavy 3D augmentation; **83.8%** RoboTwin-2.0 Easy and **64.8%** Hard vs DP3's 61.0%, **55.2%** ManiSkill2 vs DP3's 23.0%.
- **[[2603.14498|R3DP]]** — A real-time 3D-aware policy with an ==Asynchronous Fast–Slow Collaboration== module (slow 3D foundation model on key frames, fast TFPNet feature propagation) + ==Projective Positional Encoding== multi-view fusion; **69.0%** over 10 RoboTwin tasks (**+51.4%** over DP-multi, **+11.4%** over DP3), **62.18 ms** encoding latency on RTX 4090.
- **[[2602.00937|CLAMP]]** — A ==contrastive 3D pretraining== framework over multi-view point clouds, text, and action history with ==STRING== relative position encoding; up to **+30%** sim SR (**98.0%** Screwdriver-in-Caddy), wins 4/5 real tasks; dual encoder+policy pretraining.
- **[[2602.00807|Any3D-VLA]]** — A plug-in pipeline lifting 2D inputs into ==compressed 3D point clouds== fused at patch level via ==gated residual fusion==, trained on diverse cloud sources (sim GT, sensor, estimated depth) for depth robustness; **62.5%** real zero-shot SR (**+29.2%** over SpatialVLA), **+13.9%** over GraspVLA on LIBERO.
- **[[2503.08950|FP3]]** — A large-scale 3D ==foundation policy== over point clouds via a ==multimodal diffusion transformer== fusing third-person + wrist stereo clouds, CLIP language, and proprioception, pretrained on 60K DROID trajectories then LoRA-tuned; **100%** across 4 household tasks from 80 demos and in-the-wild lighting/object/background shifts, dropping to 30% with 2D images.
- **[[2510.03885|3D-Latent-Mapping]]** — A mobile-manipulation policy conditioned on an incrementally-built ==persistent multiresolution 3D latent feature map== as a state variable, with a ==global map token== aggregating scene-wide context (trainable by BC or RL); **1.00 vs 0.75** OOD sequential-manipulation SR over transient-3D baselines, zero-shot sim-to-real on a uFactory xArm6.
- **[[2509.15733|GP3]]** — A 3D geometry-aware policy using ==RoboVGGT== (a fine-tuned VGGT reconstruction encoder) over multi-view RGB with ==Global Attention-based FiLM== language modulation, no depth sensor; **86.7%** Meta-World and **78.7%** RLBench (**+9.8%/+26.7%** over implicit-3D SPA), transfers to Mobile ALOHA.
- **[[2508.11002|3D-FlowMatch-Actor]]** — A 3D policy replacing 3DDA's DDPM with ==Rectified Flow Matching== over a ==unified 3D Relative Denoising Transformer== jointly tokenizing both arms' trajectories + proprioception; **85.1%** PerAct2 bimanual (**+41.4%** over π0-keypose at ~1000× fewer params), **90.3%** HiveFormer-74, **30×** faster train/infer (18.2 Hz).
- **[[2505.10105|EmbodiedMAE]]** — A unified 3D multimodal ==masked autoencoder== jointly encoding RGB + depth + point clouds via modal-specific patchifiers + a ==ViT encoder with cross-modal decoder==, pretrained on the new depth-augmented DROID-3D; **65.4%** SO-100 (vs SPA 57.5%) and **95.4%** xArm (vs DINOv2-RGBD 84.6%), scaling with model size.
- **[[2505.16196|SEM]]** — A ==diffusion policy== projecting multi-view features + ==joint-centric robot states== into one 3D base frame via a ==Spatial Enhancer== + ==joint graph attention==; **78.38%** on 16 RoboTwin-2.0 tasks vs DP3's 56.25%, **+25.8%** real over RDT, slow camera-height decay.
- **[[2605.21258|Structural-Latent-Points]]** — A ==point-wise latent VAE== inside a PTv3 autoencoder with lightweight ==3DGS differentiable rendering== supervision; **0.56** mean SR on RLBench and **0.64** on ManiSkill2, **60×** faster pretraining, **84%** real place-mandarin.
- **[[2412.07215|RoboData]]** — A ==RoboTron-Mani== multimodal model with a ==UVFormer 3D adapter== + occupancy supervision, trained on nine unified datasets; **91.7%** LIBERO, **93.8%** CALVIN, **+19.6%** over RoboFlamingo; unifies 3D space and action representations across corpora.
- **[[2406.18977|RoboUniView]]** — A VLM policy decoupling visual representation from action via a ==UVFormer== that maps multi-camera views into one ==unified 3D representation==, pre-trained on RGB-D for 3D-occupancy prediction so features are camera-invariant; **3.855** avg length on CALVIN D→D, **95.6%** task1 under unseen camera params (<0.004 drop).
- **[[2503.19510|RoboFlamingo-Plus]]** — A depth-augmented extension of the ==RoboFlamingo VLM== fusing RGB + pre-processed depth through a frozen ViT + fine-tuned Perceiver Resampler with gated cross-attention, decoded by an LSTM-MLP head; a **10–20%** zero-shot generalization lead over RGB-only RoboFlamingo across novel CALVIN visual-depth environments.
- **[[2411.18623|Lift3D-Foundation-Policy]]** — A method ==lifting 2D foundation models (DINOv2/CLIP) into 3D==: a task-aware MAE reconstructs affordance-region depth, then a ==2D-model-lifting strategy== tokenizes point clouds onto virtual planes to reuse pretrained positional embeddings; **84.5%** Meta-World (**+18.2%** over DP3) and **88.0%** Adroit, **90%** real place-bottle from 30 demos.
- **[[2410.10803|Generalizable-Humanoid-Manipulation-3D]]** — An egocentric ==3D Diffusion Policy (iDP3)== for humanoid manipulation paired with an ==Apple-Vision-Pro whole-upper-body teleoperation== system on a Fourier GR1, using egocentric 3D point clouds + a pyramid conv encoder + extended horizon; zero-shot Pick&Place/Pour from single-scene data, validated over **2000+** real rollouts.
- **[[2410.08208|SPA]]** — Imbues a frozen 2D ==ViT== with 3D spatial awareness via ==differentiable neural rendering== (==SDF==, ==SH==, semantic fields) on multi-view images, extending the Generation-1 frozen-encoder recipe beyond R3M/VC-1/MVP; best-or-second SR on **11/13** benchmarks across **268** embodied tasks, zero-shot real-robot manipulation from **50** demos.
- **[[2408.05107|Depth-Helps]]** — A ==Depth Information Injection== framework adding 3D perception to a pretrained RGB policy via a ==Depth Completion Module== predicting depth features + a ==Depth-Aware Codebook== quantizing them against cumulative error; **63.15%** LIBERO with RGB-only inference (**0.8%** drop from RGB-D), **66.67%** real, no depth sensor at deployment.
- **[[2402.10885|3D-Diffuser-Actor]]** — A manipulation policy lifting observations into ==tokenized 3D scene representations== fused with language + proprioception, denoised by a ==3D diffusion transformer== with relative 3D attentions (rotary embeddings) for translation equivariance; **81.3%** RLBench multi-view (**+18.1%** over Act3D), **3.35** Avg. Len on CALVIN, real from 15 demos/task.
- **[[2308.16891|GNFactor]]** — A ==Generalizable Neural Feature Field== distilling Stable Diffusion semantic features into a 3D volumetric scene representation, jointly trained end-to-end with a ==Perceiver Transformer== keyframe-action policy on the shared voxel grid; **1.55×** over PerAct on RLBench multi-task, **40%** real 'relocate teapot' vs PerAct's 0%.
- **[[2306.17817|Act3D]]** — A language-conditioned transformer predicting 6-DoF keyposes over a ==continuous 3D feature field== via ==coarse-to-fine ghost-point sampling== + relative 3D cross-attention, lifting CLIP features into a 3D scene cloud; **83%** single-task / **65%** multi-task RLBench (**+10%/+22%** over PerAct), only **24%** view-shift drop vs 2D's 77%; the 3D-feature-field landmark.
- **[[2306.14896|RVT]]** — A ==multi-view transformer== reconstructing a 3D point cloud into strategic ==virtual views== with orthographic projection, fused via cross-attention and ==CLIP== language conditioning to predict actions from ==heatmaps==; **26%** higher SR than PerAct across 18 RLBench tasks at **36×** faster training and **2.3×** faster inference.
- **[[2405.04378|Splat-MOVER]]** — An open-vocabulary multi-stage manipulation framework over an ==editable Gaussian Splatting== scene (ASK-Splat distilling CLIP semantics + VRB grasp affordance, SEE-Splat real-time scene editing, Grasp-Splat affordance re-ranking); **100%** saucepan grasp vs LERF-TOGO's 40%, completing all 4 multi-stage tasks NeRF-based methods could not.
- **[[2505.07819|H$^3$DP]]** — A ==triply-hierarchical diffusion policy== layering RGB-D by depth, encoding each layer at ==multiple visual scales== (codebook-quantized), and generating actions ==coarse-to-fine== so low-frequency structure forms before fine detail; **+27.5%** relative over baselines across 44 sim tasks and **+32.3%** on real bimanual tasks, no manual point-cloud segmentation.
- **[[2410.17488|GenDP]]** — A category-level generalizable diffusion policy constructing ==3D semantic fields== from multi-view RGBD ==DINOv2== descriptors matched against category reference features, fed alongside the geometric point cloud to the action policy; **93%** SR on unseen instances vs vanilla Diffusion Policy's 20%, with semantics ablation dropping it to 58%.
- **[[2502.08449|CordViP]]** — A dexterous diffusion policy building ==interaction-aware 3D point clouds== from FoundationPose 6D estimation + digital twins + forward kinematics, pre-training an encoder on ==hand-object contact maps== and cross-attention hand-arm coordination; **>85%** over 4 base tasks and **80%** FlipCup on a Leap Hand, robust to lighting/viewpoint shifts.
- **[[2502.02562|STRING]]** — A unified ==Lie-group== generalization of rotary position encodings (Cayley- and Circulant-STRING variants) for 2D/3D Vision Transformers, integrating into ViT backbones; consistently beats RoPE on ImageNet/Places365 and on real robotics tasks with ALOHA 2 and KUKA, with better OOD generalization at comparable cost.

#### 2.2 Spatial-Reasoning VLMs for Manipulation

A complementary route: don't bake 3D into the policy — train a VLM to reason spatially, then use it as a perception oracle or VLA backbone. These papers build the spatial-reasoning competence manipulation depends on.

- **[[2406.01584|SpatialRGPT]]** — A ==region-aware spatial VLM== trained on auto-generated 3D scene graphs with a ==relative-depth plugin==; **>90%** qualitative spatial accuracy and **+20%** on BLINK relative depth; serves as a dense distance-reward annotator for robot tasks.
- **[[2511.05491|VST]]** — A ==Visual Spatial Tuning== recipe with VST-P/VST-R datasets and a three-stage SFT→CoT→GRPO pipeline; **61.2%** average spatial score, **44.2%** AP@15 3D detection without a 3D encoder, and **+8.6%** LIBERO SR when used as a VLA backbone.
