- **[[2603.27967|XVR]]** — A 100K ==cross-view-relations== VQA dataset built on ==Structure-from-Motion== correspondence/verification/localization tasks; Qwen3-VL-2B-XVR hits **68.06%** (beats GPT-5's 61.74%), and as a VLA backbone adds **+13%** absolute manipulation SR.
- **[[2602.19063|Direction-aware-3D-LMM]]** — A pose-aware 3D LMM whose ==PoseRecover== + ==PoseAlign== modules inject mission-critical ego-pose; **+30.0%** mIoU on ScanRefer (42.6→55.4) and **+11.7%** Scan2Cap; resolves directional ambiguity that breaks 3D grounding.
- **[[2601.05172|CoV]]** — A training-free ==Chain-of-View prompting== scheme turning a passive VLM into an active viewpoint reasoner via coarse selection + iterative camera actions; **+11.56%** LLM-Match on OpenEQA, SOTA **116** CIDEr on ScanQA; scales with exploration budget.
- **[[2603.00905|pySpatial]]** — A ==visual programming== approach that has an MLLM emit Python orchestrating spatial tools (3D reconstruction, novel-view synthesis, camera ops); **58.56%** on MINDCUBE (+12.94% over GPT-4.1-mini), zero-shot quadruped navigation.
- **[[2503.18769|AlphaSpace]]** — Fine-tunes a lightweight decoder-only LLM (==DeepSeek-R1-distil-Qwen-1.5B==) on synthetic tokenized 3D object configurations + 7-D action plans with reasoning annotations, encoding spatial semantics via ==semantic tokenization== instead of implicit visual features; **66.67%** on EmbodiedBench-Manipulation vs GPT-4o's 37.5% and Claude 3.5 Sonnet's 29.17%.
- **[[2503.08481|PhysVLM]]** — A dual-branch VLM encoding a robot-agnostic ==Space-Physical Reachability Map== alongside egocentric RGB so the model reasons about physical reachability, trained on Phys100K; **71.0%** EQA-phys (vs GPT-4o's 57%), **>63%** zero-shot on real UR3/XArm6, **48.4%** task planning with unreachable objects vs baselines' 21.5–35.8%.
- **[[2501.10074|SpatialCoT]]** — A two-stage method pairing ==Spatial Coordinate Bi-directional Alignment== with ==Chain-of-Thought Spatial Grounding== so a VLM both reasons spatially and emits fine-grained actions; **82.57%** manipulation SR / **61.83%** navigation SR (beating GPT-4o + RoboPoint), with the largest gains on non-unique-object + crowded-scene tasks.

#### 2.3 View-Robust & Object-Centric Encoders

A policy that overfits one camera angle or confuses two similar objects fails silently — these encoders harden the perception front-end against viewpoint shift, clutter, and attribute ambiguity.

- **[[2608.13422|Seeker]]** — A ==FiLM==-initialized query ==cross-attends== over frozen ==DINOv3== patches through ==query-dependent head gating==, trained only by a ==diffusion== action-prediction loss, then reused for RGB cropping, mask augmentation, and point-cloud filtering; **62.6%** MimicGen vs **42.6%** best crop baseline, real **76.7%** vs **48.3%**.
- **[[2608.11870|RoboSaGA]]** — ==FullGrad== encoder-saliency sets per-pixel blending weights for out-of-domain image overlay, clipped at **0.8** so even task-critical pixels get subtle perturbation, cached in a ==global saliency buffer==; cuts the distractor/background gap **0.60→0.14** sim, **0.71→0.05** real (**72%** relative over Random Overlay).
- **[[2608.05579|ARGUS]]** — A viewpoint-canonicalization pipeline lifting multi-view RGB into a ==metrically-scaled 3D point cloud== (VGGT), then rendering a fixed ==canonical virtual viewpoint== RGB image; higher SR across **5** novel camera configs, **90%** SR reached **6×** faster than KYC and **4×** faster than classical Diffusion Policy.
- **[[2608.03295|GraspMeanFlow]]** — Extends ==MeanFlow== to SE(3) via a ==finite-interval average-velocity== field, trained with a flow-matching boundary + consistency term over an ==SE(3)-equivariant Vector Neuron (VN-DGCNN)== backbone; **96.3%** sim lift success at **10 NFEs** (vs EquiGraspFlow's 91.2%), one-step (**NFE 1**) generation in **56.3 ms**.
- **[[2607.29622|Ray-Conditioned ViT]]** — Injects per-patch ==Plücker ray-map== features as positional embeddings plus a ==gated cross-attention== ==ray-conditioned class token== into a pretrained ViT, aligned by a ==cross-view cosine== loss; under camera perturbation reaches RoboCasa **37.3%** vs RGB's **24.0%**, real **2.69** vs **0.91** completed stages.
- **[[2607.18236|Patch Policy]]** — Feeds uncompressed dense ==ViT patch features== straight into a ==Transformer policy head== under a ==block-causal attention mask== (full intra-frame, causal inter-frame), keeping the encoder ==frozen== for Internet-scale transfer; **70%** real Cable Insertion vs **30%** OpenVLA-OFT at **~0.7%** of its parameters and **5×** faster (**10.99 ms**).
- **[[2607.05396|CamVLA]]** — A calibration-free VLA decomposing the policy into an ==Action Head== predicting end-effector actions in a ==camera-centric frame== and a ==Geometric Head== regressing the ==6-DoF hand-eye matrix== from visual features; **+18.2%** unseen-camera SR in RLBench (with π0), robust to **15°** viewpoint offsets real at **0.19%** parameter overhead.
- **[[2606.23625|See2Act]]** — A diffusion policy folding full ==6-DoF camera movements== into the action denoising loop to jointly refine actions and viewpoints, interpolating each step's camera between a global and an action-derived target view and re-observing; up to **96%** on occluded Ravens (vs fixed-view 0–2%), **95%** real UR10 from 50 demos, **81.1%** over 9 RLBench tasks.
- **[[2606.01847|SE3-VLA]]** — A diffusion policy (LDA) correcting the "Euclidean fallacy" by defining the generative process on the ==SE(3) Lie group== via left-invariant SDEs (exp-map noising, twist prediction in se(3)) so poses stay valid and ==coordinate-frame equivariant==; **+7.3%** zero-shot CALVIN length, **100%** real Move-Doll, rotation orthogonality error ~10⁻⁷ vs Euclidean O(10⁰).
- **[[2605.17033|Actionable-Parts-Pose]]** — A symmetry-annotation-free 6D pose estimator for Generalizable Actionable Parts using ==two-stage quaternion regression== over multiple candidates + a ==self-supervised symmetry-distribution learner== with a ==HyperS3 graph-conv== backbone; **3.23°** seen / **10.83°** unseen rotation error on GAPartNet, ablating symmetry learning cuts seen error 79.9%.
- **[[2604.21914|VistaBot]]** — A ==4D geometry estimation== + ==conditional video diffusion== pipeline synthesizing training-view-aligned latents from novel cameras; lifts ACT's view-generalization score **0.24→0.67** and π₀'s **0.33→0.87** in sim, **0.21→0.72** real, at ~3 Hz.
- **[[2604.02759|OMNI-PoseX]]** — A fast open-world ==6D object-pose estimator== pairing open-vocabulary perception (segmentation + depth) with an ==SO(3)-aware reflected flow-matching== head learning a geodesic-consistent velocity field in the ==Lie algebra==; **32.17°** rotation / **1.27 cm** translation on OMNI-6DPose at **17 ms** (~2× faster than GenPose++), zero-shot to unseen objects.
- **[[2512.05230|Invariance Co-training]]** — Adds a ==hinge-margin contrastive alignment loss== plus ==camera-extrinsics== and ==bounding-box regression== heads on cheap static multi-view/multi-lighting data, co-optimized with a ==behavior-cloning diffusion policy==; **+40%** real SR over BC, **+18%** over generative-augmentation baselines.
- **[[2509.18644|State-Free-Visuomotor-Policy]]** — A policy dropping ==proprioceptive state== entirely, using a ==relative-EEF action space== + dual wrist cameras for pose-invariance; **98%** height / **58%** horizontal generalization vs state-based 0%, across π0/ACT/DP and multiple embodiments.
- **[[2509.17684|DINOv3-Diffusion Policy]]** — Swaps a ==FiLM-conditioned U-Net== diffusion policy's ==ResNet-18== visual encoder for self-supervised ==DINOv3== ViT-S/16, comparing scratch/frozen/finetuned regimes; finetuned DINOv3 reaches **0.90** vs ResNet's **0.80** on Can (**+10pt**), frozen DINOv3 **0.60** vs frozen ResNet's **0.10** on Square.
- **[[2508.19391|LaVA-Man]]** — Self-supervised ==goal-image prediction== pretext task with ==asymmetric masking== (only the goal image masked) to force causal action-outcome learning, trained on the new **3,200**-object ==Omni-Object Pick-and-Place== dataset; **0.81** Ravens, **79.6%** OOPP, **79.2%** Franka Kitchen SOTA among self-supervised methods.
- **[[2508.18802|HyperTASR]]** — A ==hypernetwork==-driven autoencoding transform dynamically generating scene-representation parameters conditioned on task language + a VAE-encoded progression state, dropping in on GNFactor/3D Diffuser Actor; **+27.9%** relative over GNFactor (**42.6%** RLBench), **81.3%** with 3D Diffuser Actor, new single-view SOTA.
- **[[2508.08113|AimBot]]** — Overlays ==shooting lines== + ==scope reticles== onto RGB inputs as a training-free geometric spatial cue (depth + EE pose projection), color-coded for gripper state; **+5.8%** LIBERO-Long, real OpenVLA-OFT **21/50→36/50**, **<1ms** overhead vs RoboPoint/TraceVLA's 0.3–5s+.
- **[[2508.05186|TVVE]]** — A ==virtual-view exploration== method actively selecting informative camera poses + a ==task-aware MoE== visual encoder for occlusion-robust multi-task control; **86.6%** over 18 RLBench tasks (**+5%** over ARP), **67.0%** on occlusion-stressed RLBench-OG, **88%** real Dobot.
- **[[2605.25495|RepSAM]]** — A ==CKA-guided multi-scale LoRA== that allocates adaptation by layer-wise domain shift + depth-aware fusion; **89.0%** mIoU (97.9% of full fine-tune) at **158×** fewer params, **93.2%** grasp on standard / **82.1%** transparent objects.
- **[[2605.13428|SID]]** — A ==Sliding into Distribution== framework pairing an ==object-centric motion field== that iteratively slides OOD states back toward the demonstrated manifold with an ==egocentric execution policy== + kinematically-consistent ==egocentric data augmentation==; **~90%** SR under OOD initializations from two raw demos, **82–88%** robust to disturbances.
- **[[2601.17486|EquiForm]]** — A noise-robust ==SE(3)-equivariant== policy correcting equivariance deviations via a ==geometric denoising module== (normal/tangent-direction correction) + a contrastive-regularized equivariant encoder feeding a diffusion action head; **66.6%** over 16 sim benchmarks (**+24.3%** over DP3) with large gains on noisy MimicGen, **+28.1%** real average.
- **[[2601.11269|X-Distill]]** — A ==cross-architecture vision distillation== transferring a frozen DINOv2 ViT-L teacher into a compact ResNet-18 student on ImageNet, then fine-tuned end-to-end with a ==diffusion policy head==; **87.2%** over 34 sim tasks (10 demos) and **75.6%** real (20–25 demos), beating fine-tuned DINOv2 and VLAs like π0 at data efficiency.
- **[[2508.01600|CLASS]]** — A representation-learning method training a vision encoder with a ==soft supervised-contrastive objective== that pulls observations with similar future ==action sequences== (weighted by DTW similarity) together; **85%** (MLP) / **91%** (DP) over 5 sim tasks vs 63%/77% baselines, **+45–55%** real final-completion under randomly repositioned cameras.
- **[[2506.16211|ControlVLA]]** — A few-shot ==object-centric VLA adaptation== conditioning a pretrained diffusion-transformer VLA on GroundingDINO+SAM2 object features via a ==ControlNet-style dual-attention== with zero-initialized KV layers; **76.7%** over 6 short-horizon tasks from 10–20 demos (vs DP's 20.8%), **60.0%** long-horizon (~3× over π0), **70%** unseen objects.
- **[[2506.13867|ATK]]** — A distillation pipeline jointly learning a ==sparse masking model== and a keypoint policy to select a minimal set of task-relevant, trackable ==2D keypoints== via correspondence-function tracking; **~64%** avg sim-to-real SR under visual disturbance (vs RGB/Depth/PointCloud's **2–4%**), beating GPT-based keypoint selection.
- **[[2506.06196|Mid-Level MoE]]** — A ==Mixture-of-Experts== diffusion policy fusing four spatially-grounded mid-level representations via ==attention-based gating== + ==cross-attention==, trained with a "==Self-Consistency weighted imitation-learning==" objective; **69.6%** avg over 8 dexterous bimanual tasks (**+24.5%** over baseline), self-consistency adding **+10%** real SR.
- **[[2505.21351|EquAct]]** — An ==SE(3)-equivariant multi-task transformer== with an ==Equivariant Point Transformer U-Net== (spherical Fourier features, equivariant pooling) + ==iFiLM== layers enforcing SE(3)-invariant language conditioning; **53.3%** on 10-demo SE(3)-randomized tasks (**+15.4%** over best baseline), **65.0%** real vs 12.5%, equivariance ablation collapsing SR 52.8→12.3%.
- **[[2505.13431|Practical-Guide-Incorporating-Symmetry]]** — A study showing simple symmetry recipes match complex equivariant nets: ==relative trajectory actions== + eye-in-hand views induce SE(3)-invariance, and ==Frame Averaging== adds rotation equivariance to pretrained encoders without architecture change; relative actions **+5.9–7.4%**, equivariant encoder **+9.1%**, FA **+4.1%** average.
- **[[2505.11719|Zero-Shot-diffusion]]** — A visual-generalization method pairing ==Associative Latent Disentanglement== (ALDA) representations that "recall" known observations under OOD inputs with ==learned-canonicalization finetuning== for planar camera-rotation equivariance; lifts rotated PickCube 1.6–4.19% → **76.9–96.9%**, **80%** real (60% with distractors) where baselines hit 0%.
- **[[2504.21530|RoboGround]]** — A grounding-conditioned policy injecting ==VLM grounding masks== as an intermediate representation (coarser than goal images, lighter than point flow) via channel-concat + patch attention, with an automated data-generation pipeline; improves zero-shot generalization to unseen objects/categories and distractor-heavy scenes across instruction types.
- **[[2502.09389|S$^2$-Diffusion]]** — A ==DDPM policy== replacing raw RGB with a ==spatial-semantic representation== from Grounded-SAM2 zero-shot masks + DepthAnythingV2 relative depth over one RGB camera, lifting instance-level skills to category-level; **0.75–0.88** RoboCasa and **0.90** on unseen instances, **89–100%** real whiteboard-wiping on unseen colors vs an RGB-DP's 0–44%.
- **[[2407.00451|Language-Guided]]** — A language-guided ==object-centric diffusion policy== (Lan-o3dp) fusing point clouds with ==open-vocabulary segmentation== that an LLM uses to flag targets and obstacles, plus training-free ==cost-guided collision avoidance== in denoising; **68.7%** over 21 RLBench tasks (vs DP's 39.3%, DP3's 43.6%), zero-shot avoidance of language-specified obstacles.
- **[[2503.09867|OH-A-DINO]]** — A ==DINOv2== augmentation adding ==object-centric VAE latents== via PCA segmentation for attribute-level retrieval; **56.4%** Top-10 multi-object precision vs DINOv2's 13.0% and **96.6%** color precision vs 40.8%; fixes attribute-blind self-supervised features.
- **[[2503.04877|Adapt3R]]** — A 3D observation encoder decoupling semantic reasoning (frozen 2D ==CLIP ResNet==) from 3D localization (point clouds canonicalized to the end-effector frame + Fourier encoding + attention pooling), plug-in to ACT/Diffusion-Policy/BAKU; **<6%** drop on unseen camera poses (vs RGB **44.4%**), zero-shot cross-embodiment, **93** LIBERO-90 at **44.1 Hz**.
- **[[2503.02387|RGBSQGrasp]]** — A graspability-aware bin-picking framework trained on a ==cross-platform synthetic superquadric dataset==, inferring local ==superquadric primitives== from a single RGB image via Depth Pro + SAM 2 partial point clouds; **92%** grasp SR on a UR5e over 189 cluttered picks (vs **79.7–80.5%** baselines).
- **[[2506.10359|Multi-Suction Pick Success]]** — A multi-modal ==MultiMAE== encoder self-supervised-pretrained on 343K in-domain RGB/depth/semantic images then fine-tuned for pick-success prediction, fusing pick features (coordinates, suction pistons) via ==cross-attention==; **90.6 AUC** vs a shallow expert-feature model's 85.1, real deployment cutting mispicks **38%** and amnesty **41%**.
- **[[2212.08333|AnyGrasp]]** — A unified ==7-DoF grasp perception== system: a ==geometry processing module== predicts dense grasps with a ==stable score== while a ==temporal association module== tracks grasps on moving objects; **93.3%** bin-clearing on **300+** unseen objects (near human **93.9%**) at **>900 MPPH** — the reference real-time grasp detector for cluttered, dynamic scenes.
- **[[2210.03094|VIMA]]** — A manipulation agent casting diverse tasks as ==multimodal-prompt sequence modeling== (interleaved text + visual tokens) over ==object-centric Mask-R-CNN tokens== + cross-attention; **2.9×** higher SR than transformer baselines, matching 10× more-data baselines at **1%** data, zero-shot to L1–L3 — the canonical object-centric prompted manipulation agent.
- **[[2407.01812|Equivariant-Diffusion-Policy]]** — A diffusion policy embedding ==SO(2)-equivariant networks== into the denoising function, proving the noise-prediction function is equivariant when the expert policy is, with derived SO(2) representations for 6-DoF actions; **+21.9%** SR over the best non-equivariant baseline on MimicGen, up to **5×** sample efficiency, **80%** real bagel-baking.
- **[[2407.01479|EquiBot]]** — A ==SIM(3)-equivariant diffusion policy== embedding equivariance into the denoising network via a PointNet encoder that canonicalizes proprioception + noisy actions by learned object centroid/scale fed to an SO(3)-equivariant U-net; least degradation across rotation/scale/position OOD, **8/10** real Push-Chair (vs 0/10 DP) from **25** demos.
- **[[2406.11740|Imagination-Policy]]** — A keyframe pick-place method factoring action into a ==generative point-cloud model== that "imagines" target clouds, then recovers the transform via ==registration (SVD)==, trained with ==SE(3) bi-equivariant== augmentation; **26.67%** Plug-Charger (vs 0%) and **97.33%** Stack-Wine from 5 demos, **80–93.3%** real UR5 without sim pretraining.
- **[[2505.10359|NVSPolicy]]** — A language-conditioned policy using ==adaptive novel-view synthesis== (a pretrained generative model rendering context-enhancing viewpoints) plus a ==cycle-consistent VAE== disentangling semantic from imperfection features in a hierarchical policy; **56.3%** CALVIN three-task SR, **44%** real average across five tasks vs SPIL's 8%.
- **[[2505.20814|Spatial-RoboGrasp]]** — A grasping policy unifying ==domain-randomized RGB augmentation (AugFusion)==, monocular depth (Depth Anything V2), and ==YOLO-based 6-DoF grasp prediction== as explicit spatial guidance for a diffusion action head, no 3D sensor; **15–30%** TSR/GSR gains under varied conditions, **82%** PickCup TSR under extreme lighting vs Diffusion Policy's 27%.
- **[[2502.20037|FuseGrasp]]** — A ==radar-camera fusion== system for grasping transparent objects, built on a self-collected ==RGB-D-Radar dataset== with ==SAR robotic imaging== + two-stage depth training (TransCG pretrain → radar fine-tune); **93.8%** real grasp SR (**+45.9%** over camera-only), **90.0%** material-ID accuracy.
- **[[2502.03072|RoboGrasp]]** — A ==grasp-aware diffusion policy== augmenting a Diffusion Policy with a fine-tuned ==YOLOv11== Grasp Detection Module predicting 2D grasp-box coordinates as explicit spatial guidance fused into the observation encoder; **+33.75%** PickBig / **+28.7%** prompt-based TSR over DP, **100%** few-shot TSR/GSR on novel cups, promptable retail grasping.
- **[[2501.14400|SKIL]]** — A ==Semantic Keypoints Description Module== distilling Vision Foundation Models (DiFT, SAM) into sparse 3D keypoint descriptors feeding a Transformer + ==diffusion action head==, extended by **SKIL-H** to ==cross-embodiment learning== from human videos; **72.8%** mean SR on unseen objects (**2×** best baseline), **72%**/**47%** on Hang Towel/Cloth from 30 demos.
- **[[2505.11563|Object-Centric-manipulation]]** — An empirical study using ==object-centric slot representations== (DINOSAUR/VIDEOSAUR) as the visuomotor encoder, robot-data-pre-trained with temporal dynamics, benchmarked against global/dense encoders; **+9%** LIBERO-90 / **+26%** real over the best dense model, lowest SR drop under texture/lighting shift.
- **[[2503.03890|LensDFF]]** — A few-shot dexterous policy distilling ==language-enhanced sparse 3D feature fields== aligned to CLIP embeddings with **five** grasp primitives + a SAM2/FoundationPose real2sim eval pipeline; **64%** real (vs SparseDFF 54%, F3RM 60%) and **40.8%** sim at lower compute than dense-feature methods.
- **[[2411.16802|Leveraging-Foundation-Models]]** — A teacher-student framework where ==DINO== attention maps guide ==SAM2== segmentation to auto-generate mask/keypoint labels for semi-fluid deformable objects (weld pools), distilled into a lightweight ==ResNet-VAE== student for real-time keypoint inference; **13.4 px** mean Euclidean distance, beating Active-Contour/KCF/SIFT baselines.
- **[[2409.03685|VISTA-View-Invariant]]** — Replaces each demo frame with a ==ZeroNVS== render at a randomly sampled camera pose (action label fixed), rejecting bad renders via an ==LPIPS== threshold, then finetunes NVS in-domain on MimicGen/DROID; lifts Threading **9.3%→23.3%**, Nut Assembly **16.7%→36.0%**, real Franka completion **4/40→20/50**.
- **[[2310.03478|RGBManip]]** — An eye-on-hand monocular framework inferring 6D pose via ==kinematics-guided multi-view feature fusion== without explicit depth, paired with a ==PPO== Global Scheduling Policy balancing active-viewpoint exploration against manipulation and closed-loop ==impedance control==; **88.9%** Open-Door SR vs the best RGBD/RGB baseline's **74.5%**, deployed on a real Franka.
- **[[1909.06933|DD Policy]]** — A self-supervised ==Dense Descriptor== ResNet establishing pixel-to-pixel correspondences across views, feeding ==behavioral cloning== policies via four correspondence-integration strategies with noise-augmented closed-loop stabilization; **97.7%** real flip-shoe SR from **50-150** demos, generalizing across object classes.

**3D & Spatial Representation — Decision Matrix**

| Need | Recommendation |
|---|---|
| Sample-efficient 3D diffusion baseline | [[2403.03954\|DP3]] (**74.4%**, 10 demos) |
| High-precision 6-DoF from RGB-D | [[2209.05451\|PerAct]] (**34×** over 2D) |
| Strongest 3D policy on RoboTwin/ManiSkill | [[2604.15281\|R3D]] (**83.8%** / **55.2%**) |
| Spatial reasoning as a VLA backbone | [[2511.05491\|VST]] (**+8.6%**), [[2603.27967\|XVR]] (**+13%**) |
| Dense spatial reward annotation | [[2406.01584\|SpatialRGPT]] (**>90%**) |
| View-robust closed-loop manipulation | [[2604.21914\|VistaBot]] (VGS **0.24→0.67**) |
| Sim-ready 3D scene reconstruction | [[2605.26115\|TriSplat]] (**33–249×** faster) |
| Object-centric / attribute disambiguation | [[2503.09867\|OH-A-DINO]] (**56.4%** retrieval) |
| Parameter-efficient robotic segmentation | [[2605.25495\|RepSAM]] (**158×** fewer params) |

^dm-2

> [!star] Key Papers — 3D Representation Landmarks
> - [[2209.05451|PerAct]] — the voxel-classification landmark that proved discretized 6-DoF action prediction over a 3D grid beats image-to-action regression by an order of magnitude.
> - [[2403.03954|DP3]] — established that a *minimal* point-cloud encoder, not an elaborate one, is enough to make diffusion policies sample-efficient; the reference 3D-diffusion baseline.
> - [[2406.01584|SpatialRGPT]] — the canonical region-aware spatial VLM; showed automatically-mined 3D scene graphs can teach metric spatial reasoning at scale.
> - [[2604.21914|VistaBot]] — first to treat viewpoint robustness as a *generative* problem — synthesize the canonical view rather than train invariance into the policy.
> - [[2511.05491|VST]] — mapped the SFT→CoT→RL recipe that turns a general VLM into a spatial reasoner usable as a VLA backbone.

^key-papers-2

> [!tip] Two Routes to 3D — Bake It In or Query an Oracle
> The field has bifurcated. One camp ([[2403.03954|DP3]], [[2604.15281|R3D]], [[2602.00937|CLAMP]]) bakes geometry into the policy's perception module — fast, end-to-end, but the 3D competence is locked to the training distribution. The other ([[2406.01584|SpatialRGPT]], [[2511.05491|VST]], [[2603.27967|XVR]]) trains a *separate* spatial-reasoning VLM the policy queries — slower and more modular, but the spatial competence transfers across tasks and is reusable. The surprising 2026 finding is that the VLM route now *feeds* the policy route: XVR and VST report that a spatially-tuned VLM backbone adds **+13%** and **+8.6%** manipulation SR respectively — spatial reasoning learned in pixel-space transfers into action-space. For how this spatial substrate becomes a planning prior, see [[10_Manipulation-Skill-Learning#5. LLM / Neuro-Symbolic Planning & Affordance Grounding]]; for the latent-prediction lineage of 3D world models, see [[07_Latent-World-Models#3. Broader Latent Prediction Landscape]].

^insight-2

---

## Part B — Reasoning & Memory

*Once a task spans more than one contact, the policy must imagine futures, remember what it saw, and decompose goals — the world-model, memory, and planning layers above the backbone.*

### 3. World-Model & Video-as-Policy

A reactive policy answers "what action now?"; a world-model policy answers "what happens if?" — and for multi-step manipulation, the second question is the one that matters. Predicting a future (in pixels, latents, masks, or object flow) gives the policy a planning surface: it can roll out candidate actions, check whether the imagined outcome matches the goal, and only then commit. This section is the manipulation field's bet that *imagination is cheaper than trial-and-error*, and that internet-scale video already contains the dynamics a policy needs.

Three sub-strategies diverge on *what* to predict. World-action models (3.1) predict compact, action-conditioned latents and use them as a frozen simulator for policy optimization. Video-as-policy (3.2) generates a full task-solving video and extracts actions from it — leveraging pretrained video diffusion as a dynamics prior. Flow and motion-token bridges (3.3) predict an intermediate, embodiment-agnostic motion representation (2D/3D flow, latent tokens) that decouples *what should move* from *which robot moves it*. The tension across all three: richer prediction targets give better planning but cost more inference, and pixel-perfect futures are usually overkill — the winning systems predict only the *task-relevant* part of the future.

#### 3.1 World-Action Models for Manipulation

Predict compact, action-conditioned future latents, then use that learned dynamics model as a frozen simulator for policy optimization or as a planning surface.

- **[[2608.03701|LiLa-WAM]]** — A lightweight ==latent-reasoning WAM== unifying future-state prediction and action generation in one stream over a frozen ==DINOv3== encoder, with a language-free ==Visual Transition Token== task cue and a foresight loss shaping the latent; **90.48%** RoboTwin 2.0 / **97.1%** LIBERO at **10-16×** fewer params (**0.5B**), trainable on one 24GB GPU.
- **[[2608.02365|Faster-WAM]]** — The ==Dock of Transformers== framework treats a pretrained video WAM as a ==representation hub== a single-layer action head "docks" into via ==KV-Fusion== aggregation + ==video–action RoPE alignment==, decoupling action-head depth from the video backbone; **66.5 ms** latency (**3.2×** over Fast-WAM), **98.5%** LIBERO, **+23.5pp** LIBERO-Plus OOD generalization.
- **[[2607.24538|NEO]]** — The explicit-representation counterpoint to latent WAMs: a language-guided ==three-stage NeRF editing pipeline== (==adaptive ray resampling==, ==multiview inpainting==, ==teacher-student distillation==) writes planned object motion into the field's weights, so post-action state needs no re-scan; **27.20 dB** PSNR, **6 min** inpainting (**15×** faster than NeRFiller).
- **[[2607.20743|Bio-Inspired Self-Supervised Trajectory Planner]]** — Trains obstacle-aware ==Forward== and ==Inverse Models== internally supervising a recurrent ==Trajectory Model== via ==self-supervised rectification==, mitigated by ==supervised pretraining== + ==end-effector geometric priors==; **7.5%** collision rate / **92.5%** collision-free success, smaller models generalizing better.
- **[[2607.14739|FoMoVLA]]** — A training-only VLA augmentation pairing ==future feature prediction== (Foresight tokens vs. an EMA teacher) with ==point tracking== (CoTracker-v3 2D motion) via ==Future-Conditioned Cross-Attention==, Foresight tokens persisting at inference (**+9.4 ms**); **98.8%** LIBERO, **80.5%** LIBERO-Plus zero-shot (**+6.4pp** over StarVLA), **56.9%** RoboCasa GR-1.
- **[[2606.27374|REGEN]]** — A continual-imitation framework using a World Action Model's generative ability to synthesize ==pseudo-demonstrations== for prior tasks (recursively conditioning on its own generated obs/actions), then BC-fine-tuning on the mix so old skills rehearse without stored demos; cuts Negative Backward Transfer **>50%** on LIBERO / **~40%** real while keeping Forward Transfer.
- **[[2606.26025|ICWM]]** — An ==in-context world model== reformulating a VLA to condition actions on world dynamics implicitly inferred from a short ==active-probing== history (random moves yielding obs-action-obs tuples), prepended to the task query for parameter-free adaptation to novel viewpoints/morphology; **+13.0%** OOD-viewpoint SR in sim, **71%** over a Multi-View baseline real on a UR5e.
- **[[2606.24742|WVM]]** — A ==World Value Model== fusing a video world model with value estimation, trained as a ==distributional value chunk== via flow matching for dense, expressive supervision, plus Suboptimal-Value-Bench (800 annotated hesitation/retry trajectories); **0.05** Hesitation-RMSE (vs 0.14) and **0.95** Value-Order Correlation, lifting AWR/Filtered-BC over vanilla BC.
- **[[2606.21501|UniviewVLA]]** — A multiview VLA training a ==generative world model== to predict future visual states from auxiliary viewpoints using only agent + wrist views, with ==motion-informative token compression== (625→16) and training-free ==min-action-entropy view selection==; **4.60** CALVIN ABCD→D / **95.8%** LIBERO, **40.0→73.3%** on occlusion tasks matching a physical third camera.
- **[[2606.20562|MemoryWAM]]** — A World Action Model with a hybrid ==Mixture-of-Transformers== memory: a sliding-window short-term store, persistent ==event-boundary anchor frames==, and ==gist tokens== (8/frame, 15× compression) for long-range history; **83.0%** RMBench (vs full-history LingBot-VA's 78.2%, FastWAM's 5.9%), 18/20 real Shell-Game at full-attention SR with lower latency/GPU memory.
- **[[2606.19531|ImageWAM]]** — A World Action Model repurposing a pretrained ==image-editing backbone== as the visual prior, extracting compact ==editing-aware KV-cache representations== for an action expert under a joint flow-matching objective, replacing full video generation; **93.56%** RoboTwin 2.0 and **83.1%** LIBERO-Plus (vs FastWAM's 51.5%), **~75%** lower latency / **~83%** fewer FLOPs.
- **[[2606.06041|iCEM+TL]]** — A ==sample-efficient Cross-Entropy Method== with zero-shot sampling-distribution ==transfer learning== and modular ==reward redesign==; **+23.0%** absolute on FetchStack over vanilla iCEM, beating CEE-US and PointFlowMatch; real Franka stacking transfer.
- **[[2604.19683|MWM]]** — A ==Mask World Model== shifting the prediction target from RGB to ==future semantic masks==, a geometric bottleneck, then fine-tuning a diffusion policy head; **0.983** LIBERO, **67.5%** real vs RGB baseline GE-ACT's 23.8%, **42.1%** OOD-SR; masks supervise but inference is RGB-only.
- **[[2604.16391|DeFI]]** — A backbone-pretraining scheme ==disentangling separate forward and inverse dynamics==, the forward branch acting as the world-model objective; CALVIN ABC-D multi-view avg length **4.51** (beats OpenVLA **3.27**, VPP **4.33**) using only ~**60%** of the data, and **81.3%** real Franka Panda vs Diffusion Policy **48.2%** / OpenVLA **43.8%**.
- **[[2511.04812|MDF]]** — A ==multimodal diffusion forcing== model using a 2D time-modality noise matrix to let one model act as policy / world model / inverse dynamics / anomaly detector; **100%** Nut-Thread vs DP3's 96%, **4%** vs 18% noise-induced drop, **66%** time-modality anomaly localization.
- **[[2511.03077|WorldPlanner]]** — A planner learning a ==diffusion visual world model==, action prior, and image-conditioned rewards from play data, then doing global ==Monte Carlo Tree Search== rollouts over the world model (action-prior-guided) with a local zeroth-order ==MPC== closing the loop; **97%** push-T vs Diffusion Policy's 88%, 64-step/11 s predictions, task-swappable via the reward.
- **[[2603.28955|WAM]]** — A ==DreamerV2== augmentation adding an ==inverse-dynamics head== that regularizes latents toward action-relevant content; **+8.7×** fewer WM steps for better imagination quality, CALVIN BC **61.7%** vs DiWA's 45.8% and **92.8%** post-PPO; frozen latent simulator.
- **[[2603.12553|Structured-WM-Planner]]** — A generative WM reformulated as an explicit planner over ==kinematically-grounded sparse subgoal frames==; **94.8%** LIBERO and **75.0%** SimplerEnv-WidowX, **87.5%** real pick-place, **80%** on unseen objects and under human intervention.
- **[[2605.20752|GaussianDream]]** — A policy injecting ==renderable 3D Gaussian== prefix tokens with current + future Gaussian supervision, discarded at inference; **98.4%** LIBERO, real **34.4%→50.0%** on spatial/long-horizon tasks at **531 ms**/chunk via asymmetric train-infer design.
- **[[2605.18373|Koopman-Cloth-Folding]]** — An MPC framework for dynamic single-grasp cloth folding pairing a physics simulator with an efficient ==Koopman-operator surrogate== (Nyström-regularized linear model) for receding-horizon control; zero-shot sim-to-real on a UR5 with **5.18%** wool / **2.93%** denim fold error under **1.5 s**, linearizing deformable dynamics for derivative-free control.
- **[[2605.14805|Cross-Coupled]]** — A residual-dynamics learner for aerial manipulators pairing an offline ==temporal + cross-variable attention encoder== over history with an online Kalman-updated linear decoder using ==consistency-driven covariance inflation==; lowest open-loop RMSE **0.18** (vs DNN 0.34) and lowest closed-loop tracking error, fast adaptation to payload-drop regime shifts.
- **[[2605.14937|Slot-MPC]]** — A goal-conditioned ==gradient-based MPC== over ==object-centric slot representations== (SAVi parsing + a transformer ==Conditional Object-Centric Predictor== forecasting future slots), warm-started by a BC policy; matches Dreamer-v3 on Meta-World, beats baselines on robosuite (Stack 0.42), planning at **0.42 s**/step (**~99%** faster than DINO-WM).
- **[[2512.24974|DLO-Planning]]** — A deformable-linear-object framework pairing a hierarchical planner (clearance-aware RRT* + Mass-Spring optimization) with a neural ==MPC tracker over a Transformer Deformation Model== (decoder-only online fine-tuning) for collision-free cable manipulation in clutter; **54.7–70.4%** lower long-horizon error, **1.58–1.94 cm** target error in real trials.
- **[[2512.16811|GeoPredict]]** — A VLA predicting future ==robot keypoint tracks== + ==3D Gaussian scene geometry== purely as training-time supervision shaping its representations, zero inference cost; **+10.1pp** on RoboCasa Human-50 (42.3→52.4%), **96.5%** LIBERO, real spatial generalization **60→85%**.
- **[[2510.04041|SITCOM]]** — An inference-time MPC-inspired framework augmenting a pre-trained VLA with ==model-based rollouts + reward-ranking==, using a transformer dynamics model (DAgger-adapted to curb drift) to simulate candidate sequences and execute the best plan's first action receding-horizon; **72%** SIMPLER vs OpenVLA-SFT's 48%, FID **17.0→11.2** after in-domain fine-tuning.
- **[[2512.23541|Act2Goal]]** — A goal-conditioned visual WM with ==Multi-Scale Temporal Hashing== (dense-proximal/sparse-distal) + reward-free online HER+LoRA improvement; RoboTwin-2.0-Hard **0.43 vs 0.06**, real plug-in **0.30→0.90** via online adaptation.
- **[[2512.03538|AdaPower]]** — A parameter-efficient specialization of a frozen ==World Foundation Model== for predictive manipulation (**<10%** trainable layers) with ==Temporal-Spatial Test-Time Training== and a ==Memory Persistence== module (object-centric cross-attention); **41.5%** on 10 unseen LIBERO tasks (**+41%** over VLA), CogACT **+41%**, π0 **+27%**.
- **[[2510.27607|DUST]]** — A ==dual-stream MMDiT== processing action and vision tokens in separate pathways with decoupled noise schedules and asynchronous sampling; **+18%** RoboCasa and **+13%** real over GR00T-N1.5, **+17%** from action-free BridgeV2 video pretraining.
- **[[2603.29315|IMPASTO]]** — A robotic oil-painting system pairing a ==differentiable forward pixel-dynamics model== (a U-Net predicting the canvas consequence of a brush action, trained purely by robot self-play) with a receding-horizon ==MPPI== planner over brushstroke parameters; **0.0285** L1 dynamics error from 900 samples, **−15.7–35.4%** LPIPS on human strokes, no demos or simulator.
- **[[2603.18336|ManiDreams]]** — A ==sample-predict-constrain== uncertainty-aware planner using a ==domain-randomized instance set== as distributional state + ==task-specific intuitive physics== forward dynamics; beats PPO under noise/delay/randomization, zero-shot to Franka with diffusion dynamics.
- **[[2606.05699|DexFuture]]** — A hierarchical ==future-state visuomotor target predictor== forecasting hand-tool-object configs that condition a high-frequency dexterous policy; **59.69%** bimanual tool-use (~90% of privileged baselines) at **60 Hz**, **250×** faster than action-conditioned WM planning.
- **[[2510.06199|DYMO-Hair]]** — A model-based hair-styling system pairing a ==3D volumetric hair-combing dynamics model== (pre-trained latent + action-conditioned state editing) with an ==MPPI planner==, trained on a PBD hair simulator; **22%** lower geometric error / **+42%** absolute SR over rule-based on 7 unseen hairstyles, zero-shot to real wigs.
- **[[2503.11999|Diffusion-Dynamics-Models-Generative]]** — A unified cloth framework (UniClothDiff) casting ==state estimation== and ==dynamics prediction== (two diffusion models for full 3D cloth state + future state) as conditional generation, plugged into ==MPC==; **>50%** lower state-estimation MSE than TRTM, order-of-magnitude lower long-horizon error, **+30–50pp** real cloth SR.
- **[[2504.16925|LDP]]** — A ==Latent Diffusion Planning== framework learning a VAE latent of observations, then diffusion models for both latent-space planning and action prediction split by an ==inverse-dynamics model==, so it trains on suboptimal and action-free demos; competitive or superior IL SR with limited expert data, transferring to real lifting.
- **[[2606.01950|OC-GS-World-Model]]** — An action-conditional ==object-centric Gaussian-splatting world model== (MRO-GWM) modeling multi-rigid-object dynamics via per-object-ID Gaussians + a ==spatio-temporal transformer==, integrated into ==iCEM-MPC==; **0.77** single-object pushing / **0.66** multi-object clear-middle in sim, robust to partial occlusion and unseen objects.
- **[[2506.13761|PWTF]]** — An MPC framework pairing a VLM ==critic== with an ==interactive digital twin== (Gaussian Splatting + mesh from a handheld scan) providing explicit physics foresight, planned via ==Cross-Entropy Method== over multi-view rendered rollouts; beats VoxPoser*/MOKA/OpenVLA/π0 across **8** real tasks with zero robot demos or fine-tuning.
- **[[2504.18719|Vysics]]** — Fuses vision (modified ==BundleSDF==) with contact-rich physics (modified ==PLL==) via bidirectional loss supervision; vision constrains physics, physics infers occluded geometry, unifying ==SDF== visible-surface and ==Deep Support Function== representations into a URDF; **−55%** Chamfer distance and **0.54 vs 0.24** volumetric IoU over vision-only BundleSDF.
- **[[2407.07889|AdaptiGraph]]** — A ==material-conditioned Graph-Based Neural Dynamics== model with online few-shot ==curiosity-driven inverse property estimation==, inferring stiffness/granularity in ~5 interactions to drive model-based planning; improves forward-dynamics accuracy and manipulation SR across rigid/rope/granular/cloth where single-material GNN baselines fail.
- **[[2407.09829|VLMPC]]** — A ==vision-language MPC== framework where a VLM samples candidate action sequences, an ==action-conditioned video-prediction model== (DMVFN-Act) forecasts each sequence's future frames, and a ==hierarchical cost== (pixel + VLM-semantic) ranks them; **78%** RoboDesk (vs VP²'s 64%), strong on multi-step push/open-drawer, handling novel objects on a real UR5.
- **[[2309.16118|D3Fields]]** — A training-free ==implicit 3D field== fusing 2D visual-foundation-model features into dense semantic + signed-distance representations from sparse multi-view RGBD, paired with 3D keypoint tracking and a learned ==GNN dynamics model== driving ==MPPI== rearrangement from 2D goal images; built in **0.166s** (vs F3RM's 88s), zero-shot to stylized/AI-generated goals.
- **[[2203.06856|ACID]]** — An end-to-end framework learning ==implicit neural geometry + action-conditional flow dynamics== for volumetric deformable objects with ==geodesics-based contrastive correspondence==, used for model-based planning; **55.6%** sim SR (+30% over the strongest baseline), transferring sim-trained dynamics to a real robot manipulating plush toys.

#### 3.2 Video Generation as Policy

Generate a full task-solving video from a pretrained video diffusion model, then extract executable actions — turning internet-scale video into a zero-shot or few-shot policy.

- **[[2605.27817|VERA]]** — A decoupled generalist-policy architecture pairing an action-free ==video generative planner== with a ==Jacobian Inverse Dynamics Model== (image-space Jacobian + optical flow) in closed-loop receding-horizon control; zero-shot language-conditioned manipulation on a real Panda + Allegro hand, **~2×** IDM data efficiency scaling with DoF.
- **[[2604.03181|MV-VDP]]** — A ==multi-view video diffusion== policy on a 5B ==Wan2.2== backbone jointly predicting future RGB + end-effector heatmaps; **89.1%** on Meta-World (5 demos) vs DP's 37.7%, **57.1%** real vs DP3's 0%; ~89% at one denoising step (5 Hz).
- **[[2507.12898|Vidar]]** — A factorized policy pairing an ==embodied video diffusion== prior + ==masked inverse dynamics model==; **68.2%** seen real tasks from ~20 min of data vs VPP's 4.5%, **66.7%** unseen; one prior, many embodiments via a unified observation space.
- **[[2507.00990|RIGVid]]** — A demo-free pipeline using ==AI-generated videos== (Kling) filtered by ==GPT-4o== for plausibility, then extracting ==6D object-pose trajectories== via FoundationPose; **85%** real over 4 tasks, matching human demos and beating ReKep's 50%; no physical demonstrations.
- **[[2509.22652|DAWN]]** — A two-stage ==Motion Director== (latent diffusion over dense pixel-motion) + ==Action Expert== (diffusion policy); CALVIN ABC→D task length **4.00** vs VPP's 3.93, **65.4%** MetaWorld, **20/20** real avocado pick-place.
- **[[2509.00361|Gen-Visual-Foresight-Pose]]** — A decoupled framework (GVF-TAPE) separating ==generative visual foresight== (rectified-flow video predicting future RGB-D, implicit depth) from a ==task-agnostic pose estimator== trained only on random-exploration data, no action labels; **83.0%** LIBERO (**+11.56%** over action-labeled IL), **56%** real rising to **86%** with human-video transfer.
- **[[2505.24156|Bimanual-Flow-Video-Prediction]]** — A ==CogRobot== framework injecting ==optical flow== as intermediate guidance into pretrained text-to-video models for bimanual policy, then extracting actions via a lightweight diffusion head; **22.663** PSNR on RDT, **100%** on "Put Apple Cabinet" sim, **0.75** real "Pull box"; flow bridges the text-to-action modality gap.
- **[[2502.09268|GEVRM]]** — A goal-expressive video model pairing a ==text-guided video-diffusion behavior planner== (generating future visual goals) with an ==Internal-Model-Control state-alignment== (prototypical contrastive learning inferring perturbations) feeding a goal-guided diffusion policy; FID **35.70**/FVD **4.16** vs AVDC/SuSIE, **1.62** perturbed task length vs SuSIE's 1.11.
- **[[2501.01895|EnerVerse]]** — A ==chunk-wise autoregressive video-diffusion== future-space model with a sparse context memory + a ==Multi-View Diffusion Generator== for 3D-consistent prediction, plus a 4D-Gaussian-Splatting data engine (EnerVerse-D); **88.5%** LIBERO with two rendered auxiliary views, **26.1** PSNR on RT-1, **100%/89%** real grasp/place.
- **[[2310.10639|Zero-Shot]]** — A subgoal-synthesis policy (SuSIE) using a fine-tuned ==image-editing diffusion model== to generate visual subgoal images from a scene + instruction, executed by a separate ==goal-conditioned low-level policy==, trained on robot trajectories + Something-Something video; SOTA CALVIN beating data-heavier baselines, zero-shot to novel objects on real robots.
- **[[2309.08587|HiP]]** — ==Compositional Foundation Models== for hierarchical planning chaining an LLM subgoal planner, a ==video-diffusion== visual planner, and an inverse-dynamics action model, with iterative refinement enforcing cross-modal consistency; outperforms goal-conditioned baselines on long-horizon tabletop tasks, **10×** less data for the inverse model via Ego4D/VC-1 pretraining.

#### 3.3 Flow & Motion-Token Bridges

Predict an intermediate, embodiment-agnostic motion representation — 2D/3D flow or latent motion tokens — that decouples *what should move* from *which robot moves it*, enabling cross-embodiment transfer.

- **[[2607.24493|KAI]]** — A ==Kinematic-Aware Articulation Interface== predicting future keypoint locations *and* displacements as one pose-plus-motion target, regularized by a ==geometric loss== with prismatic/revolute terms; its ==action-agnostic== phased-reasoning transformer permits co-training on human video; **82.9%** over six sim tasks, **+22pp** at 100 demos, **+13.4%** real in clutter.
- **[[2607.13017|FlowWAM]]** — A dual-stream diffusion transformer jointly generating RGB + ==HSV-encoded optical flow== as the unified action interface (not an auxiliary signal), pretrained action-free on EgoDex then decoded by a cross-attending ==action expert==; **92.94%** RoboTwin 2.0, **63.71** EWMScore on WorldArena (**+18.4%** trajectory accuracy), **75.7%** real vs π0.5's 61.4%.
- **[[2607.11397|WALA]]** — Pretrains a ==Semantic-Geometric Latent Action Model== on action-free video (DINOv3 + depth future-deltas), then has a VLA regress its frozen latent-action targets while a trainable ==latent world model== decoder grounds them in future dynamics; **75.2%** RoboCasa-GR1 (**+5.0pp** over DIAL), **74.2%** real from 50 demos + 400 human videos matching a 200-demo baseline.
- **[[2606.23090|Flow-as-Flow]]** — A flow bridge modeling robot velocity fields as dense ==probability velocity fields== via ==Conditional Flow Matching==, generated autoregressively over past point coordinates by a DiT trained on cross-embodiment human+robot video; ADE **21.23** Fractal / **27.11** Bridge V2 (beating baselines by 15.91/20.18), **~33×** faster (44 ms), **58%** real mobile.
- **[[2605.17522|RoboFlow4D]]** — A lightweight end-to-end ==flow world model== predicting multi-frame ==3D flows in 4D spacetime== from RGB + text via a ==3D Perceiver== (aligned to frozen VGGT) + diffusion ==FlowDiT==, driving a slow-fast closed loop; **+4.0–8.2%** LIBERO / **+11.0%** ManiSkill3 / **+12.5%** real, a flow plan in **0.68 s** vs minute-level video pipelines.
- **[[2602.03668|MVP-LAM]]** — An action-centric latent-action model trained with a ==cross-viewpoint reconstruction== objective (decode a future view from a latent action derived from a *different* synchronized view) over frozen DINOv2 + VQ-VAE; **1.96**-bit action MI (vs UniVLA's 1.42) on Bridge V2, SIMPLER **39.6→60.4%**, **94.1%** LIBERO.
- **[[2512.22688|ARFM]]** — An ==autoregressive flow matching== model over sparse point tracks via ==SAMv2==+==CLIP== feature fusion with online horizon updating; outperforms regression objectives and lifts CALVIN ABC→D task success when conditioning downstream policies.
- **[[2509.18676|3D-Flow-Diffusion-Policy]]** — A 3D policy modeling ==scene-level 3D flow== as a structured intermediate via two jointly-trained diffusion models (flow predictor + flow-conditioned action generator), with 3D ground-truth lifted from 2D CoTracker + depth; **56.9%** real avg (2× DP3's 27.5%), uniquely solving long-horizon/non-prehensile tasks where DP3 scores 0%.
- **[[2412.04445|Moto]]** — A ==VQ-VAE latent motion tokenizer== + autoregressive ==Moto-GPT== pretrained on video, co-fine-tuned with action queries; **61.4%** SIMPLER vs 48.0% without tokens, **52.5%** CALVIN at **1%** action-labeled data (baseline 0%), real **23.3%→60%**.
- **[[2506.14198|AMPLIFY]]** — A modular framework decoupling visual motion prediction from action inference, tokenizing keypoint tracks into discrete ==FSQ motion codes== a forward model predicts from action-free video, then an inverse model maps tokens to actions; **60.5%** zero-shot SR on unseen LIBERO (**27×** over BC), **1.94×** few-shot gain over ATM.
- **[[2506.06199|3DFlowAction]]** — A ==3D-flow world model== trained on the ManiFlow-110k dataset (110K human+robot videos) bypassing latent compression; **70%** vs 2D-flow's 25%, cross-embodiment Franka↔XTrainer, **+20%** from GPT-4o closed-loop verification; no action labels.
- **[[2510.08568|NovaFlow]]** — A demo-free system synthesizing task-solving videos from ==internet-scale video generation==, extracting ==actionable 3D object flow== (embodiment-agnostic) + ==VLM rejection sampling==; beats 10–30-demo imitation policies on rigid/articulated/deformable objects (Franka, Spot); goal image lifts block-insertion SR **40%→80%**.
- **[[2503.23877|ZeroMimic]]** — A demo-free pipeline distilling ==robot skills from web human videos==: affordance-predicted grasping plus an ==action-chunking transformer== trained on web-extracted human wrist trajectories, deployed zero-shot with no robot-specific training; **71.0%** real / **73.8%** sim, generalizing to unseen objects and embodiments.
- **[[2503.07135|VidBot]]** — A zero-shot system extracting ==metric-scale 3D contact points + interaction trajectories== from in-the-wild 2D human videos (SfM + depth foundation models) via coarse-to-fine ==conditional-diffusion affordance learning== with test-time cost guidance; **88.2%** sim (13 tasks) and **80.0%** real zero-shot on Stretch/Spot.
- **[[2502.20391|Point-Policy]]** — A human-to-robot bridge converting demonstration video into ==3D key points== for hands and objects, training a ==transformer to predict future robot points== mapped to end-effector poses via rigid-body constraints; **+75%** absolute over prior methods across 8 real tasks and **+74%** on novel instances, robust to distractors with no robot teleoperation.
- **[[2502.10040|DTP]]** — A ==Diffusion Trajectory Model== generating 2D end-effector particle tracks that condition a ==causal Transformer VLA==; **+33.9%** CALVIN sequence length over GR-1, real aggregate **0.84** (doubling 2nd-best on long-horizon), strong at 10% data.
- **[[2411.18369|G3Flow]]** — A pose-aware bridge building a real-time ==object-centric 3D semantic flow== by fusing multi-view observations, 3D generative digital twins, and ==DINOv2 features tracked via FoundationPose==, then conditioning a diffusion policy; **68.3%** terminal-constrained (**+22.1%** over DP3) and **50.1%** cross-object SR, occlusion-robust at **34 Hz** (~6× GenDP).
- **[[2411.09658|Motion-Before-Action]]** — A plug-and-play module (MBA) using a ==cascaded diffusion== to decouple ==object-motion inference== (first model predicts future 6D object-pose sequences) from action generation (second model conditions actions on predicted motion + observations); **+14.2%** over DP and **+6.2%** over DP3 across 57 tasks, faster convergence, gains on 6-DoF real tasks.
- **[[2411.00965|SPOT]]** — An object-centric, embodiment-agnostic bridge tracking ==SE(3) object-pose trajectories== via zero-shot 6D estimation and a ==diffusion model synthesizing future object-pose trajectories== executed by receding-horizon control; **79.4%** over 13 RLBench tasks (single camera, matching 4-camera SOTA), transferring from **8 action-less iPhone human videos** to a real robot.
- **[[2407.15208|Im2Flow2Act]]** — An ==object flow== cross-domain interface: an Im2Flow generator predicts task flow from action-less human video, a flow-conditioned diffusion policy trained only on sim play executes it; **90%** pick-place / **70%** cloth-fold real with no real-robot training, **+30%** over grid-flow ATM.

**World-Model & Video-as-Policy — Decision Matrix**

| Need | Recommendation |
|---|---|
| Frozen latent simulator for policy opt | [[2603.28955\|WAM]] (**92.8%** post-PPO) |
| Task-relevant (not pixel-perfect) futures | [[2604.19683\|MWM]] (**0.983** LIBERO, masks) |
| 3D-aware imagined planning surface | [[2605.20752\|GaussianDream]] (**98.4%**) |
| Online self-improvement, reward-free | [[2512.23541\|Act2Goal]] (real **0.30→0.90**) |
| Few-shot video-prior policy | [[2507.12898\|Vidar]] (**68.2%** from 20 min) |
| Zero-shot, no physical demos | [[2507.00990\|RIGVid]], [[2510.08568\|NovaFlow]] (**85%**) |
| Cross-embodiment motion transfer | [[2506.06199\|3DFlowAction]] (**70%**), [[2412.04445\|Moto]] |
| Decoupled vision/action denoising | [[2510.27607\|DUST]] (**+18%** RoboCasa) |
| JEPA world-model design guidance | [[2512.24497\|JEPA-WM]] |

^dm-3

> [!star] Key Papers — World-Model Manipulation Landmarks
> - [[2412.04445|Moto]] — the bridge that proved latent motion tokens learned from action-free video transfer into robot control with near-zero action labels.
> - [[2604.19683|MWM]] — established that predicting *task-relevant masks* rather than full RGB futures is both cheaper and more OOD-robust — the "predict less" insight.
> - [[2507.12898|Vidar]] — the canonical "one video prior, many embodiments" factorization separating a pretrained generator from a per-robot inverse-dynamics model.
> - [[2603.28955|WAM]] — showed an inverse-dynamics regularizer turns a Dreamer world model's latent space into an action-aware frozen simulator for cheap policy optimization.
> - [[2512.23541|Act2Goal]] — first manipulation world model to close a reward-free online self-improvement loop, lifting real OOD tasks 3× without new supervision.

^key-papers-3

> [!tip] Predict Less, Plan More — The Task-Relevant-Future Bet
> The naive world model predicts every pixel of the future; the 2026 winners predict *only what the policy needs*. [[2604.19683|MWM]] predicts semantic masks (geometry, not texture), [[2603.12553|Structured-WM-Planner]] predicts sparse kinematic keyframes (decision points, not dense rollouts), and [[2412.04445|Moto]]/[[2506.06199|3DFlowAction]] predict motion flow (what moves, not how it looks). Each "predicts less" and each generalizes *better* OOD — because the discarded detail was exactly the part that overfit. This is the same lesson the latent-world-model lineage learned: representation, not reconstruction, is the planning signal. For the JEPA-style latent-prediction theory behind this, see [[07_Latent-World-Models#1. The JEPA Principle]]; for the WAM-architecture design space these draw from, see [[06_WAM#6. Efficient & Action-Centered WAMs]].

^insight-3

### 4. Memory & Long-Horizon Non-Markovian Control

The Markov assumption — that the current observation contains everything the policy needs — is a convenient fiction that breaks the moment a task spans more than one contact. "Put the object back where it was," "count how many scoops you've done," "find the item you saw in the other room": each requires the policy to *remember* something no longer in view. This section exists because frame-stacking, the lazy answer, scales quadratically and forgets anything older than its window. Real long-horizon manipulation needs structured memory.

The two sub-sections divide on *how memory is structured*. Episodic and retrieval memory (4.1) stores a compact, queryable record of past experience — keyframes, anchors, memory banks — and retrieves the relevant slice on demand, the way human episodic memory works. Object-permanence and keyframe-history policies (4.2) take a complementary tack: maintain explicit beliefs about *objects* (including occluded ones) or distill history into a few semantically salient frames. The common enemy is *visual aliasing* — two moments that look identical but demand different actions — and the common cure is a memory representation that separates them. (The efficient history-conditioning backbones — [[2605.14598|DSSP]], [[2604.18933|Gated-Memory-Policy]] — live in §1.4 because their contribution is the *backbone*, not the memory structure.)

#### 4.1 Episodic & Retrieval Memory

Store a compact, queryable record of past experience and retrieve the task-relevant slice on demand — the episodic-memory analog for long-horizon robot control.

- **[[2607.06678|NativeMEM]]** — A ==Native Memory Compression== scheme repurposing a pretrained VLA's own vision encoder to compress each historical frame-view into one action-relevant token, via a ==two-stage== tokenizer-then-VLA finetune pipeline; **84.0%** sim / **98.7%** real long-horizon SR, **5,000**-frame histories under real-time (**<100 ms**) latency.
- **[[2606.30318|Chronos]]** — Treats the full observation history as an intrinsic ==selective state-space model (Mamba)== latent, refined by a ==physics-informed second-order Schrödinger-inspired action bridge== that predicts ==acceleration fields== via a ==quartic bell noise schedule==; RMBench **73.6%** avg SR (**+62.4pp** over π0.5), ALOHA insertion **90%** vs diffusion **66%**.
- **[[2606.29774|ACM]]** — An ==Analytic Concept-centric Memory== framework organizing experience around structured object concepts (parts, templates, affordances, transitions, skills) with ==manipulation-aware retrieval== + precondition/effect checking; **70%** RMBench SR (vs memory-augmented VLA baselines' 28-53%), **84%** real memory tasks at **98%** retrieval accuracy.
- **[[2606.25136|Long-Horizon]]** — HALO, an attention-based visuomotor policy distilling ==VLM priors== via a co-trained ==Video Question-Answering== objective to guide ==top-k sparse-attention== memory retrieval, suppressing spurious historical correlations; **41%** sim / **55%** real long-horizon SR (vs Standard Transformer's 22%/36%), cutting drift (JSD 0.07→0.06).
- **[[2606.21188|CAMP]]** — A Compressed Action Memory Policy learning a recurrent ==behavioral memory== by self-supervised reconstruction of past actions, compressing them via ==DCT low-frequency coefficients== + a Vector Quantizer, fused into a diffusion policy; **94%** Push-T-Multi-Goals (vs memoryless DP's 56%), **64.3%** on 3D Memory-Manip-Bench, 7/10 real where memoryless baselines score 0.
- **[[2606.20562|MemoryWAM]]** — A World Action Model with a hybrid ==Mixture-of-Transformers== memory: a sliding-window short-term store, persistent ==event-boundary anchor frames==, and ==gist tokens== (8/frame, 15× compression) for long-range history; **83.0%** RMBench (vs full-history LingBot-VA's 78.2%, FastWAM's 5.9%), 18/20 real Shell-Game at full-attention SR with lower latency/GPU memory.
- **[[2604.15814|Continual-Hand-Eye-Calibration]]** — A ==Spatial-Aware Replay Strategy== (hybrid-distance ==Poisson disk sampling== + density-based replacement) paired with ==Structure-Preserving Dual Distillation== decomposing localization knowledge into coarse topological + fine metric components; **98.4%** accuracy / **1.6%** forgetting rate on a robotic-manipulation dataset.
- **[[2509.01657|IWR]]** — Reframes few-shot-IL data retrieval as ==importance sampling==, replacing the L2-nearest-neighbor rule (shown equivalent to a degenerate zero-bandwidth KDE) with ==Gaussian KDE== ratios of target-vs-prior latent densities; **+4.4–5.8%** over Behavior/Flow Retrieval and SAILOR on Robomimic/LIBERO-10, **+30%** real Bridge V2 long-horizon SR.
- **[[2510.20328|MemER]]** — A hierarchical ==VLM keyframe-nomination== high-level policy + generalist low-level policy with ==single-linkage-clustering experience retrieval==; **59/60** object retrievals and 1 wrong scoop on long-horizon tasks, on par with human-provided subtasks, at ~1 Hz/~2 Hz.
- **[[2603.24576|Chameleon]]** — A human-episodic-memory-inspired policy with ==spatiotemporal anchors== + ==multi-timescale episodic states== + a ==HoloHead imagination objective== for goal-directed retrieval; **100.0%** episodic-recall DSR, **73.5%** spatial-tracking, **72.2%** sequential; pattern separation over aliased states.
- **[[2603.18494|MemoAct]]** — An ==Atkinson-Shiffrin-inspired hierarchical memory== policy pairing a lossless ==Short-Term Memory Bank== with a compressed ==Long-Term Memory Bank== via a consolidation module, decoded by conditional diffusion; **96.5%** on its MemoryRTBench vs MVMP's 72% (ACT 4.5%, DP 3%), the plug-and-play module lifting DP3 **26→76.5%**.
- **[[2501.18564|SAM2Act]]** — A multi-view ==SAM2-encoder== transformer with cascaded upsampling, extended by ==SAM2Act+=='s explicit ==memory bank + attention==; **86.8%** RLBench and **94.3%** on the non-Markovian MemoryBench, smallest **4.3%** Colosseum perturbation drop.

#### 4.2 Object-Permanence & Keyframe-History Policies

Maintain explicit beliefs about objects — including occluded ones — or distill the observation history into a few semantically salient keyframes, rather than carrying raw frames.

- **[[2608.05042|BridgeVLA++]]** — Extends [[2506.07961|BridgeVLA]]'s ==2D-heatmap pre-training== + orthographic-projection 3D fine-tuning with a unified ==spatio-temporal memory== (temporal keyframes for coarse reasoning, point-cloud memory for occlusion-robust localization); **93.7%** RLBench, **96.0%** RMBench (**+13pp** over MemoryWAM), **95.4%** real Franka at **3** demos/task.
- **[[2607.08283|TFP]]** — A memory-fusion policy augmenting a chunked VLA with an episode-local latent belief driven by ==Liquid Time-Constant networks==, modulating a ==flow-matching action decoder== via ==AdaLN== conditioning; **98.75%** LIBERO (vs π₀.₅'s 96.9%), **75.0%** on occluded ShellGameTouch, fewer real stage-memory failures on a Galaxea A1.
- **[[2606.31493|ChronoFlow-Policy]]** — A diffusion visuomotor policy unifying past-current-future gripper-object interaction via a compact ==3D keypoint representation (ChronoFlow)==, jointly learning ChronoFlow prediction + actions via a ==co-training objective== to resolve non-Markovian dependencies; **72%** MetaWorld / **66%** RoboTwin 2.0, **87%** real deformable towel-folding.
- **[[2606.23589|KEMO]]** — A lightweight plug-in memory augmenting a VLA with a temporally-ordered bank of ==event keyframes==, detected from deceleration cues + a visual-change filter (no labels) and fused via masked cross-attention + gated residual fusion; **+23.6pp** Task SR (27.8→51.4%) and **+34.1pp** Stage Completion (42.3→76.4%) over a memory-free baseline across 6 real dual-arm tasks.
- **[[2309.15278|Out-of-Sight-Still-in-Mind]]** — A ==DOOM/LOOM object-oriented memory== hallucinating point clouds or propagating latents for occluded objects, with a ==relational dynamics + CEM planner==; **0.976** relational F1, near-1.0 planning success, **19/20** real; +10–20% F1 over implicit memory.
- **[[2602.15010|BPP]]** — A ==Big Picture Policies== method conditioning on VLM-detected (Gemini-3-Pro) salient ==keyframes== at 1 Hz with ==latency masking==; **+70%** real bimanual SR over history-conditioned baselines, beating even the oracle on Variable-Password; robust to imperfect keyframes.
- **[[2503.05189|Persistent-Object-Gaussian-Splat]]** — A persistent object representation (POGS) embedding ==grouping + CLIP + DINO features into 3D Gaussians== with an online tracking loop optimizing object poses to keep identity through occlusion and handover, no CAD models; **2.92 cm** placement error, up to 12 consecutive resets, recovering from **80%** of in-grasp tool perturbations.
- **[[2503.00193|ProDapt]]** — A proprioceptive policy conditioning a diffusion model on ==contact "keypoints"== — past contact events a ==keypoint manager== selects by spatial distance and normal-force direction — fed alongside short-term proprioception as long-term memory; **80%** on an "Elbow" task where all baselines (even H_o=50) fail, completing tasks faster at real-time inference.

**Memory & Long-Horizon — Decision Matrix**

| Need | Recommendation |
|---|---|
| Retrieve from minutes-long visual memory | [[2510.20328\|MemER]] (**59/60** retrievals) |
| Disambiguate visually-aliased states | [[2603.24576\|Chameleon]] (**100%** recall DSR) |
| Explicit memory-bank policy + benchmark | [[2501.18564\|SAM2Act]] (**94.3%** MemoryBench) |
| Reason about occluded / out-of-view objects | [[2309.15278\|Out-of-Sight-Still-in-Mind]] (**19/20** real) |
| Cheap semantic keyframe history | [[2602.15010\|BPP]] (**+70%** bimanual) |
| Sub-quadratic full-history backbone | [[2605.14598\|DSSP]], [[2604.18933\|Gated-Memory-Policy]] (§1.4) |

^dm-4

> [!star] Key Papers — Memory Architecture Landmarks
> - [[2501.18564|SAM2Act]] — paired an explicit memory-bank policy with MemoryBench, giving the field both an architecture and the non-Markovian benchmark to measure it.
> - [[2510.20328|MemER]] — showed VLM-nominated keyframes plus clustering retrieval scale robot memory to minutes, matching human-provided subtask decomposition.
> - [[2603.24576|Chameleon]] — imported the episodic-memory cognitive architecture (pattern separation/completion) into closed-loop manipulation.
> - [[2309.15278|Out-of-Sight-Still-in-Mind]] — the object-permanence landmark: explicit per-object memory beats implicit recurrence when objects leave the frame.
> - [[2602.15010|BPP]] — demonstrated that semantic keyframe abstraction can be *more* robust than ground-truth state, inverting the usual oracle hierarchy.

^key-papers-4

> [!tip] Visual Aliasing Is the Real Enemy — Structure Beats Window-Length
> Every failure in this section traces to one root: two moments that look the same but require different actions. Naive frame-stacking can't separate them no matter how long the window. The winners impose *structure* — episodic anchors ([[2603.24576|Chameleon]]), per-object beliefs ([[2309.15278|Out-of-Sight-Still-in-Mind]]), retrieved keyframes ([[2510.20328|MemER]]), or VLM-salient frames ([[2602.15010|BPP]]) — so aliased states cluster apart and decision-relevant history survives. The strategic read: don't ask "how long a history?" — ask "what abstraction separates my aliased states?" For the backbone side of cheap history conditioning, see [[10_Manipulation-Skill-Learning#1. Generative Policy Architectures]]; for how self-evolving agents accumulate memory across episodes, see [[16_Self-Evolving-VLA-WAM#7. Self-Evolving Embodied Agents]]. This §4.1/§4.2 split is the manipulation-side half of the full cross-domain memory picture — see [[09_Robot-Memory#1.3 Episodic & Compression Memory for Manipulation]] for §4.1 and [[09_Robot-Memory#2. Object-Permanence & Keyframe-History Policies]] (both its 2.1 and 2.2 sub-axes) for §4.2.

^insight-4
