### 5. LLM / Neuro-Symbolic Planning & Affordance Grounding

A diffusion backbone can grasp a mug, but it can't decide *which* mug, in what order, under a free-form instruction it has never seen. That decision layer is what this section covers — and the field's dominant answer has been to borrow the reasoning of a frozen foundation model rather than train it from robot data. An LLM decomposes the goal; a VLM grounds the language to pixels and 3D; the low-level policy executes. The category exists because semantic generalization is *expensive to learn from demonstrations* but *cheap to borrow* from a model already trained on internet-scale text and images.

The sub-sections track where the foundation model plugs in. Task decomposers and planners (5.1) use the LLM as the brain, often with neuro-symbolic guarantees the raw LLM lacks; reward and cost designers (5.2) have it write the objective instead of the plan; code-generation planners (5.3) emit the plan as an executable, verifiable program. Affordance and value-map grounding (5.4) uses VLMs to answer *where and how to act* — turning "open the drawer" into a 3D value map, a keypoint constraint, or a contact-motion curve. Interactive perception and scene graphs (5.5) close the loop the other way: act to *reduce uncertainty*, building an explicit world representation by poking the environment. The recurring tension: foundation-model reasoning is broad but ungrounded, so every system here is really a *grounding harness* that catches the LLM's hallucinations before they reach the motors.

#### 5.1 LLM & VLM Task Decomposers & Planners

Use a foundation model as the high-level brain — decomposing goals into sub-tasks, skill sequences, or symbolic plan structures (PDDL, behavior trees, finite state machines) — with symbolic scaffolding that catches the model's errors before execution.

- **[[2201.07207|LLM-Zero-Shot-Planners]]** — A method extracting executable plans from frozen LLMs via ==semantic translation== + ==autoregressive correction==; lifts VirtualHome executability from **7.79%→73.05%** (GPT-3) while keeping correctness competitive with human plans; the zero-shot-planning origin.
- **[[2204.00598|Socratic-Models]]** — A framework composing LMs/VLMs/ALMs with ==language as the universal interface== and closed-loop dialogue, zero-shot; SOTA zero-shot CIDEr **44.5** captioning, enabling language-conditioned robot perception and planning by orchestration.
- **[[2311.10678|DROC]]** — An LLM-based framework classifying arbitrary high/low-level language corrections via a ==Correction Handler==, with a ==Knowledge Distillation== module and a ==Knowledge Retrieval== module indexing past interactions by semantic + visual similarity; needed **under half** the online corrections of Code as Policies.
- **[[2608.08884|SHRIMP]]** — An LLM emits a ==hierarchical primitive plan== grounded by ==YOLO== object tracking, verified for grasp-target/collision feasibility, and rehearsed in an ==Isaac Sim== digital twin before bimanual-Franka execution, with user re-prompting and parameter editing; **35**-participant study: perceived autonomy **3.50** vs **3.15** (p=**0.007**).
- **[[2604.02812|Neuro-Symbolic-Robot-Policies]]** — A compact VLM synthesizing executable ==Behavior Trees== from ==fully synthetic Gemini-generated== instruction-BT pairs with reactive-guarding priors; Pixtral-12B hits **100%** task/validity/schema, sim-to-real to Franka and UR5e with no real fine-tune.
- **[[2604.26569|LLM-Flax]]** — An LLM that auto-generates ==PDDL relaxation/complementary rules== + zero-shot object scoring, replacing GNN training; **0.945** average over MazeNamo (+0.117 vs manual), **0.733** on Expert tasks where the manual planner scored 0.
- **[[2605.01448|Decompose-Recompose]]** — A training-free framework reasoning new skills by converting demos into interpretable ==atomic skill–action pairs== (VLM-annotated), retrieving via a ==dual dynamic+coverage-aware library==, then ==Skill-Augmented In-Context Learning== prompting an LLM to recompose them; **26.4%** on 23 AGNOSTOS unseen tasks (vs π0's 17.5%), **36%** real xArm6.
- **[[2602.21198|Reflective-Test-Time-Planning]]** — A system of three interacting MLLMs unifying ==reflection-in-action== (candidate scoring) and ==reflection-on-action== (hindsight LoRA updates); **33.65%** on Long-Horizon Household vs 3DLLM-Mem's 11.13%, transfers to photorealistic HM3D.
- **[[2603.30022|Hybrid-LLM-RL-Manipulation]]** — An ==LLM task planner== + ==RL skill executor== with a continuous feedback loop; **−33.5%** completion time, **+18.1%** accuracy (78.4→92.6%), and **+36.4%** dynamic-environment adaptability over RL-only.
- **[[2603.09542|NS-VLA]]** — A neuro-symbolic VLA grounding language+vision into ==structured primitives (neuro-symbols)==, then a ==symbolic solver== (token sparsification + causal Transformer) emitting chunked actions refined online with ==GRPO==; **69.1%** LIBERO / **79.4%** LIBERO-Plus at 1-shot, **91.2%** CALVIN ABC→D.
- **[[2606.24338|RoBoSR]]** — A representation-centric embodied-reasoning framework using ==structured 3D scene graphs== as the primary state space to separate reasoning from execution, trained ==SFT then RFT== on the new Manip-Cognition-1.6M; superior GSR-bench task progress over LLM baselines with **+11.7%** absolute on spatial tasks from RFT, plus real-world state-consistency self-correction.
- **[[2606.19340|ZeroDex]]** — A zero-shot long-horizon dexterous framework decoupling ==VLM semantic reasoning== from execution via ==multi-view 3D fusion== (RANSAC triangulation + ray voting), a 'Bag of Atomic Actions' library, and closed-loop replanning; 10/10 Throw-Trash and 8/10 Broom-Clean (vs fine-tuned GR00T/Being-HO's 0/5), **4.58 cm** grasp error vs single-view's 16.43 cm.
- **[[2606.15654|PO-PDDL]]** — A symbolic POMDP formulation extending PDDL plus a ==two-stage pipeline== learning predicates, ==stochastic operators==, and observation functions directly from visual robot demonstrations, finding partial observability via symbolic-vs-visual contradictions; **100%** task success and **50.4–85.7%** less planning time on 5 long-horizon real tasks from 29 demos.
- **[[2603.25903|ENAP]]** — An **Emergent Neural Automaton Policy** unsupervised-discovering a ==Probabilistic Mealy Machine== from visuomotor demos via HDBSCAN clustering + RNN history embedding, extracted via an extended ==L* algorithm==, used as a coarse action prior refined by a residual net; **98.8%** DualStackCube at **2.66M** params (vs Oracle's 2.98M), robust to **25%** of training data.
- **[[2510.07882|Proprio-MLLM]]** — A proprioception-aware MLLM (Qwen2.5-VL) adding ==motion-based position embedding== + a ==cross-spatial encoder== for dual-arm humanoid planning, paired with the DualTHOR simulator; **+19.75%** planning SR over MLLM baselines like DAG-Plan on long-horizon tasks, with improved re-planning under stochastic contingencies.
- **[[2508.21501|Neuro-Symbolic IL]]** — Jointly learns a symbolic ==PDDL== domain (graph ==bisimulation== + ==ASP==) and per-skill ==diffusion==-policy controllers from raw demos, an 'Oracle' restricting observations to task-relevant EE-relative objects; solves Hanoi/Nut-Assembly/Kitchen from **5** demos vs failing neural baselines, zero-shot **7-cube** generalization from 3-cube demos.
- **[[2508.15021|ICPI]]** — Frames dynamic-manipulation policy adjustment as sequence completion, using an LLM's ==in-context learning== as a ==policy improvement operator== over tokenized parameters + ==KNN==-retrieved relative-error examples, no weight updates; real roll-to-goal mean cost **17.1** vs Linear-KNN's **33.8** at ~300 examples, beating in-weights LLM reasoning.
- **[[2506.19212|VLM-Dexterous-Scaffolding]]** — An off-the-shelf VLM emitting coarse ==3D keypoint + wrist-pose scaffolds== that a ==low-level RL policy== tracks, removing demos and reward engineering; **81%** over 8 sim dexterous tasks vs 19% pre-recorded, **90%** zero-shot real Allegro-hand place-bottle.
- **[[2505.08444|Vis2Plan]]** — A neuro-symbolic planner extracting ==symbolic states + a transition graph== from unlabeled play data via VFM temporal-similarity segmentation, then ==A* search== plus a ==reachability estimator + beam search== ground symbolic subgoals into physically-feasible visual subgoal sequences; **0.71** avg real SR (**+53%**), **35x** faster than video-diffusion planners.
- **[[2502.16707|ReflectVLM]]** — A ==diffusion visual-dynamics model== that lets a VLM imagine outcomes and ==reflect-then-refine== plans for multi-stage long-horizon manipulation; outperforms commercial VLMs and classical planners and generalizes to novel configs while keeping test-time compute bounded.
- **[[2410.01345|GemBench]]** — A modular ==3D-LOTUS++== policy combining a ==LLaMa3-8B task planner== + ==OWLv2+SAM 3D object grounding== feeding a language-conditioned ==Point Cloud Transformer== controller, evaluated on the 4-level GemBench generalization suite; **64.5%** novel rigid / **41.5%** articulated / **17.4%** long-horizon, **7.9/10** real vs 3D-LOTUS's 0/10.
- **[[2505.00527|DeCo]]** — A plug-in for multi-task IL decomposing demos into ==contact-event atomic tasks==, having a VLM schedule atomic skills from an instruction, then chaining them via a ==spatially-aware skill-chaining== module for collision-free transitions; up to **66.67%** on 12 novel long-horizon sim tasks (baselines 0–0.14%), **53.33%** real RVT-2+DeCo vs RVT-2's 0%.
- **[[2503.17309|LLM+MAP]]** — A bimanual task planner pairing an ==LLM== with ==PDDL multi-agent planning==, using spatial scene understanding to split coordinated vs uncoordinated work areas and iterative prompting to generate valid PDDL instances solved by FMAP/BFWS; highest SR across domains with reduced planning time and better Group-Debits task allocation on a NICOL bimanual robot.
- **[[2503.06814|Unlocking-Generalization-for]]** — A modular framework unifying ==parameterized action primitives== (RAPS), planner-distilled visuomotor policies (PSL), and a VLM-planning + neural-motion-planning + local-policy stack (ManipGen) for zero-shot text-conditioned manipulation; PSL **>85%** long-horizon SR, ManipGen **76%** across 50 real zero-shot tasks up to 8 stages.
- **[[2503.02748|Bridging-VLM-and-KMP]]** — A framework (VL-MP) bridging VLM decisions to movement primitives via a ==3D semantic-keypoint Bridge Layer== + a ==Local-Feature-Enhanced KMP== resampling start/end points for shape-preserving generalization; **90%** normal / **80%** ambiguous pouring vs VLM+Prim's 20% and DP's 0%, **+42.82%** topological similarity over KMP.
- **[[2501.18733|Integrating-LMM-Planners-3D]]** — A framework (LMM-3DP) coupling a GPT-4V high-level planner with a ==3D semantic-geometric skill policy== (DINO + PointNext + CLIP keyframe 6-DOF), closed by a critic agent + in-context memory; **90%** skills without distractors, **70–90%** untrained object-location composition, **56.5%** long-horizon vs SayCan/VoxPoser's 7–10%.
- **[[2503.05114|Look-Before-You-Leap]]** — A state-aware IL framework integrating ==LLM-generated Finite State Machines== (a State-Machine Serialization Language for states/operations/transitions) with environment-conditioned policy training for state-dependent long-horizon tasks; **98.0%** Towers-of-Hanoi/Chess and **96.2%** River-Crossing at 1000 demos vs a control's 5.5–58.0%.
- **[[2501.03841|OmniManip]]** — A zero-shot framework grounding tasks in ==object-centric interaction primitives== defined in canonical space by object functionality, run by a ==dual closed-loop== (planning via primitive resampling + VLM checking, execution via 6D pose tracking) with no VLM fine-tuning; **+15%** from closed-loop planning, viewpoint-robust, auto-generating BC data.
- **[[2304.11477|LLM+P]]** — A planner where the LLM translates a problem into a ==PDDL problem file== (given a domain file + ICL example), solves it with an off-the-shelf ==FAST-DOWNWARD== classical planner, then translates the plan back to language; optimal plans across 7 robot-planning domains where direct LLM planning (incl. Tree-of-Thoughts) fails — the LLM-plus-classical-planner landmark.
- **[[2503.07317|InversePrompt]]** — A self-correcting planner having the LLM generate each step's ==inverse action==, apply it to the resulting state, and compare against the original, using any discrepancy as explicit re-planning feedback; **89%** avg SR with Gemini-1.5-Flash (up to **+30%** over no-validator), validated on real Blocksworld/Cooking where vague-feedback self-correction fails.
- **[[2506.11261|Gondola]]** — A grounded VLM planner (LoRA-tuned InternVL-4B + SAM2) emitting ==fine-grained multi-view segmentation masks== over bounding boxes, with history-plan text context for long-horizon reasoning; **74.8%** L2 / **52.4%** L3 GemBench (**+10.3%/+10.9%** over 3D-LOTUS++), **87.9%** grounding IoU vs box's 62.8%.
- **[[2505.01709|RoBridge]]** — A hierarchical architecture bridging cognition and execution via a ==VLM Cognitive Planner== emitting an ==Invariant Operable Representation== (masked-depth symbolic bridge) that a guided embodied agent translates to actions; **82.12%** MetaWorld (**+11.28%**), **100%** Robosuite contact-rich, **75%** zero-shot on novel tasks with 5 real samples each.
- **[[2503.13055|CoT-SC-Affordance-Planner]]** — An MLLM task planner pairing ==Chain-of-Thought + Self-Consistency== majority voting against cross-modal distraction with an ==affordance-guided replanning== feasibility check, plus the QuARC benchmark; **76.7%** overall (2× ViLa's 36.7%), lifting reachability 40→80% and collision-avoidance 30→76.7%.
- **[[2503.05064|Perceiving-Reasoning-Adapting-Dual-Layer]]** — A ==dual-layer framework== for VLM-guided precision manipulation pairing a 2D topology graph with a ==3D spatial network of minimal-volume Gaussian envelopes==, plus a progressive VLM planner switching coarse-motion/fine-manipulation; **91.01%** truss assembly / **81.21%** connector docking vs pure VLM's 0%.
- **[[2503.04557|Language-Conditioned-Cloth]]** — A long-demo cloth framework using ==GPT-4o CoT== to decompose multi-step demonstrations into language-conditioned pick/place skills, training a ==Transformer point-predictor== then re-planning unseen tasks; **100%** demo decomposition, **98–100%** sim on unseen multi-step tasks, **100%** real Franka folding.
- **[[2503.01616|RoboDexVLM]]** — A closed-loop dexterous framework pairing ==GPT-4o task planning== with Grounding-DINO/SAM segmentation, AnyGrasp 7-DoF perception, ==kinematic retargeting== to a five-fingered hand, and two-layer recovery; **93.33–100%** language-grounded segmentation vs YOLOv11's 46–53%, **+76.67%** recovery on object-position changes, **>85%** overall.
- **[[2502.05485|HAMSTER]]** — A hierarchical action model where a ==VLM predicts coarse 2D paths== (fine-tuned on cheap off-domain action-free video + sim) and a ==3D-aware low-level policy== executes them from a small real dataset; **+20%** absolute SR over OpenVLA across 7 generalization axes, 2× the SR of standard 3D-DA at 50% less data.
- **[[2502.10090|Manual2Skill]]** — A VLM-guided (==GPT-4o==) pipeline reading IKEA-style visual manuals into a ==hierarchical assembly graph==, a multi-modal ==Graph Neural Network== predicting per-step 6D poses, then ==RRT-Connect== motion planning; **32.4%** graph-generation SR across furniture complexities, **58%** full-assembly SR in sim, **60–85%** real completion on xArm6.
- **[[2501.15068|Atomic-Skill-Library]]** — A ==three-wheeled self-driven== pipeline interleaving a VLP planning agent (Prismatic/Dino-X/SAM-2/GPT-4) with a VLA skill wheel that dynamically defines and few-shot-expands an ==atomic-skill library==; **+20–40%** OOD-object SR over end-to-end baselines and **80–100%** on reordered novel sequences where end-to-end scores 0%.
- **[[2402.11871|LAMP]]** — Learns ==PDDL-based world models== from raw unannotated demos: ==Relational Critical Regions== (GMM-fit interaction zones in relative object-pose space) invent a symbolic vocabulary, and abstract-state transitions synthesize generalizable actions; up to **18x** zero-shot generalization factor, **100%** unseen-task SR matching STAMP, from **40** demos.

#### 5.2 LLM-as-Reward & Cost Designers

Here the foundation model writes the *objective* rather than the plan: executable reward code, cost maps, or constraint-derived cost functions that a downstream RL trainer or trajectory optimizer then solves. This is the Eureka / ARCHIE / CoRAL lineage.

- **[[2605.02600|CoRAL]]** — An approach using LLMs as ==MPPI cost designers== and online diagnosticians decoupled from grounded control; **+50%** average SR over OpenVLA/π0.5 on unseen contact-rich tasks, zero-shot sim-to-real with force regulation on Franka.
- **[[2511.17855|QuickLAP]]** — A ==Bayesian== framework jointly interpreting physical corrections and language via a ==dual-LLM== (Attention LLM + Preference LLM), with an attention-modulated conditional prior enabling a closed-form ==MAP update==; **>2×** lower reward-weight NMSE than physical-only feedback, **0.46** vs Language-Only's 0.57.
- **[[2503.10110|IMPACT]]** — A motion planner using a ==VLM (GPT-4o)== to assign zero-shot ==contact-tolerance costs== to non-target objects, converted into an anisotropic directional cost map a ==contact-aware A* planner== uses for acceptable contact in clutter; **73.75%** sim (vs A*'s 15.75%) and **61%** real, with human-preferred trajectories.
- **[[2503.04280|Autonomous-Reinforcement-Real-World-Robotic]]** — A one-shot pipeline (ARCHIE) where GPT-4 generates Python ==reward functions + success/failure conditions== from language, enforcing terminal-over-shaping dominance, then trains via modified SAC; solves all 10 single/bi-manual tasks on a real ABB YuMi over human-engineered and direct-GPT baselines, with sim-to-real transfer.
- **[[2501.09783|GeoManip]]** — A largely training-free framework translating language into ==symbolic geometric constraints== via foundation models (GPT-4o + SAM), with a ==select-process Geometry Parser== emitting Python that becomes cost functions for an optimization-based trajectory solver; **71.1%** Meta-World / **60.0%** OmniGibson and **65.0%** real vs OpenVLA's 15.0%, no training.
- **[[2310.12931|Eureka]]** — An ==LLM reward-design== algorithm where a coding LLM (GPT-4) writes executable Python reward code from environment source, refined by ==evolutionary search== + ==reward reflection== textual feedback over GPU-accelerated RL; outperforms human-engineered rewards on **83%** of 29 tasks (**+52%** avg), enabling dexterous pen-spinning.

#### 5.3 Code-Generation Planners

The plan itself is a *program*: executable Python policies, TAMP solvers, or parameterized skill code whose structure makes the LLM's reasoning verifiable, debuggable, and reusable in a way free-form plans are not.

- **[[2603.04560|MEMO]]** — A ==retrieval-augmented skillbook== that paraphrases human corrections and clusters successful code into parameterized templates; **88%** real SR at **1.52** feedbacks/task vs π0.5's 12%; offline clustering is critical to generalization.
- **[[2606.03047|ModuLoop]]** — A ==Modular Code Synthesizer== + ==Closed-Loop Debugger== with simulation validation; **96.67%** code-gen and **86.67%** calibration success, **100%** collision-free coordinate generation, beating ProgPrompt and Code-as-Policies.
- **[[2505.03725|MOPS]]** — Frames language-conditioned TAMP as three-level ==meta-optimization==: an LLM does ==program search== selecting discrete NLP constraints + initial guesses, a ==black-box optimizer (CMA-ES)== refines continuous parameters, and a ==gradient-based trajectory optimizer== solves the program with LLM feedback; beats Code-as-Policies/PRoC3S on precision pushing/drawing.
- **[[2504.21769|LLM-based]]** — An interactive imitation-learning framework (LLM-iTeach) where an LLM generates a task policy as ==hierarchical-prompted Python code==, then provides ==similarity-based corrective-or-evaluative feedback== comparing agent actions to the generated policy; matches or beats human-supervised CEILing across RLBench tasks without extra training data.
- **[[2504.13351|Chain-of-Modality]]** — A VLM program-synthesis recipe learning cross-embodiment manipulation programs from ==multimodal human videos== (RGB + EMG + audio) via ==Chain-of-Modality prompting== that reasons over each modality sequentially; force/contact cues from EMG+audio beat vision-only task-plan extraction, deploying cross-embodiment via abstracted API calls.
- **[[2503.01700|Code-as-Symbolic-Planner]]** — A multi-agent framework having ==LLMs generate executable Python== as the TAMP planner/solver/checker, with three agents (Task/Steer/Check-LLM) + a rule-based ==Symbolic Complexity Checker== iterating to refine and verify the code; **52.3%** avg SR with GPT-4o (**+24.1%**), 100% on real BoxNet, degrading slowly as complexity grows.

#### 5.4 Affordance & Value-Map Grounding

Use VLMs to answer *where and how to act* — converting free-form language into 3D value maps, keypoint constraints, contact-motion curves, or attention masks the low-level policy can execute.

- **[[2608.09778|RoboSeg]]** — Builds an online part-level semantic 3D map from a single eye-in-hand RGB-D stream: ==Qwen3-VL== proposes 1–3 functional part prompts constraining ==SAM3==, ==TSDF fusion== runs asynchronously while per-voxel ==temporal-consensus voting== stabilizes masks, indexing ==AnyGrasp== candidates by part; **83.4%** mean part IoU, **21/24** combined real task successes.
- **[[2608.01603|AffordTrajDP]]** — Propagates a retrieved static anchor contact point through a ==DDIM trajectory-diffusion== model's predicted future object SE(3) pose, then conditions a ==1D conditional U-Net diffusion policy== on the resulting dynamic affordance trajectory; **70.0%** ManiSkill3 (vs DP3's 52.2%, AffordDP's 57.8%, SPOT's 62.1%), **66.1%**/**72.5%** real on Galaxea A1/UR7e.
- **[[2608.01083|Sparse-Dense Correspondence]]** — Combines structure/task/interaction-aware ==sparse keypoints== with ==dense correspondences== for rigid-deformable tracking, formulating execution as ==closed-loop constraint optimization==; **73.5%** sim / **65.0%** real across four rigid-deformable scenarios, dropping **71.4%→20.1%** on a key task without the sparse keypoints.
- **[[2608.00502|SpatialAfford]]** — A two-stage coarse-to-fine VLM affordance grounder: ==Spatial Attention Alignment (SAA)== aligns cross-modal attention to the affordance mask, then Spatial-Aware ==GRPO== refines coordinates; **86.57%** P@50 / **76.37%** gIoU on PartAfford at 4B params, beating 7B+ baselines, **37.44%** P@50 zero-shot on AGD20K.
- **[[2607.28293|RGB-D Affordance HW-NAS]]** — A reformulated ==Hardware-Aware Neural Architecture Search== that evolves multi-branch RGB-D Grasp/Don't-Grasp segmentation DNNs (plus a lightweight RGB-network fine-tuning alternative) for embedded wearable-robot deployment; **+2.6–3.6%** accuracy from depth (up to **+31.6%** on specific objects), **14–16 FPS** on a Jetson Nano under **11 W**.
- **[[2607.07897|StiffNET]]** — A unified monocular framework fusing open-vocabulary detection/segmentation/tracking/depth models with a language-based ==stiffness estimation network== that selects deformable- vs rigid-grasp mode for gripper-width control; grasps lettuce, mozzarella, and rigid bottles at **30 FPS** with a standard parallel gripper, no tactile sensing.
- **[[2606.31909|CoDex]]** — A zero-demonstration dexterous framework translating ==VLM== task descriptions into geometric/spatial constraints, synthesizing grasp candidates via ==analytic constrained optimization== then refining grasp-move-actuate sequences with ==RL==; **73%** real success across six unseen compositional tasks, **40-73pp** over analytical and VLM-guided-RL baselines.
- **[[2606.30632|GROW²]]** — A framework decomposing ==open-world affordance grounding== for tool use into VLM-driven ==semantic tool/part selection== + ==SAM3==/==SAM3D== geometric 3D localization, fusing multi-view 2D segmentation onto a reconstructed mesh for occlusion-robust regions; **86.7%** sim / **78.0%** real SR vs MOKA's 33.0%/30.0%.
- **[[2506.16652|CodeDiffuser]]** — A modular framework where a VLM (==ChatGPT-4o==) generates Python calling perception APIs (==DINOv2==/==SAM==) to fuse multi-view observations into a ==3D attention map==, conditioning a ==Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Model== policy; **86.5%** sim / **80%** real SR on ambiguous instructions vs Lang-DP's **5.5%**, staying **>90%** as ambiguity increases.
- **[[2307.05973|VoxPoser]]** — An LLM that writes ==Python composing 3D value maps== (affordance/avoidance/velocity) executed by an ==MPC planner==; **88%** static and **70%** disturbed real SR vs primitives' 24%/0%, and **80–91.7%** contact-rich with <3 min interaction; the value-map landmark.
- **[[2409.01652|ReKep]]** — A ==Relational Keypoint Constraints== formulation as GPT-4o-written Python cost functions over DINOv2/SAM keypoints, solved hierarchically at 10 Hz; **44.3%** over 7 real tasks vs VoxPoser's 10.0%, generalizing to 8 garment categories.
- **[[2606.02551|AFUN]]** — An ==affordance foundation model== jointly predicting ==functional segmentation masks== + ==3D Bézier post-contact motion curves== from 10 unified datasets; **+23.9** gIoU over baselines, **81.0%** contact-in-mask, **90%** real on Franka with no robot-specific fine-tune.
- **[[2606.27036|RelAfford6D]]** — A constraint-driven framework introducing the ==Relational 6D Affordance Graph==, modeling SE(3) relations + kinematic constraints between object parts (LLM topology + grounding + 6D pose), executed as ==SE(3) constraint satisfaction== with replanning; **74%** zero-shot over 10 articulated categories (beating data-driven baselines), **100%** on rigid tasks.
- **[[2606.04172|Affordance2Action]]** — A2A pairs an agent-assisted ==A2A-AffordGen== annotation pipeline (VLM + iterative SAM3 refinement) with a real-time ==A2A-GroundingModel== (staged ORPS-to-TRPS adaptation + text-conditioned visual prompts) whose masks explicitly highlight a ==Diffusion Policy=='s RGB input; **94.4%** LIBERO-object SR vs DP-RGB's 87.8%, 26/40 real trials at **9.7 FPS**.
- **[[2604.11320|CLASP]]** — An open-vocabulary desktop grasping system using ==dual-pathway semantic/geometric perception==, VLM structured grasp reasoning, and an ==asynchronous closed-loop evaluator==; **87.0%** cluttered-sim pick vs Qwen3-VL's 72.27%, **−39.8%** execution time and **−59.5%** time variance.
- **[[2506.18448|GraspMAS]]** — A ==multi-agent system== (GPT-4 Planner / Coder / GPT-4o Observer) over 9 foundation-model tools for language-driven grasping; **0.62** OCID-VLG and **0.68** GraspAnything++ zero-shot, **0.80** real single / **0.76** cluttered.
- **[[2512.13660|RoboTracer]]** — A ==spatial-trace VLM== with a metric-scale decoder, SFT then ==RFT with metric-sensitive process rewards== on the 4.5M TraceSpatial dataset; **+36%** over Gemini-2.5-Pro on real spatial tracing, collision-free traces for UR5/G1 at 1.5 Hz.
- **[[2509.06233|O3Afford]]** — One-shot 3D object-to-object affordance grounding fusing ==DINOv2==-distilled semantic point clouds with a ==joint-attention transformer decoder== predicting per-point affordance from one example per category, then LLM-generated Python constraints drive 6-DoF optimization; **26.19** aIOU vs O2O-Afford's **14.31**, real Franka **~80%**/**50%** common/complex.
- **[[2509.00310|TReF-6]]** — Infers a latent influence point + ==6-DoF task-relevant local frame== from one demonstration by optimizing a ==directional consistency score== over trajectory acceleration, semantically grounded via ==GPT-4o== + ==Grounded-SAM==, then reparameterizes DMPs within it; real cabinet-door-opening **66.7%** vs baseline **8.3%**, peg-in-hole **53.3%** vs **20.0%**.
- **[[2508.02093|StackItUp]]** — Translates a rough 2D sketch into an ==abstract relation graph== of geometric relations + local stability patterns, grounded into precise 3D poses by ==compositional diffusion models== and repaired by a ==physics-engine-guided== search inferring hidden supports; **79.9–84.4%** resemblance, **94.5–98.8%** stability vs an end-to-end baseline's **16.2%**/**12.1%**.
- **[[2504.12636|A0]]** — A hierarchical ==affordance-aware diffusion model== predicting object-centric ==2D contact points + post-contact trajectories== then translating them to actions over ==embodiment-agnostic 2D waypoints==, pretrained on 1M contact points; **62.50%** Franka / **53.75%** Kinova (**+18.75pp** over Molmo, **+33pp** over RDT-1B/π0), 4–5 execution steps vs 25–50.
- **[[2411.02704|RT-Affordance]]** — A hierarchical policy conditioning on an ==affordance plan== (a sequence of end-effector poses at critical task stages) inferred from an initial image + instruction, trained cheaply on web data + post-hoc affordance-annotated images; **68%** grasping unseen objects vs RT-2's 28%, **70%** on complex tasks vs RT-2's 3%, **77%** under novel camera views.
- **[[2410.12124|OAF]]** — **Oriented Affordance Frames**, a task-aligned 3D coordinate system anchored to the tool at task initiation, define a compact object-centric state for per-subtask ==Diffusion Policy== with ==self-progress prediction== for autonomous arbitration; **90.9%** SR from **10** demos/subtask (**30x** more data-efficient), **83.1%** OOD spatial generalization.
- **[[2407.04689|RAM (Retrieval Affordance Transfer)]]** — A retrieval-based framework building a unified ==affordance memory== of 2D contact points/trajectories from robot, human, and custom sources, using ==hierarchical retrieval== + ==VFM dense features== to transfer and 3D-lift affordances; **52.62%** sim / **66.7%** real zero-shot SR, **+35.0pp** from policy distillation.
- **[[2505.11865|GLOVER++]]** — An affordance-learning framework distilling ==human-behavior affordances== into actionable grasp regions via ==global-to-local decoding== over the new HOVA-500K dataset with ==KLD-based distribution alignment==; SOTA on HOVA-500K with zero-shot novel-object manipulation, enabling multi-task imitation and bimanual downstream control.
- **[[2405.19783|IVM]]** — An ==Instruction-guided Visual Masking== method suppressing instruction-irrelevant regions, trained on IVM-Mix-1M via ==discriminator-weighted learning==; **+26.2%** GPT-4V on V*Bench (55.0→81.2%), and hardens language-conditioned BC under visual distraction.
- **[[2503.10546|KUDA]]** — An open-vocabulary framework using ==keypoints to unify visual prompting and dynamics learning==: a VLM emits keypoint-target code interpreted by a ==neural dynamics + MPPI== two-level closed loop with periodic VLM re-querying; **48/60** trials across rigid/rope/granular tasks, outperforming MOKA and VoxPoser on conceptual commands.
- **[[2503.03556|Afford-X]]** — A slim end-to-end affordance-reasoning model with ==Verb-Attention + Bi-Fusion== modules and ==Noun-Pronoun Distillation== for category-agnostic inference, trained on GPT-4-built COCO-Aff/LVIS-Aff; **45.3%** mAP_box on COCO-Tasks at **2.38 FPS** (~50× faster than GPT-4V), **80.44%** mean task-oriented grasp SR.
- **[[2502.04531|AnyPlace]]** — A two-stage placement framework: a ==VLM== proposes coarse locations from language, then a ==transformer-based diffusion model== refines fine-grained relative poses within the cropped region, trained entirely on procedurally-generated synthetic data; **80%** real zero-shot vial insertion, **92.74%** sim, generalizing to rigid and deformable objects.
- **[[2403.03174|MOKA]]** — A training-free ==mark-based visual prompting== method converting continuous 2D affordance prediction into VQA for a hierarchical VLM (GPT-4V), unifying manipulation skills via ==point-based affordances==; zero-shot matches Code-as-Policies/VoxPoser, in-context learning reaching **0.9–1.0** SR, and 50 distilled trajectories fine-tune Octo to match it.
- **[[2309.02561|Physically-Grounded-VLM]]** — A VLM fine-tuned on the **225K**-box ==PhysObjects dataset== (10 physical concepts: material/fragility/contents) so an LLM planner can query object physics; **87.5%** concept accuracy (**+21.3%** over InstructBLIP), lifting planning accuracy to **88.2%** vs 56.9% and 9/10 real Franka tasks vs 4/10.

#### 5.5 Interactive Perception, Scene Graphs & Human Collaboration

Close the loop the other way: act to *reduce uncertainty* — poking the environment to build an explicit world representation, or estimating a human partner's intent to act *with* them rather than alone.

- **[[2402.15487|RoboEXP]]** — An interactive explorer building an ==Action-Conditioned 3D Scene Graph== via GPT-4V-driven exploration encoding static relations + action effects; **70–90%** on interactive-exploration tasks vs 0–30% baselines, near-zero unexplored space, robust to human intervention.
- **[[2602.18374|ZS-IP]]** — A ==zero-shot interactive perception== loop pairing GPT-4o with an ==Enhanced Observation== module (pushlines, grasp keypoints, virtual grid) and ==memory-guided action==; **0.7–0.8** SR on complex multi-step tasks vs MOKA's 0.0–0.5.
- **[[2608.12683|FUSE Affordance Grounding]]** — Actively grounds a functional-language query by fusing ==SAM3== semantic evidence with ==SSNR geometric uncertainty== from a ==3D Gaussian Splatting== map, gated by ==action-value entropy== between a CLIP-MLP amortized planner and explicit exploration; **72%** success on a new Habitat benchmark, **1.33×** faster than fully-explicit search.
- **[[2603.12939|RoboStream]]** — A training-free VLM framework binding visual evidence to metric 3D as ==Spatio-Temporal Fusion Tokens== and tracking object identity + action causality in a ==4D Causal Spatio-Temporal Graph== for CoT spatial verification; **88.9%** real under total occlusion, **90.5%** sim long-horizon, removing the graph collapses SR to 14.5%.
- **[[2603.02511|Unveiler]]** — A decomposed ==Spatial Relationship Encoder== (IL + PPO) + ==convolutional push-grasp decoder== for retrieving fully occluded objects; **90.0%** completion in 6–9-object scenes at **260 ms** inference, zero-shot to a real Dofbot-Pro.
- **[[2608.13028|PassGen]]** — LoRA-tunes ==Stable Video Diffusion== with an ==Intention-Aware Temporal Face Encoder== embedding gaze/micro-expression tokens, plus ==morphological depth erosion== reinstating sensor-void artifacts, feeding an ==Intention Gating== module fusing gaze confidence with object-approach velocity; real UR5e **54/60** success, false triggers **2/30** vs **25/30** ungated.
- **[[2606.18189|E-MPC]]** — An ==Engagement-aware Model Predictive Control== framework for human-in-the-loop robots that explicitly optimizes user engagement alongside task success + workload via a predictive interaction-dynamics model and strategic ==engagement-only queries==; on robot-assisted bite acquisition, engagement **4.00 vs 2.40** and agency **4.30 vs 2.60** at **100%** task success.
- **[[2509.00178|Poke and Strike]]** — RL jointly learns a ==task-informed exploration policy== + online physical-property estimator, with exploration rewards auto-derived from a privileged task policy's sensitivity to estimation error, switching to execution via ==ensemble uncertainty==; **90.1%** sim Striking SR (vs baselines' ≤40%), real KUKA iiwa **8/9** Striking / **8/10** Edge Pushing.
- **[[2409.00215|Intent-Aware-Co-Manipulation]]** — A co-manipulation method using ==dynamical-system intent models== + dual particle filters driving ==confidence-based variable impedance== without F/T sensing; **83%** faster than admittance control with **24%** less human linear effort on a 4.5 kg object.
- **[[2410.18964|DISaM]]** — A dual-policy framework formalizing active information-gathering as a ==factorized Contextual MDP==, pairing an ==Information-Seeking policy== (trained by an ==intrinsic reward== from the manipulation policy's action discrepancy) with an Information-Receiving policy + uncertainty switching; outperforms baselines on 5 tasks where sparse-reward RL fails.
- **[[2311.11893|CBP]]** — A ==model-based conditional behavior prediction== framework switching between courtesy and influence modes by detecting human goal uncertainty, with long-term safe control; matches a data-intensive baseline, **99.96%** empirical safety, humans hesitate less (0.87 s vs 1.2 s).
- **[[2506.14968|FEAST]]** — An ==LLM-powered assistive feeding== system with modular tool-changing and natural-language skill personalization grounded in a 21-user study; addresses **36/46** personalization requests, **89.27%** bite-acquisition and **93.07%** bite-transfer in a 5-day in-home study.
- **[[2506.14727|Casper]]** — A ==VLM intent-inference== assistive-teleoperation system generating open-set intent candidates with visual prompting + confidence estimation, executing an 8-behavior skill library on confirmation; **88.9%** mobile-manipulation SR (beating full teleop's 76.4%), lower NASA-TLX workload, **+5.7%** from visual prompting.
- **[[2505.03815|Semantic-Level]]** — Survey proposing a ==Cognitive Synergy Architecture== integrating semantic perception, cognitive planning, explainable control, safety design, and multimodal intention recognition for human-centric collaborative robots; reviewed work cuts behavior-planning failures **37%** and lifts gaze+gesture intent accuracy **78.5%→92.3%**.
- **[[2504.00907|Ask-to-Act]]** — A VLA (LLaVA-OneVision) trained by ==online RL (DD-PPO)== with a ==Llama-3 process-reward model== giving dense rewards for both task progress and useful clarifying questions on ambiguous rearrangement; **45.9%** SR / **40.6%** ambiguity-resolution on unseen scenes, **+24–30%** over the best zero-shot MLLM baseline.
- **[[2503.13250|MindEye-OmniAssist]]** — A gaze-driven assistive-robot system inferring implicit user intent from gaze patterns over ==YOLO-World== open-vocab detections via a Transformer gaze network, with ==DeepSeek-R1== translating intentions to action plans + gaze verbal confirmation; **96.52%** intention-recognition accuracy, **74.5%** overall SR across 55 undefined assistive tasks.
- **[[2503.09335|NVP-HRI]]** — A zero-shot voice + deictic-posture HRI system inferring human intent and using the ==Segment Anything Model== for zero-shot segmentation of unknown objects, with GPT-4-turbo translating multimodal intent to action sequences under a real-time collision cross-check; **+59.2–65.2%** interaction efficiency, collision-free trajectory SR **73% → 94%**.
- **[[2503.04163|VLA-Expert-Collaboration]]** — A bi-directional framework pairing a ==VLA model with an expert-in-the-loop== via a cyclic N-model:1-expert interaction so both improve together, deployable with a brain-computer interface; improves SR across MT10/MT50 while cutting required expert steps and operator workload.
- **[[2503.03579|Generative-System-Robot-to-Human-Handovers]]** — A generative handover system pairing an ==MLLM== (Gemma 2) for intent inference from verbal + receiving-hand cues with a ==text-guided diffusion model== generating a unified collision-free hand-object-gripper spatial configuration; **44.24%** fuzzy-intent accuracy, **80–93%** real execution SR across 5 objects in ~3 s.
- **[[2502.20606|CNABU]]** — Formalizes manipulation-enhanced mapping as a ==Partially Observable Markov Decision Process== over a metric-semantic grid, with ==Calibrated Neural-Accelerated Belief Updates== (==evidential deep learning==) propagating beliefs after actions, chosen by a ==Volumetric Information Gain== planner; zero-shot UR5 revealing **39%** more unseen shelf objects than baselines.
- **[[2310.00433|ActPerMoMa]]** — A receding-horizon ==active-perceptive mobile-manipulation== planner ranking candidate paths by a utility fusing ==visual information gain== with ==reachability-map grasp executability==, plus a momentum term against oscillation; **92.6%** sim SR (61.8% on Hard-Grasps vs 43.8%), **80%** real on a cluttered-room TIAGo++.

#### 5.6 Runtime Monitoring & Failure Detection

The grounding harness works the other direction too: rather than catch the foundation model's errors before acting, watch the *policy's own execution* and flag failures as they happen — the safety net that turns an open-loop policy into a recoverable one.

- **[[2608.13438|ContactGuard]]** — A multi-view ==JEPA==-style latent world model rolls a chunked policy's own planned actions forward in embedding space, scored by a frozen ==logistic== probe that aborts before gripper closure; live cup-grasp AUC **0.992** vs single-view's **0.928**, shuffling actions collapses AUC to near-chance **0.483–0.535**.
- **[[2606.23085|Foresight]]** — A policy-agnostic failure detector extracting ==action-conditioned predicted latents== from a frozen V-JEPA 2-AC world model, a causal sequence model trained on sparse trajectory labels with ==Functional Conformal Prediction== for guaranteed-FPR thresholds; **0.94** balanced accuracy / **0.89** ROC-AUC on LIBERO-Long, up to **0.93** real on ReactorX/Franka.
- **[[2606.16690|PATCH]]** — An ==action-chunk-conditioned latent patch innovation== monitor predicting the expected top-view latent patch field under normal execution, restricting attention to a projected ==execution corridor== with self-motion filtering and temporal latching; **0.96** balanced accuracy offline (0.04 false-positive on static changes), **85%** real task completion at 55.2 Hz.
- **[[2604.13788|FIDeL]]** — A policy-independent monitor pairing ==optimal-transport nominal-behavior memory== + ==spatially-aware conformal thresholds== with a ==VLM semantic filter== that splits benign drift from real failure; **70.50%** AUROC on BotFails (**+5.30%** over SOTA), filtering averts a **23%** true-negative-rate loss.
- **[[2509.01746|Fail2Progress]]** — Detects real-world 'incorrect symbolic prediction' failures then casts targeted fine-tuning-data generation as constrained ==variational inference==, using ==Stein Variational Inference== to parallel-sample states/actions maximizing epistemic-uncertainty entropy; lifts Points2Plans **11%→86%** SR, beating Gradient (**45%**)/Sampling (**53%**) baselines.
- **[[2504.11170|Sparse-MAF-AAE]]** — An unsupervised real-time anomaly detector for robot pick-and-place fusing ==Masked Autoregressive Flow== latent modeling, an ==adversarial autoencoder==, and ==sparse L1== feature selection over an LSTM encoder, robust under limited noisy torque signals; **89.23%** AUROC on torque alone (+4.96% over MVT-Flow), **0.86 ms** inference for embedded deployment.
- **[[2412.04455|Code-as-Monitor]]** — A monitor recasting failure detection as ==spatio-temporal constraint satisfaction==: a ==Constraint Painter== abstracts task entities into 3D elements a VLM tracks via ==per-subgoal Python monitor code==; **+17.5%** Stack-in-Order SR, **−34.8%** execution time and **−52.2%** tokens, **90%** real reasoning pick-place.
- **[[2310.17552|Sirius-Runtime]]** — A ==cVAE latent-dynamics model== rolling out futures to anticipate undesirable states, with a ==failure classifier trained on human interventions== as implicit labels; **+32%** sim / **+40%** hardware SR and **16%** more efficient human supervision than IIL baselines.
- **[[2309.08603|Fallback-Safe-MPC]]** — A robust-MPC framework closing the loop on ML-perception runtime monitors: a ==conformal-prediction monitor== detects the first perception fault beyond tolerance with probabilistic guarantees, triggering a planned-for ==safe-recovery fallback== on non-ML measurements; near-**0%** false positives at 5% risk, preventing crashes under degraded vision.

#### 5.7 Dexterous & Task-Oriented Grasp Synthesis

The other half of affordance grounding: don't just locate *where* to act, generate the full multi-finger grasp pose that *uses* the object correctly. These models condition grasp synthesis on language, affordance, and part semantics — turning "use the hammer" into a functionally-aligned dexterous grasp.

- **[[2607.25049|FIRMGrasp]]** — Treats the friction coefficient as a random variable and folds ==Conditional Value-at-Risk== into grasp quality, defining a ==CVaR margin== plus a closed-form analytic counterpart that generalizes the ==Ferrari-Canny margin==; correlation with nominal metrics falls **0.95 → 0.32**, AUC **0.629/0.78**, friction-sensitive grasps **67.7% → 57.6%**.
- **[[2607.04554|HUGS]]** — A three-stage dexterous grasp framework learning ==object-conditioned human priors== (an MLP for contact mode, a ==diffusion model== for wrist-pose init) from the new **HUGS-Human** dataset (1.8K grasps/304 objects) to guide robot-specific ==force-closure optimization==; synthesizes **3.2M** grasps across 157K scenes, **0.934** F1 cross-scale transfer.
- **[[2606.09314|KPGrasp]]** — A dexterous grasp generator using an ==all-Euclidean 3D hand-keypoint== parameterization with a ==Transformer conditional flow-matching== model learning implicit grasp priors from data, no contact losses or test-time refinement; **76.3%** GSR (+47.4pp) at **2.4 mm** penetration on Dexonomy, **0.032 s**/grasp (**87×** faster).
- **[[2604.05697|GraspSense]]** — A language-guided dexterous grasp planner building a ==physics-informed force map== of spatially-distributed admissible contact loads (via local wall-thickness approximation) for force-map-aware grasp re-ranking + ==impedance-based per-finger stiffness==; **8–12×** higher minimum admissible contact force and **zero** force-bound violations vs baselines.
- **[[2604.04138|Sparse-Taxonomy-Grasp]]** — A two-stage dexterous framework (GRIT) decoupling grasp generation from ==taxonomy-conditioned control==: a planner picks a sparse grasp taxonomy from 30 templates + a wrist orientation, then an RL policy with a ==multiplicative composite reward== enforces adherence; **87.9%** Objaverse SR (+6.0% over RDG), real task-specific grasp adaptation.
- **[[2603.08021|AffordGrasp]]** — A ==cross-modal latent diffusion== grasp synthesizer bridging the language-geometry gap with a ==self-looping Affordance Generator== (point-wise affordance maps) + a ==Distribution Adjustment Module== for physical/semantic constraints; up to **80.08%** semantic accuracy on OakInk, strong zero-shot on HO-3D/AffordPose, real ShadowHand deployment.
- **[[2601.08246|FSAG]]** — A finger-specific affordance grounder extracting multi-scale ==Stable Diffusion hyperfeatures== to predict a ==Finger-Specific Affordance Field== (five per-finger likelihood maps from human videos), injected into ==grasp refinement==; **2.491** KLD vs CMKA's 11.184, **85–90%** real unseen-object grasps, hardware-agnostic to a Linker Hand L20 without retraining.
- **[[2601.05499|TOSC]]** — A ==task-oriented shape completion== method reconstructing only task-relevant ==potential contact regions== from partial point clouds (VLM + 3D generative candidates, a discriminative AE selector) then a ==FlowGrasp== model; **−55.26%** Chamfer error and **+16.17%** grasp displacement over baselines, zero-shot to 9 novel categories.
- **[[2512.03874|OmniDexVLG]]** — A framework integrating grasp ==taxonomy, contact semantics, and functional affordance== via an LMM reasoner (RAG + CoT) feeding a ==diffusion VLM grasp generator==, plus a force-closure-validated data engine; **0.97/0.83** coarse/fine taxonomy accuracy mitigating hallucination, **0.80** sim / **0.71** real SR beating GraspGPT.
- **[[2511.09602|ScaleADFG]]** — An ==affordance-based functional grasping== framework with an automated foundation-model pipeline generating a multi-hand ==scale-variant dataset== + a lightweight ==CVAE (ScaleADFG-Net)==; **94.58%** functional SR in benchmarks, **76.0%** real on an Allegro Hand across 15 novel objects, consistent across small/medium/large scales.
- **[[2511.13327|ZeroDexGrasp]]** — A zero-shot task-oriented dexterous grasp synthesizer using ==prompt-based multi-stage MLLM reasoning== to infer contact regions/grasp-type/rotation, ==geometry-guided verification==, then ==contact-guided energy refinement==; **3.322 cm³** penetration vs 7.846, **90.1%** part accuracy nearly doubling baselines, real on Kinova + Inspire/Papert hands.
- **[[2511.09558|IFG]]** — An ==internet-scale VLM-guided== functional grasp pipeline using SAM/VLPart to localize ==useful regions==, force-closure optimization for robustness, distilled into a real-time ==conditional diffusion transformer==; **62.8%** lift SR on a bottle vs 49.1% baseline, more natural functional grasps (bottle-neck, hammer-handle), no human demos.
- **[[2511.07418|Lightning-Grasp]]** — A procedural dexterous-grasp synthesizer decoupling geometry from search via a ==Contact Field== (6D contact abstraction in a BVH) optimized by ==blockwise zeroth-order== contact search + multi-chain IK; generates **1,000–10,000** diverse grasps in **2–5 s** (hundreds-to-thousands× faster), serving as a large-scale grasp data engine.
- **[[2508.15002|GraspQP]]** — A rigorous differentiable ==force-closure metric== as an implicit ==Quadratic Program== (KKT-differentiated) paired with ==MALA*== dynamic-resetting + adaptive-temperature optimization against mode collapse; **52%** Unique Grasp Rate vs next-best **45%**, **1.6M** grasps over 5,700 objects/5 grippers/3 taxonomies.
- **[[2508.08896|Affordance-Dexterous-Grasp]]** — A two-stage dexterous grasping framework pairing ==Human Hand Trajectory Imitating== priors with a ==Negative Affordance-aware Segmentation== module (VLM/SAM/CLIP-guided) distilled via teacher-student; **8.6** Human-likeness Score (vs UniDexGrasp++'s 5.4) and **4** Affordance Score (vs 28), avoiding unsafe grasp regions like knife blades.
- **[[2508.03339|UniFucGrasp]]** — A ==human-hand-inspired unified annotation== strategy (sparse-matrix JAM mapping human motions to diverse hands) producing a 100K+ multi-hand functional-grasp dataset + a ==CVAE gesture generator== over object+hand point clouds; **69.55%** sim SR vs DFG's 65.52%, **+11.54–23.08%** real over DFG across InspireHand/HnuHand.
- **[[2506.05576|TD-TOG]]** — A hand-annotated real-world RGB-D ==task-oriented-grasping dataset== (30 categories / 120 subcategories) plus modular ==Binary-TOG/OS-TOG== frameworks fusing CLIP+SAM zero-shot recognition with one-shot affordance recognition; **70.9%** real TOG SR on unknown categories (vs 52.7%/44.1%), **F1 0.819** novel-subcategory discrimination.
- **[[2505.13441|GraspMolmo]]** — A ==fine-tuned Molmo VLM== predicting task-oriented grasp points from a single RGB-D frame + language, trained on the 379K synthetic ==PRISM dataset==, with point-to-grasp matching to a stable proposal network; **76.7%** TaskGrasp-Image Top-1, **61.1%** real overall SR, more than double the closest baseline.
- **[[2505.12294|PartDexTOG]]** — A ==language-driven multi-scale part analysis== method using LLMs for category/part grasp descriptions feeding a ==conditional diffusion== grasp generator with ==geometric-consistency== grasp-part selection; SOTA on OakInk-shape (**3.58%** better Penetration Volume, **2.87%** better Grasp Displace), zero-shot over **5** tasks / **47** unseen categories.
- **[[2503.19457|G-DexGrasp]]** — A three-stage ==retrieval-augmented generation== grasp synthesizer where an MLLM infers affordance/contact parts to retrieve generalizable priors, a ==part-aware CVAE== generates coarse poses, then ==prior-guided optimization== refines; **2.94 cm³** intersection vs 7.98 and **71.6%** part accuracy vs 46.5% on unseen categories.
- **[[2503.07360|AffordDexGrasp]]** — An open-set language-guided grasp method introducing a ==Generalizable-Instructive Affordance== intermediate, with an MLLM intention pre-understanding stage + ==cascaded Affordance/Grasp Flow Matching== and non-parametric optimization; **0.480** R-Precision and **45.1%** SR on Open Set A, zero/one-shot generalization to novel categories on a Leap Hand.
- **[[2504.18829|Dexonomy]]** — A two-stage ==differentiable-physics== (Warp + MuJoCo) grasp-synthesis pipeline template-aligning then refining object poses via simulation, with a ==self-improving template library==; **9.5M** grasps over **10,700** objects across **31** GRASP taxonomy types, **60.50%** benchmark SR (vs BODex's 49.23%), **82.3%** real Shadow-hand SR on unseen objects.
- **[[2504.04573|DexTOG]]** — A language-guided ==task-oriented dexterous grasp== framework pairing a ==DiffuTOG== conditional diffusion model (point cloud + hand model + task text) with an autonomous RL-validated data engine producing DexTOG-80K; **0.1067** Q1 stability (over UniDexGrasp), **67%** stapler-click vs a baseline's 10%, collision-free grasps 6→63%.
- **[[2502.04873|Training-free-TOG]]** — A ==training-free two-stage pipeline== combining a pretrained 6-DoF grasp generator with a ==VLM filter== selecting task-compliant grasps from a single RGB-D image + language, over 5 query strategies incl. ==contact-point generation==; **70%** combined SR in real clutter, capable VLMs best when free to suggest contact points.
- **[[2407.17348|DexGANGrasp]]** — A ==conditional GAN== with a dedicated evaluator generating diverse dexterous grasps in real time from single partial point clouds, extended by ==DexAfford-Prompt== (MLLM affordance reasoning + VLM 2D segmentation) for task-oriented filtering; **95.0%** sim / **91.43%** real general grasp, **72%** task-oriented at 30 ms/grasp.
- **[[2407.15161|FFHFlow]]** — **FFHFlow-lvm**, a flow-based ==Deep Latent Variable Model== pairing a ==Grasp Flow== likelihood with an input-dependent ==Prior Flow==, exploits exact NF likelihoods for view- vs object-uncertainty in an ==uncertainty-aware grasp evaluation==; **65.0%** confined-space real SR (cVAE's **10.0%**), **130ms** sampling — **10x** faster than diffusion.
- **[[2406.18722|Open-World-Grasping-Large-Vision-Language]]** — An open-world grasping pipeline (OWG) integrating an ==LVLM (GPT-4v)== with segmentation + 4-DoF grasp synthesis in a three-stage ==visual-prompting== closed loop (refer-segment → grounded plan → LVLM-rank); **80.8%** mIoU grounding (+27.7% over SoM), **83.3%** seen / **50.0%** cluttered-unseen real, planning critical (−22%).
- **[[2405.19291|Grasp-as-You-Say]]** — A language-guided dexterous framework (DexGYSGrasp) with the LLM-annotated ==DexGYSNet dataset== and a ==progressive learning== strategy decoupling intention/diversity generation (a diffusion model without penetration loss) from quality refinement; SOTA intention alignment and diversity at comparable quality on an Allegro hand.
- **[[2404.15189|Text2Grasp]]** — A two-stage method pairing a ==text-guided conditional diffusion model== (TextGraspDiff) with ==text-guided contact optimization== and ==LLM semi-automatic text generation== for part-level grasp control; **87.76%** grasp-part accuracy with template text on OakInk, lower penetration (0.40) and more diverse natural poses than GrabNet.
- **[[2404.10399|FoundationGrasp]]** — A 6-DoF task-oriented grasp framework integrating ==open-ended LLM semantic + VLM geometric knowledge== over distinct semantic/geometric Transformer branches, with the new ==LaViA-TaskGrasp dataset==; **~+10%** mAP over semantic-only GraspGPT, **71–74%** real action SR across held-out instance/class/task settings.
- **[[2503.00508|HGDiffuser]]** — A single-stage task-oriented grasp generator injecting ==human-derived constraints== (hand-object contact regions, wrist orientations) into a ==DiT-based diffusion== model's guided sampling via a differentiable loss, no task-specific training; **81.21%** sim task-oriented SR (**+9.74%** over the best two-stage baseline) at **0.191 s**/sample (**−81.26%** time).
- **[[2412.10694|EDGS]]** — A voice-driven dexterous grasping system fusing voice + vision via ==Referring-Expression Representation Enrichment== for disambiguation, then ==grasp candidate generation== over a skeleton/centroid + hand-workspace model refined by force-closure/wrench-space analysis; **96.1%** SR across 360 cluttered attempts, **+19.5** IoU from RERE on Grounded-SAM.
- **[[2503.06227|GAT-Grasp]]** — A gesture-driven task-aware grasping method where a human points at a target then demonstrates the grasp, retrieving and ==transferring affordance== from a memory bank of human-object-interaction videos via a hand-to-gripper mapping; **70%** seen-object SR (vs GPT-4o's 48%) and **40%** on unseen objects (vs 33%), generalizing from gesture rather than language.

**Planning & Affordance Grounding — Decision Matrix**

| Need | Recommendation |
|---|---|
| Zero-shot LLM task decomposition | [[2201.07207\|LLM-Zero-Shot-Planners]] (**73%** executability) |
| Structured, verifiable plan synthesis | [[2604.02812\|Neuro-Symbolic-Robot-Policies]] (**100%** schema) |
| Contact-rich cost design + diagnosis | [[2605.02600\|CoRAL]] (**+50%** unseen) |
| Learn from sparse human corrections | [[2603.04560\|MEMO]] (**88%** at 1.52 feedbacks) |
| Zero-shot 3D value-map control | [[2307.05973\|VoxPoser]] (**88%** static) |
| Keypoint-constraint manipulation | [[2409.01652\|ReKep]] (**44.3%** vs 10%) |
| Affordance mask + motion prediction | [[2606.02551\|AFUN]] (**90%** real) |
| Language-driven grasp in clutter | [[2506.18448\|GraspMAS]] (**0.76** cluttered) |
| Build a world model by interaction | [[2402.15487\|RoboEXP]] (**70–90%**) |
| Retrieve occluded objects | [[2603.02511\|Unveiler]] (**90.0%**, 260 ms) |
| Detect execution failures at runtime | [[2412.04455\|Code-as-Monitor]] (**90%** real), [[2604.13788\|FIDeL]] |

^dm-5

> [!star] Key Papers — Foundation-Model Grounding Landmarks
> - [[2201.07207|LLM-Zero-Shot-Planners]] — the origin paper showing a frozen LLM's plans become executable with a thin grounding harness, launching the LLM-as-planner line.
> - [[2307.05973|VoxPoser]] — the canonical demonstration that an LLM writing code over 3D value maps yields zero-shot manipulation without any robot training data.
> - [[2409.01652|ReKep]] — established relational keypoint constraints as a reusable, optimizable interface between VLM reasoning and real-time control.
> - [[2604.02812|Neuro-Symbolic-Robot-Policies]] — proved a small VLM trained on synthetic behavior-tree data can match large foundation models while guaranteeing structural validity.
> - [[2402.15487|RoboEXP]] — the interactive-perception landmark: an action-conditioned scene graph built by *acting to perceive*, not perceiving then acting.

^key-papers-5

> [!tip] Foundation Models Reason Broadly but Ungrounded — Everything Here Is a Grounding Harness
> The raw LLM/VLM brings generalization the policy can't learn from demos, but it hallucinates affordances, mislocalizes objects, and proposes physically impossible plans. Every system in this section is a *harness* that catches those errors: symbolic scaffolds ([[2604.26569|LLM-Flax]]'s PDDL, [[2604.02812|Neuro-Symbolic-Robot-Policies]]' behavior trees), optimization layers ([[2409.01652|ReKep]]'s solvers, [[2605.02600|CoRAL]]'s MPPI), retrieval memory ([[2603.04560|MEMO]]), or interaction to verify ([[2402.15487|RoboEXP]], [[2602.18374|ZS-IP]]). The 2026 read: the value isn't the foundation model — it's the grounding interface that makes its reasoning *physically committable*. For the reasoning/CoT lineage that trains this competence directly into VLAs, see [[05_VLA-Reasoning-and-CoT#5. Reasoning-Traced Training]] and the spatial substrate in [[10_Manipulation-Skill-Learning#2. 3D & Multi-View Spatial Representation]]. The same harness pattern applied to a moving base — where grounding must survive locomotion — is [[13_Navigation-and-Mobile-Manipulation#5. Mobile Manipulation & Loco-Navigation]].

^insight-5

## Part C — Learning Signal

*What actually trains the backbone — reward signals and policy-steering, demonstrations and data generation, and the transfer that lets a skill cross embodiments.*

### 6. RL & Policy-Steering for Manipulation

Imitation learning gives a policy a good prior but a hard ceiling: it can only be as good as its demonstrations, and it has no mechanism to *improve* from its own experience. Reinforcement learning is how manipulation policies break that ceiling — but RL on real robots is brutally sample-inefficient, and the action space (continuous, chunked, high-dimensional) fights the algorithms designed for discrete Atari. This section is the field's effort to make RL *work* for manipulation: at scale on real fleets, with action chunking, and without destabilizing a pretrained backbone.

The split is between *training a policy from reward* and *steering an existing one*. RL algorithms (6.1) attack the core sample-efficiency and action-space problems — chunked Q-learning, flow-policy gradients, GPU-parallel multi-task RL, hierarchical task/joint decomposition. Policy-steering and residual control (6.2) take a complementary, increasingly dominant tack: freeze a capable imitation backbone and learn only a *small residual* — in latent space, action space, or impedance — so online RL gets the backbone's competence for free and only fine-tunes the last mile. The strategic lesson the field is converging on: don't RL the whole policy, RL the *correction*. Rounding out the section: hard safety guarantees that wrap either camp (6.3), the classical optimal-control and motion-planning toolbox that predates and complements both (6.4), and the curricula, resets, and exploration schemes that keep real-robot RL running unattended (6.5).

#### 6.1 RL Algorithms for Manipulation

Attack the core problems — sample efficiency, the continuous chunked action space, and multi-task scale — that make reinforcement learning hard on real manipulators. Boundary rule: manipulation-benchmark-specific flow/diffusion-policy RL lives here; algorithm-general treatments live in [[03_Imitation-Learning-and-RL#4.2 Flow & Diffusion Policy Optimization]].

- **[[1806.10293|QT-Opt]]** — A distributed ==continuous-action Q-learning== system via ==CEM== over 580K real grasps on 7 KUKA arms; **96%** grasp on unseen objects vs 78% prior, with emergent singulation and regrasping; the canonical scalable real-robot RL system.
- **[[2104.08212|MT-Opt]]** — An off-policy ==multi-task Q-learning== system with a learned ==success detector== and skill-based task impersonation across a 7-robot fleet; **3×** average SR over single-task baselines, learning pick-cloth to 70% in under a day; shared-representation transfer.
- **[[2605.21330|Joint-Sensor-In-Hand]]** — A ==teacher-student== framework for tendon-driven in-hand reorientation where a ==Proprioceptive Transformer== student learns from only noisy joint position/velocity histories (auxiliary pose + clean-state reconstruction), no vision or tactile; **11.83 RPM** cube rotation on a real ORCA hand (**3.1×** over proprioceptive PPO), **100%** accuracy.
- **[[2605.17486|DyGRO-VLA]]** — A cross-task RL fine-tuning method for VLA scaling pairing an ==Information-Bottleneck shared representation== with an online ==Mixture-of-RL-Residuals== routing task-conditioned action refinements without distorting the base policy; **97.1%** avg LIBERO (**+4.4%**), **+9.8%** long-horizon, **79.2%** zero-shot RoboTwin2.0, curing catastrophic forgetting.
- **[[2605.11151|RankQ]]** — An RL method shaping Q-values with a ==self-supervised pairwise action-ranking loss== over success/failure-partitioned data, ordering good actions above perturbed/random ones; **+64.3%** in low-data vision manipulation and real cube-stacking **43.1%→84.7%** (+41.6pp) sim-to-real.
- **[[2604.19677|MATCH]]** — An RL framework learning ==hybrid position-force control== selecting position or force mode per end-effector dimension, with ==Mode-Aware Training== propagating gradients only through the active mode and a ==Supervised Selection Loss== guiding mode choice; **97.0%** fragile peg-in-hole at **0.0%** breaks, real Franka FR3 transfer at **~30%** lower force.
- **[[2603.18246|Rapid-Adaptation-of]]** — An RMA extension for deformable objects pairing a privileged-simulator ==Dynamics/Shape Encoder== with real-world ==Adaptation Modules== (L1-distilled from depth+action buffers) trained via two-phase RL; **85%** 1D-Inserting / **80%** 2D-Covering real SR (vs baselines' 10-17.5%), zero-shot sim-to-real, no real demos.
- **[[2505.08078|Batch-Online-RL-Study]]** — An empirical study distilling a three-step recipe — ==expressive IL policy== → ==Q-function== → ==implicit policy extraction== — with temporally-correlated exploration noise; **2×** over IL across 6 sim tasks and **+30%** real tape-hanging from self-collected data.
- **[[2508.11143|AC3]]** — An off-policy ==actor-critic over action chunks== with ==intra-chunk n-step TD== and asymmetric success-only updates; robust online improvement over IL on 15 BiGym + 10 RLBench tasks from 10 demos at **2.9 ms**/chunk.
- **[[2509.09671|Dexplore]]** — Treats human MoCap demos as ==soft references== via ==Reference-Scoped Exploration== (adaptive termination + reward weighting) in a PPO imitation stage, distilled into a ==vision-based skill-conditioned generative controller== for depth-only deployment; **87.7%** Inspire-hand / **78.7%** Allegro-hand SR, RSE ablation drops SR to **29.9%**.
- **[[2507.07969|Q-chunking]]** — An RL formulation redefining the ==Q-function over action sequences== for ==unbiased n-step value backups== with behavior regularization; SOTA offline-to-online sample efficiency on long-horizon sparse-reward tasks, more temporally coherent exploration.
- **[[2605.11009|ACSAC]]** — An ==adaptive chunk-size actor-critic== whose ==causal Transformer critic== outputs prefix-conditioned Q-values across horizons, with joint rejection sampling over a flow-BC policy; best across **25 OGBench tasks** (offline + offline-to-online), adaptively picking long chunks (≈4) for coarse approach, short (≈1) for precise placement.
- **[[2503.04089|OPG-Policy]]** — A deep ==RL== push-grasp framework for retrieving occluded targets in clutter that feeds RGB-D + ==amodal-mask== heightmaps to a ==Deep Q-Network==, with an MLP coordinator deciding push vs grasp from Q-values + occlusion features; **82.66%** sim success at **2.61** attempts and **85%** real (**90%** on unseen objects) with zero-shot sim-to-real.
- **[[2503.03660|Transformer-Critic-SAC]]** — A ==causal Transformer critic== for SAC consuming short state-action segments with ==gradient-averaged n-step returns== (no importance sampling) and target-free freezing; **96.8%** dense Box-Pushing, solving most Meta-World ML1 in ~5M steps.
- **[[2507.21053|FPO]]** — A ==Flow Policy Optimization== method extending PPO to flow policies by proxying the likelihood ratio with the ==CFM loss==, integration-method-agnostic; wins 8/10 MuJoCo tasks and **70.6%** vs 46.5% on under-conditioned humanoid control.
- **[[2606.22303|FlowDPG]]** — An off-policy actor-critic applying ==deterministic policy gradient== to flow-matching policies by ==distilling the critic gradient into the velocity field== (BPTT-free, L2 to a Q-improved clean action) under an IQL twin-critic, offline-to-online; **92%** real dual-arm AirPods assembly (vs BC's 64%, RLT/QAM's 80%), **+18%** disturbance-recovery from online RL.
- **[[2503.02738|Variable-Friction-In-Hand]]** — An end-to-end ==diffusion-policy imitation== for a variable-friction gripper reposing arbitrary objects in-hand, with a ==hybrid discrete-mode + continuous-joint action space== and ==sim-real co-training== from a smoothness-optimized RL teacher; **71.3%** avg over 5 objects, co-training lifting 32%/54% → **71.3%** at −63.3% training time.
- **[[2502.18423|Retrieval-Dexterity]]** — A large-scale parallel-RL dexterous-hand system for object retrieval in clutter, learning emergent ==push/stir/poke== skills via a ==potential-based reward== under PPO then distilling to a Transformer student from simple visuals; **+59.4%** retrieval SR / **−90%** steps in sim, **−51.2–61.9%** real retrieval time, zero-shot transfer.
- **[[2608.00554|DexMani]]** — Introduces ==contact-conditioned manipulability==, an analytic descriptor distilled by an ==energy-based prior model== into a ==residual bias== that guides a downstream RL policy; **57.5%** avg SR on LEAP Hand, **43.4%** avg cross-embodiment SR guiding four distinct hands on Unscrew Cap from one prior.
- **[[2606.15133|DragMesh-2]]** — A contact-driven dexterous framework for ==articulated objects== where hand-handle contact alone drives object motion, trained by ==PICA== PPO injecting observable physical proxies through a Gated-Linear-Attention encoder, no force feedback; **0.56** deterministic success under 4× OOD damping (vs state-only PPO's 0.27), exposing nominal-success brittleness.
- **[[2510.08884|Lookahead-RL-In-Hand]]** — A ==model-based lookahead RL== for in-hand manipulation pairing a PPO actor-critic with a concurrently-learned deterministic dynamics model, then ==PPO-MPC online planning== over short-horizon rollouts guided by the value function; **+1.065** reward / **+2.2** consecutive cube successes over PPO, robust to **4×** density but no cross-object transfer.
- **[[2508.01695|DexReMoE]]** — An in-hand reorientation ==Mixture-of-Experts== combining specialist policies via a soft gating network over a compact ==PointNet++ + one-hot category== representation, PPO-trained then per-category expert fine-tuned to avoid forgetting; **6.05** S_min on training objects (vs MMoE's 3.36), **19.12** average consecutive successes on 50 OOD objects.
- **[[2504.21585|Multi-Goal-MBRL]]** — A ==model-based RL== framework (GC-PMPC) for multi-goal dexterous in-hand manipulation pairing ==goal-conditioned probabilistic neural-network ensembles== with an ==asynchronous MPC== policy + state smoothing; outperforms SAC/TD7/TDMPC in sim, learns 3 die-manipulation goals in **~84 min** total on a real DexHand 021 while retaining prior goals.
- **[[2502.18015|SPIN]]** — A two-stage long-horizon prehensile+non-prehensile framework distilling a simulator ==Skill-RRT== planner into a reactive ==Diffusion Policy== via IL, with RL-trained ==goal-conditioned connectors== bridging skill state-gaps and noise-based plan filtering; **95/93/98%** sim, **2.68–3.02 s** vs Skill-RRT's 79–121 s, **80–90%** zero-shot real Franka.
- **[[2606.32027|FPL]]** — A ==Freeform Preference Learning== framework letting annotators define arbitrary natural-language preference axes, extending ==Bradley-Terry== to a ==language-conditioned VLM reward== and training a ==multi-axis reward-conditioned policy==; **+38pp** real SR over the best baseline, compositional generalization + test-time steerability, **50%** faster annotation.
- **[[2606.30474|GOMP]]** — Reformulates non-prehensile manipulation from pose-reaching to ==graspability-field optimization==, using a smooth ==scalar graspability field== (distance to a graspable set) as a dense RL signal that also decides when to terminate and grasp; **75.5%** sim / **62%** real success, **97%** grasp success post-manipulation, vs pose-conditioned baselines' **36.1%**.
- **[[2502.11918|VLP-manipulation]]** — A ==Vision-Language Preference== framework using VLMs (GPT-4V) to auto-annotate trajectory preferences over the new MTVLP dataset, with a CLIP + cross-modal-transformer model scoring trajectory preferences for offline RLHF; matches scripted-reward labels and beats R3M/VIP/LIV/VLM-RM on Meta-World, generalizing to unseen tasks/instructions.
- **[[2502.08054|COMBO-Grasp]]** — A bimanual occluded-grasping method self-supervising a ==non-dominant constraint arm== (force-closure approximation) and training the ==dominant grasping arm== via PPO with ==value-function-guided policy coordination==, distilled to vision students; **68.3%** real average over 60 trials on seen + unseen objects, robust sim-to-real from point clouds.
- **[[2502.04307|DexterityGen]]** — **DexGen**, a ==diffusion-based generative controller== combining RL-trained motion primitives (Anygrasp-to-Anygrasp data + domain randomization) with ==guided sampling== translating coarse human commands into precise dexterous actions; **10–100x** manipulation-stability improvement, first system for certain complex real-world dexterous tool tasks.
- **[[2412.01791|DextrAH-RGB]]** — The first ==RGB-based visuomotor dexterous-grasping== policy to sim-to-real transfer, distilling a PPO state-teacher (==Automatic Domain Randomization== over ==geometric fabrics==) into an RGB student via online DAgger; competitive grasping on **11** real objects, robust under direct sunlight where depth fails, **1–2 s** faster bin-packing (8.22 vs 10.66s).
- **[[2502.02316|DIME]]** — A reformulation of ==MaxEnt-RL for diffusion policies== via a tractable entropy lower bound with convergence guarantees; SOTA across **13** continuous-control envs, competitive with CrossQ/BRO at lower compute (4.5 h vs 8.5 h on humanoid-run).
- **[[2505.22094|ReinFlow]]** — A method turning a deterministic flow-matching policy into a ==discrete-time Markov process== via ==learnable noise injection==, enabling exact log-probs and a new policy-gradient theorem for PPO fine-tuning; **+40.34%** manipulation SR at **−23.2%** compute, often 1–4 denoising steps.
- **[[2606.23623|dVLA-RL]]** — A policy-gradient method for discrete-diffusion VLAs casting multi-step generation as a ==Markovian denoising trajectory== so PPO uses its tractable trajectory log-prob (gradients on newly-denoised tokens), with a ==hybrid denoising-step== strategy assigning 1/2/4 steps by complexity; LIBERO **88.1→99.7%** and RoboTwin 2.0 **61.4→92.0%** over SFT-only.
- **[[2505.13925|TR-DRL]]** — An RL method exploiting full and ==partial time-reversal symmetry== via dynamics-filtered trajectory-reversal augmentation + reversal-guided reward shaping; sharply better sample efficiency (TR-SAC **0.33** vs SAC's 0.11 IQM at 50k) across Robosuite and Meta-World MT50.
- **[[2312.14134|Diffusion Reward]]** — Compresses observations via ==VQ-GAN== then trains a ==VQ-Diffusion== model on the latent video, defining the RL reward as the ==negative conditional entropy== of its predicted future frames given the agent's history; **+38%** MetaWorld and **+35%** Adroit SR over baselines, zero-shot to 5 unseen MetaWorld tasks.
- **[[2606.10305|SARM2]]** — A multi-task ==stage-aware reward== model fusing an ==action-primitive stage estimator== with a ==multi-gate Mixture-of-Experts== value decoder for dense per-step rewards, driving the ==SPIRAL== on-policy residual-RL self-improvement loop; **0.006** demonstration MSE and ρ=**0.667** rollout classification vs over-optimistic VLM baselines, 12/12 on Folding-Shorts-Flat.
- **[[2602.19313|TOPReward]]** — A zero-shot reward method extracting ==affirmative-token log-probabilities== from video VLMs rather than generated numbers, normalized into dense per-episode progress, plus the ManiRewardBench suite; **0.857** value-order correlation on OXE (**+0.663** over GVL), **0.654** failure ROC-AUC, lifting AWR policy SR over BC across 6 real tasks.
- **[[2606.23280|CRWM]]** — A ==Causal Reward World Model== pre-trained on multi-task offline data to capture causal relations among reward variables (Explicit Mechanism Decoupling), distilled into an LLM that does ==causal pruning== to emit reward functions in one pass for unseen tasks; **0.92/0.87** zero-shot Dexterity SR (0 search iterations), **96.5%** sim-to-real distinguishability vs 53.9%.
- **[[2608.03872|EvoHIL]]** — Pairs a ==Self-Evolving Reward== with ==Action Flow Stabilization== (a ==flow-matching== policy over action chunks) and ==Retention-Aware Offline Fine-tuning== on relit replay data; **1.00** SR on USB insertion under both original and **90%**-shifted lighting, **−44.5%** command irregularity.
- **[[2608.03753|GORDON]]** — Learns dense, ==object-centric rewards== from action-free video via a ==Graph Neural Network== with ==activity-aware weighted pooling==, then auto-discovers subtask boundaries from the temporal gradient of the learned reward; **74.4%** avg full-task SR (**+35.3pp** over the best learned baseline), lowest classification "rho" of **0.629**.
- **[[2606.22027|RARM]]** — A ==confidence-gated progress reward model== turning a single successful demo into dense RL feedback via a ==self-supervised contrastive visual comparator== with per-clip ==confidence gating== calibrated from the reference to suppress false-positive progress; superior over 9 sim tasks, up to **4×** real DSRL SR on Bimanual Clothes-Folding vs sparse-reward.
- **[[2509.23155|LAGEA]]** — A reward-shaping framework using a ==VLM for schema-constrained language feedback== on failed trajectories, localizing ==key frames== via saliency, then aligning language with visual states to emit dense failure-focused ==RL== rewards; **80%** Meta-World MT10 (**+5.3%**), **51.67%** Fetch (**+17%**), with faster convergence.
- **[[2509.22402|ReLAM]]** — A two-stage reward method learning a ==keypoint-based anticipation model== from action-free video (Grounded-SAM + CoTracker keypoints, a DINOv2 Transformer predicting subgoals) that supplies dense Euclidean-distance rewards to a policy; often **100%** SR with faster learning on Meta-World, beating image-based-subgoal baselines on ManiSkill.
- **[[2505.10911|ReWiND]]** — Trains a ==language-conditioned reward function== from a handful of demos, robustified by synthesizing failure-like trajectories via ==video rewinding== and ==LLM-driven instruction generation==, guiding offline IQL pre-training + online RL fine-tuning on unseen tasks; **79%** IQM on 8 unseen MetaWorld tasks (**+97.5%**), real bimanual **12%→68%** SR in **1 hour**.
- **[[2606.25700|LoRA-Policy-Libraries]]** — A study applying ==Low-Rank Adaptation== to online multi-task PPO, injecting low-rank matrices into a frozen base policy to build a memory-efficient specialist library instead of full fine-tunes; **92.8–99.4%** fewer trainable params and up to **93%** storage savings for 50 specialists while matching full-fine-tune SR across 6 Meta-World tasks.
- **[[2510.14768|CADRE]]** — A ==Contact-Aware Dynamic Recovery== RL framework augmenting PPO observations with pretrained ==Neural Descriptor Field== contact features to catch dropped objects *and* recover a task-favorable grasp; **96.27%** OOD-screwdriver catch in sim, **80.0%** real screwdriver recovery vs baselines' 0–33.3%, zero-shot to unseen geometries.
- **[[2512.16861|ReinforceGen]]** — A framework decomposing long-horizon tasks into hierarchical stages (initiation / skill / termination), pre-training via automated object-centric data generation from ~10 demos then fine-tuning each component online with ==residual RL== + real-time replanning; **80%** SR on long-horizon Robosuite tasks, **+89%** relative over Hybrid Skill Policies.
- **[[2512.02011|DexScrew]]** — Trains RL finger-rotation gaits on simplified simulated fastener geometry, deploys them as ==skill primitives== in human-assisted teleoperation to collect real tactile multisensory data, then ==behavior-clones== a policy fusing tactile feedback + temporal history; **98.75%** nut-fastening / **95.00%** screwdriving progress ratio.
- **[[2606.09615|DexPIE]]** — A dexterous real-world policy-improvement framework pairing ==human-in-the-loop staged DAgger== with ==asynchronous inference== + ==continuous optimality-conditioned== diffusion-actor supervision from a distributional critic; **+37%** average SR over the initial BC policy across three real tasks, with **+14%** from async inference and **+8%** from staged DAgger.
- **[[2506.05168|Fabrica]]** — A hierarchical dual-arm ==assembly-hold sequence + grasp optimizer== (DP state-space search from CAD) hands off to a single-step insertion policy trained via RL over ==path-centric SE(3)-equivariant coordinates== + ==plan-guided residual actions==; **80%** real step-wise SR, **81%/95–100%** cumulative SR with 1–2 interventions, generalist matching specialists.
