- **[[2606.03335|DGPO]]** — A ==Demonstration Guided Policy Optimization== method with importance-weighted PPO + adaptive BC on the GPU-parallel MT-Libero benchmark (26,800 SPS); **85.2%** state / **69.8%** visual SR vs MT-PPO's 42.5%; adaptive BC is load-bearing.
- **[[2604.10165|MoRI]]** — A ==Mixture of RL and IL experts== routing deterministic coarse motions to BC and fine-grained manipulation to RL for long-horizon tasks; **97.5%** avg over 4 tasks (vs BC **37.5%** / RL **77.5%**), with **−21.0%** training time and **−85.8%** human intervention.
- **[[1910.11956|Relay-Policy-Learning]]** — A ==bi-level hierarchical policy== bootstrapped by ==relay imitation== on unstructured play (relabeled into subgoals) then refined by ==goal-conditioned RL==; RIL alone scores **2.4/4** steps (**21.7%** full success) and RRF lifts SR across **17** Franka-Kitchen compound goals where flat BC/HRL fail; the relay-IL+RL origin.
- **[[2602.13810|Mean-Velocity-Policy]]** — A ==mean-velocity flow field== for one-step RL action generation, with an ==Instantaneous Velocity Constraint== supplying the missing boundary condition + Q-guided generate-and-select; **0.88** avg SR over 9 manipulation tasks (vs FQL 0.44), **10.93 ms** inference, fastest online training at 153.6 it/s.
- **[[2601.10930|Contact-Intention-RL-MPC]]** — A hierarchical framework pairing a high-level ==RL policy== predicting an object-centric ==contact intention== (contact locations + post-contact subgoal pose) with a low-level ==contact-implicit MPC==; **100%/99.69%** seen/unseen-shape sim SR, real **100%/95%** seen/unseen pushing, **40×** fewer RL steps than end-to-end.
- **[[2407.08028|AutoMate]]** — A learner of ==specialist + generalist assembly policies== over **100** diverse parts via ==disassembly-based demo generation== + ==RL with an assembly curriculum== + IL distillation; specialists hit **81.50%** sim SR (vs RL-only **38.45%**) and a 20-assembly generalist reaches **80.42%**, transferring to real — assembly skill at geometric scale.
- **[[2602.16863|SimToolReal]]** — A single ==object-centric goal-reaching RL policy== trained in sim over ==procedurally generated tool primitives== with an LSTM history, deployed strictly zero-shot via human-video goals + FoundationPose tracking; **98.0%** real brush-sweeping progress vs 0–10.8% over 24 unseen tasks.
- **[[2605.03363|Hierarchical-RL-QP-Grasp]]** — A method decoupling ==multi-agent RL task-space planning== (CTDE) from a ==GPU-parallel QP joint controller==; **81.4%** vs end-to-end RL's 13.2% on a 20-DoF hand, zero-shot grasping 22/26 unseen objects with reactive recovery.
- **[[2508.12296|robust-and-compliant]]** — A ==Force-Vision Fusion Controller== driving ==Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning== over decomposed fit-amount subtasks, then ==policy distillation== into a single generalist for batch precision assembly under uncertain fits; **98.5%** average SR, **96-100%** on unseen batch-manufactured components, lowest contact forces vs baselines.
- **[[2310.01404|H-InDex]]** — Pre-trains a visual representation from an off-the-shelf ==3D human hand pose estimator==, adapts it to robots by fine-tuning only ==BatchNorm affine parameters== (0.18% of weights), then keeps it frozen during RL while updating BatchNorm statistics via ==EMA==; **98.2** normalized avg score over 12 dexterous tasks (vs VC-1's 81.4, R3M's 72.8), **91.3%** avg SR.
- **[[2306.06799|Point-Cloud-RL-Study]]** — A systematic study of ==3D point-cloud RL== with a framework integrating post-processing, ==PointNet/SparseConv backbones==, point frame-stacking, and 3D augmentation against 2D RGB/RGB-D agents; point-cloud agents win on 3D-spatial-reasoning tasks (ManiSkill), PointNet showing intrinsic perturbation robustness reducing the need for augmentation.
- **[[2306.12372|Dress-Them-All]]** — A ==Dense Transformation== RL policy + Q-function over partial point clouds for deformable dressing, distilling multiple pose-specific ==teacher== policies into one generalist via ==Earth Mover's-distance== policy distillation with guided domain randomization; **0.86±0.17** whole-arm dressed ratio over 17 users, **66% vs 1%** SR over no distillation.
- **[[2304.03223|DexDeform]]** — A dexterous deformable-manipulation framework learning an ==implicit scene representation== + a ==hierarchical skill model== from teleoperated human demos, refining trajectories with ==differentiable physics== in a bootstrapping loop; **0.842–0.972** normalized Earth-Mover's-Distance improvement over RL and trajectory-opt baselines across 6 tasks.
- **[[2603.10971|ContactExplorer]]** — A dexterous RL exploration method with a ==contact-coverage intrinsic reward== combining sparse post-contact finger-region novelty with a dense pre-contact reaching signal, conditioned on ==learned-hash object-state clusters==; **86%** average over 7 tasks (88% Constrained-Retrieval vs baselines' 0%), **2–3×** fewer steps, sim-to-real on a LEAP hand.
- **[[2503.04538|SRSA]]** — An assembly framework with a ==skill-retrieval== model predicting which pre-trained specialist transfers best to a new task and ==skill-adaptation== via PPO + self-imitation fine-tuning; **+19%** SR / **2.4×** faster (dense) and **+135%** SR (sparse), **90%** mean sim-to-real transfer with no real-world training.
- **[[2309.00987|Sequential-Dexterity]]** — Chains PPO-trained dexterous sub-policies via a learned ==Transition Feasibility Function== that ==bi-directionally fine-tunes== earlier policies toward states favorable to later ones, reused at deployment for ==policy-switching==; **46%** sim SR on 4-stage block-building (T-STAR's **19%**), **33%** zero-shot real Franka+Allegro vs baselines' **0%**.
- **[[2312.17116|SAM-G]]** — Offloads visual-RL generalization to a frozen ==Segment Anything Model==: one-shot ==PerSAM adaptation== + ==DINOv2+SAM point-feature correspondence== turns a single labeled training frame into iteratively-refined masked observations for any RL backbone; **44%** relative gain over PIE-G on DMControl video-hard, **29%** on Adroit dexterous-manipulation video-hard.

#### 6.2 Policy-Steering & Latent / Residual Control

Freeze a capable backbone and learn only a small correction — in latent space, action space, or impedance — so online RL gets the backbone's competence for free and fine-tunes only the last mile.

- **[[2607.20912|URF]]** — A diffusion policy predicting virtual target pose + stiffness + an ==impedance-admittance switch ratio== that drives a low-level unified controller, with switch-ratio labels derived from ==measured force magnitude== (no stiffness ground truth needed); **90%** box-flipping SR at **0%** critical failures (vs admittance-only's 25%/86.7%), **100%** line-pressing SR.
- **[[2607.08877|FlowDAgger]]** — Inverts expert corrections into a frozen generative policy's noise space via ==action inversion== (fixed-point reverse-time integration), training a lightweight ==noise policy== with a dual-buffer DAgger loop; **+0.25** mean SR over frozen π0.5 on 12 MetaWorld tasks, Toolbox-Packing **13%→80%** real, held-out-task drift **−0.08** vs SFT's **−0.94**.
- **[[2606.29201|MoRE (Mode Redirection Distillation)]]** — A post-hoc policy-editing framework using a ==differentiable mode-redirection editor== guided by a frozen ==K-way behavior-mode classifier==, applying a ==retain loss== + ==gated redirect loss== to suppress undesired modes directly in weights; **+44pp** avg Deployment SR with zero inference-time overhead, beating CPL/DynaGuide steering.
- **[[2606.01865|SDP]]** — A ==Set-Supervised Diffusion Policy== extending CLIC's single-step ==desired action set== to action-chunks from paired robot-undesired / human-corrective actions, sampled via ==constrained diffusion sampling== approximating the ==CLIC loss==; beats DP/DP-DPO/Ambient-DP/CLIC, most robust to noisy intervention feedback, best real Insert-T/Round-table SR.
- **[[2605.19919|ZPRL]]** — A residual-steering method freezing an IL policy and learning a ==residual perturbation in a VIB bottleneck latent==, steering behavior through a low-dimensional space; **−29%** velocity / **−39%** acceleration (smoother) and **+12.5%** real on Flip-Egg vs action-space residuals.
- **[[2603.05296|LPS]]** — Latent Policy Steering optimizes a latent actor via gradients through a differentiable one-step ==MeanFlow== base policy and a fixed action-space critic, constrained to a ==spherical latent geometry== preventing norm explosion; beats DSRL/one-step-distillation on OGBench and real robots, robust across the α range that breaks tuned baselines.
- **[[2602.22088|Force Policy]]** — Formalizes a physically-grounded ==interaction frame== from stiffness + task intent, driving a ==global-local vision-force policy== decoupling a low-freq Global Vision Policy from a high-freq Local Force Policy via a dynamic router, scheduled by ==Dynamic Time Warping==; **90%** SR on 'Scrape off Sticker' vs **50%** wrench-only baseline.
- **[[2602.04208|SCALE]]** — A training-free single-pass VLA strategy jointly modulating ==vision-encoder attention temperature== and ==action-sampling temperature== from a ==self-uncertainty measure== (KL to high/low-uncertainty references); **+5.8pp** OpenVLA on LIBERO / **+19.5pp** real in-distribution, beating MG-Select by **10.7pp**.
- **[[2602.01789|RFS]]** — A ==Residual Flow Steering== method jointly learning an ==initial-noise latent== for global modulation and a ==residual action== for local correction over a frozen flow policy, PPO in sim + TD3+BC real; **0.861** mean sim SR, real grasping **30→74%** from 50 corrections.
- **[[2509.19301|ResFiT]]** — A residual-RL method freezing a chunked-BC base policy as a black box and learning a per-step ==off-policy residual== (REDQ + n-step + symmetric sampling); **~200×** more sample-efficient than on-policy residual RL and the first real RL on a **29-DoF** bimanual humanoid, **14→64%** sparse-reward.
- **[[2505.16892|FlashBack]]** — **CSA** distills a diffusion shared-autonomy teacher into a ==consistency model== mapping a noisy user action to a refined expert-like action in one step, conditioned on a predicted next-state; **5–18x** speedup (**NFE=1**, **<2ms**) over DDPM, real peg-insertion SR **66.7%→83.3%** with faster, smoother assistance.
- **[[2505.03077|Latent Adaptive Planner]]** — **LAP** casts dynamic manipulation as inference in a low-dim latent space via a ==causal Transformer== + ==classical variational Bayes==, adapted online by ==variational replanning== into an MPC executor; matches model-based box-catching (**29–30/30**) at **11.47J** vs BC's **31.39J**, transferring across robot platforms.
- **[[2605.05925|DexSynRefine]]** — A ==flow-matching HOI motion prior== refined by ==task-space residual RL== with GRU contact/dynamics adaptation; **68.1%** mean sim SR vs kinematic retargeting's 5.8%, and **+50–70pp** real (9/10 Bowl) over retargeting.
- **[[2603.10052|OmniGuide]]** — An ==inference-time energy-function guidance== method modulating a frozen flow-matching VLA's generation via attractive/repulsive Cartesian gradients; collision-avoidance safety **7.0%→93.5%**, **+26%** task success and **−46%** collisions, no retraining.
- **[[2506.12678|ABA]]** — **Adapting by Analogy** detects OOD observations, then uses expert language + ==semantic segmentation== to retrieve functionally-corresponding in-distribution observations (refined via retrieved-plan entropy), averaging embeddings to intervene on a frozen policy's input; **+76%** SR on novel-category objects vs generic-embedding retrieval, **3–5** expert queries/rollout.
- **[[2406.15917|BGR]]** — A ==Bellman-Guided Retrials== method adding deployment-time trial-and-error using only expert data: a ==progress-rate value function== flags suboptimality, the robot recovers and resamples, biasing away from failed states; **+20%** sim / **+50%** real with no new data or supervision.
- **[[2605.29564|VE2VF]]** — A ==HIL-RL teacher-student distillation== from a vision-enabled teacher into a ==vision-free proprioceptive student== with task-relative poses; **95.0%** overall and **100%** on OOD USB insertion where the vision teacher scored 0%.
- **[[2509.19696|Diffusion-Impedance-Learning]]** — A diffusion model reconstructing a ==simulated zero-force trajectory== to drive ==energy-based directional impedance adaptation==; **100%** tight-clearance peg insertion across geometries vs fixed-impedance's 0%, parkour traversal without jamming.
- **[[2502.09614|DexTrack]]** — A generalizable neural ==tracking controller== for dexterous manipulation learned from human references via an iterative ==RL+IL data flywheel==, with ==homotopy optimization== solving hard tracking and per-trajectory transfer; **>10%** SR over leading baselines, generalizing to thin objects and robust to noisy kinematics/unreachable goals in sim and real.
- **[[2608.06210|VIDP]]** — Extracts motion primitives from position-only demos via a ==Task-Parameterized Directionality-Aware Mixture Model (TP-DAMM)==, mapping variability to target stiffness for a ==Diffusion Policy== predicting pose + impedance; **70%** Pulley Assembly / **75%** Cable Routing (vs a fixed-impedance Stiff-DP's 35%/0%), lower peak/mean interaction forces throughout.
- **[[2410.19235|DIPCOM]]** — A classifier-free ==diffusion policy (DIPCOM)== predicting Cartesian poses *and* stiffness for compliant contact-rich tasks, with 6D-continuous orientation + an extended ==Temporal Ensemble==; **55.88%** fine powder (vs 9.96%) on grinding, **77.32%** erased / 52.3% SR on erasing, matching baselines on bimanual peg insertion.

#### 6.3 Safe & Constrained Control

Steering catches OOD; this axis *guarantees* it can't fail unsafely — barrier functions, reachability shields, and differentiable constraint layers that bound forces, velocities, and collisions while preserving the task, often with hard rather than soft guarantees.

- **[[2608.13284|PARRY]]** — A ==Rodrigues-rotation velocity deflection== safety layer for teleoperated manipulators, rotating the relative end-effector velocity around an obstacle-normal axis instead of superimposing potential-field forces, with ==overshoot-protected time-to-collision prediction==; **82.7%** collision-avoidance rate vs **79.1%** potential-field, **76.2%** CBF-QP.
- **[[2607.14455|MD-COAS]]** — Unifies an ==inexact Augmented Lagrangian Method== soft feasibility prior with a ==Convex Feasible Set== hard-projection operator inside model-based diffusion, scheduled by ==primal-dual adaptive scheduling== tuning enforcement intensity against a compute budget; **100%** success / zero violations on a 7-DoF arm-avoidance task across all difficulty levels.
- **[[2607.10842|D-SafeMPC]]** — Integrates discrete-time ==Control Barrier== and ==Lyapunov Function== guidance into the reverse diffusion process (gradient-based steering toward safe, goal-directed regions), then an iterative ==MPC== projection enforces hard constraints at each denoising step; **100%** safe-goal-reach in Dynamic-Env-1, **92.5%** in Dynamic-Env-3, sim-to-real on a Franka.
- **[[2607.08948|SplatCtrl]]** — Fits a fixed-budget ==isotropic Gaussian Splatting== scene from RGB-D with dynamic relocation, extending a ==Gaussian Process distance field== into a differentiable ==SDF== + collision probability feeding ==QP-IK barrier constraints==; **95.01%** sim planning SR vs 3D-GS's **32.83%**, **95.8%** real Franka SR across twelve unseen scenes.
- **[[2607.00424|Conformal CBF-OSCTC]]** — A robust operational-space controller pairing an ==Extended State Observer== (position-only disturbance estimation) with a ==high-order CBF== whose disturbance bound is estimated online via ==sliding-window conformal prediction==; **1.712e-05 m** MSE tracking (vs OSCTC's 2.033e-04), zero violations at **1 kHz** on Franka FR3.
- **[[2607.00326|NeHMO]]** — A decentralized multi-arm planner learning a ==physics-informed neural Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability== value function (symmetry + boundary-residual learning) modeling inter-arm safety as a ==zero-sum differential game== in receding-horizon optimization; **92%** SR / **2%** collision on dual-UR5 at **36ms**, scaling to 30-DoF where centralized CHOMP collapses.
- **[[2606.15594|SLS2]]** — A probabilistically-safe pixel-to-control framework pairing a ==JEPA latent world model== with ==conformal prediction== (latent-dynamics error sets + in-domain region) and a GPU-accelerated ==System-Level-Synthesis robust MPC==; **93.75%** sim rope manipulation, **100%** safety on robustly-constrained tasks, deployed real for bimanual rope manipulation.
- **[[2606.08414|PACT]]** — A post-training framework aligning pretrained ==diffusion policies== to physical safety as a ==CMDP regularized projection==, distilling an implicit ==safety teacher's score== under a curriculum ramping constraint strength to avoid forgetting; **−31.0%** safety violations / **+30.7%** SR in sim, **+36.0%** real Safe-Rate, **5×** faster convergence than RL.
- **[[2605.25672|Compliant-Pushing]]** — A framework letting ==impedance-controlled== robots perform compliant non-prehensile pushing via an extended ==MPC== regulating contact force + sliding velocity, with an ==energy-tank passivity filter== preventing unsafe force escalation during human interference; **1.65×10⁻²m** mean error over 216 KUKA tests, safe set-point limiting on a YuMi.
- **[[2605.21811|Safe-Steerable-Geometric-Policy]]** — A model-based dexterous controller (SafePBDS) extending ==Pullback Bundle Dynamical Systems== with ==Pullback Control Barrier Functions== (hard acceleration constraints) plus a ==Task Manifold Action Interface== for steering; **92.5%** 4-finger grasp on 20 objects, first model-based palm-down in-hand reorientation.
- **[[2605.19029|SV-DRO]]** — Stein Variational DRO uses ==Stein Variational Inference== to evolve parameter particles into a task-aware posterior weighted by an ==optimality gap==, fed into a receding-horizon ==MPC== penalizing that gap instead of worst-case cost; **93.25%** bimanual pushing / **100%** within-hand positioning, up to **3×** more robust than DRO/ensemble MPC.
- **[[2604.23863|Safety-Value-MPC]]** — A framework augmenting finite-horizon ==MPC with a Hamilton-Jacobi reachability terminal constraint== (a learned DeepReach safety value function) so the terminal state stays in a certified control-invariant safe set, giving recursive feasibility; **81%** sim / **80%** hardware safety on a 14-DoF manipulator (vs vanilla MPC's 30%), robust across horizons.
- **[[2604.16667|Liquid-E-Stop]]** — A plug-and-play ==MPC emergency-stop== layer for robots carrying open liquid containers, formulating a time-optimal spill-free QP over a ==linearized spherical-pendulum slosh model== retaining bilinear coupling, with no-spill tilt constraints; **0.87 s** real stop at **5.3°** max slosh (within a 5° limit) vs a no-slosh baseline's 22.3° spill.
- **[[2604.03868|Belief-Space]]** — A risk-constrained ==belief-space MPPI== controller maintaining a particle filter over ==latent uncertainty== (unobservable object/geometry params) and minimizing a CVaR objective under a hard ==CVaR safety-margin constraint== with a probabilistic guarantee; **82%** SR at zero external contact on simulated dexterous stowing (vs chance-constrained MPPI's 50%).
- **[[2603.09083|Provably-Safe-TrajGen]]** — A manipulator trajectory generator pairing a ==Deep Stochastic Koopman== dynamics model with hierarchical collision-risk verification inside ==MPPI== (parallel physics sim then ==Sum-of-Squares== certification), bounding collision probability under non-Gaussian non-convex uncertainty; **94%** sim / **90%** real UR5e human-robot collaboration, zero-shot.
- **[[2602.12794|SafeFlowMPC]]** — A safe planner combining a ==flow-matching== demonstration model with online MPC projecting proposals onto a ==safety manifold== (kinematic, collision, terminal-safety constraints); **86%/82%** static/reactive grasp SR with zero collisions (vs BC's 2%, MPC's 76%) at 62–63 ms/step, handovers within 0.12 m of demos.
- **[[2508.18268|SafeBimanual]]** — Test-time trajectory optimization injecting ==differentiable safety cost functions== (from an unsafe-bimanual-interaction taxonomy) into a diffusion policy's final denoising steps, dynamically scheduled by a ==Chain-of-Thought VLM==; **+13.7%** SR / **−18.8%** danger rate in sim, **+32.5%** real SR on a Galaxea-R1 humanoid.
- **[[2503.06736|OSC-CBF]]** — A framework integrating ==Control Barrier Functions== with ==Operational Space Control== via a task-consistent QP that minimally perturbs nominal commands while enforcing full second-order dynamics and torque limits; **2.94 kHz** for 168 constraints scaling past 1000, robustly enforcing 5 simultaneous safety constraints on a Franka Panda.
- **[[2409.19190|RAIL]]** — A safety wrapper enclosing an IL policy in a ==continuous-time reachability safety filter== + ==model-based backup planner==, swapping in a guaranteed-safe plan whenever the IL plan is unsafe; **0%** collisions (vs 5–35% baselines) at **0.42 s**/plan on a real Franka, with safety sometimes lifting weak-policy success.
- **[[2401.17500|LeTO]]** — A visuomotor policy embedding a ==differentiable trajectory optimization== layer (a QP enforcing position/velocity/acceleration constraints + smoothness) end-to-end, fed by an LSTM observation encoder as a linear cost; **100%** Can-task SR at **2 m/s²** vs LSTM-GMM's 15.5, **16.1 N** peak contact force vs DiffusionPolicy's 27.3, intrinsically safe and interpretable.
- **[[2205.06311|Provably-Safe-RL-Shield]]** — A framework pairing a low-frequency ==SAC== agent with a **250 Hz** ==safety shield== that verifies intended and failsafe trajectories under ISO Speed-and-Separation Monitoring, overriding unsafe actions; **0%** safety-critical collisions vs the baseline's ~20–25%, while learning effective human-evasion strategies.
- **[[1906.08880|Variable-Impedance-Control]]** — A systematic action-space study introducing ==Variable Impedance Control in End-Effector Space== (VICES), outputting EEF poses + per-axis stiffness/damping gains over an analytical controller; **~100%** constrained-task SR with better sample efficiency, zero-shot Panda→Sawyer and real-Panda wiping (**75%** of the line) at controlled forces.

#### 6.4 Optimal Control & Motion Planning for Manipulation

The classical model-based toolbox that has accreted alongside the RL work: sampling-based MPC/MPPI planners and trackers, search- and optimization-based trajectory generation, and QP constraint-projection layers. Learning enters only as an auxiliary (a learned dynamics model, a Q-value prior, a diffusion warm-start), never as the deployed policy itself.

- **[[2604.21456|TSMC]]** — Reframes trajectory/policy optimization as sampling from a ==Boltzmann-tilted control-as-inference distribution== via ==Tempered Sequential Monte Carlo== (adaptive tempering + importance reweighting + ==Hamiltonian Monte Carlo rejuvenation==); beats Parallel-IPOPT/MPPI on Acrobot swing-up and Pusher-Slider, solves swing-up tasks where PPO/SAC fail outright.
- **[[2604.17833|DART]]** — A two-layer ==learning-enhanced MPC== for dual-arm non-prehensile tray manipulation, a high-level MPC computing tray-tilt trajectories over object-tray dynamics (physics / regression / RL variants) atop a low-level impedance controller; the RL variant hits **0.82 s** settling and **~2 mm** error, generalizing across 18 mass/geometry/friction configs.
- **[[2605.30778|Object-Informed-MPPI]]** — A hierarchical ==Model Predictive Path Integral== framework for non-prehensile manipulation decoupling a long-horizon ==object-level planner== (treating contact forces as direct inputs) from a short-horizon ==robot-level tracker==; **82%** sim SR (SOI vs standard MPPI's 42%), **90%** hardware (vs 70%), **22%** faster execution in cluttered pushing.
- **[[2604.02021|Discrete-Continuous-Planning-Bridge]]** — A bridge connecting a discrete RL planner to continuous execution via a ==geometry layer== (26-connected actions, spline smoothing) + ==physics layer== (TP-DLS IK); **100%** SR vs baseline 56–70%, order-of-magnitude smoother joint motion.
- **[[2510.03768|Adaptive-Precision-Pushing]]** — A model-based planar-pushing controller pairing a ==GRU dynamics model== (PyBullet domain-randomized) with a modified ==MPPI== enabling variable-length pushes + side-switching, retargetable without retraining; **0.3 mm** one-step prediction error, **100%** precise-posing at a 3 mm/2° threshold sim and 5 mm/2° real on a Franka.
- **[[2508.02649|Manip4Care]]** — An assistive-care pipeline manipulating human limbs via antipodal grasp sampling + an ==MPPI trajectory planner== with IK-Handshaking joint-limit feasibility and a next-config-predicting network exposing new task regions; **97%/92%** supine/sitting limb-repositioning SR within biomechanical limits, lifting bed-bathing wiping coverage **14%→49.7%**.
- **[[2507.15677|Cable-Arm-DDMPC]]** — A data-driven ==MPC== for Flexible Cable-Driven Robotic Arms inferring implicit system dynamics from input-output data via behavioral-systems theory (no physical model), with a ==Data Search Algorithm== selecting the most relevant data subgroup per step; **2.070 mm** positioning accuracy, **0.541° vs 1.418°** tracking error over PID, **80%** solve-time cut.
- **[[2507.06625|Q-Guided-SVMPC]]** — A learning-guided ==MPC== framing trajectory optimization as ==Q-guided posterior inference== on an RL policy prior, replacing hand-designed costs with learned soft Q-values and ==Stein Variational Gradient Descent== for mode-preserving particles; **2.42%** collision rate in Reach-with-Obstacles, real Kinova fruit-picking **93.3%**/**80%** avoidance/reaching.
- **[[2410.09740|GS-MPC]]** — Represents a granular pile as reconstructed ==3D Gaussian splats== and learns a ==graph neural network dynamics model== predicting per-splat motion, planning via ==gradient-based visual MPC== against a ==density-field occupancy cost==; **0.89/0.79/0.75** sim SR on collect/split/redistribute, **0.85** zero-shot real collection on pistachios/almonds/peanuts.
- **[[2410.00841|Diffusion-Contact-Search]]** — A hybrid contact-planner (DIPS) for multi-finger manipulation integrating an offline ==diffusion trajectory predictor== + discriminator into ==A* search over contact modes== with particle-based variability propagation; discovers contact sequences absent from training data, turning a screwdriver **41%** further with 10/10 real robustness.
- **[[2211.16657|Task-Driven-Model-Reduction]]** — A method learning a ==task-relevant reduced-order Linear Complementarity System== (few hybrid modes) that minimizes the MPC ==task-performance gap== vs full dynamics via a violation loss + trust-region LCS-MPC; cuts active modes thousands→**~7** at <5% loss, learning cube reorientation in **~4 min** at >50 Hz.
- **[[2606.22143|Eikonal-Caging]]** — A whole-arm manipulation planner recasting dynamic ==caging== as a minimum-time object-escape problem satisfying an ==eikonal equation==, learning a differentiable ==escape-time field== via a PINN that biases trajectory optimization toward escape-resistant configs; robust to contact-model mismatches (friction, shape) with open-loop transfer to a real Franka.
- **[[2606.14606|Impedance-MPC]]** — An efficient ==Impedance MPC== for dexterous hands cancelling tendon-transmission dynamics to a scalar double integrator, with an integrating ==Kalman disturbance state== for offset-free rejection under ISO/TS 15066 limits; **0.1 mrad** steady-state / **6.6 mrad** contact deflection at **500 Hz** (32×/23× over classical impedance), scaling to a 16-DoF LEAP.
- **[[2605.24813|Manifold-MPPI]]** — A real-time controller (MC-MPPI) enforcing ==hard manifold equality constraints== by running ==MPPI in a VAE-learned latent space== of the manifold, then a ==single-step QP== projecting residual error exactly onto it; **95%** real dynamic obstacle avoidance on a 14-DoF closed-chain dual-arm at **0.0066** violation norm (vs vanilla MPPI's 0.0314).
- **[[2605.03288|Adjoint-Neural-Control]]** — A control method for systems with implicit ==equilibrium constraints==, computing trajectory-dependent gradients by ==differentiating the equilibrium conditions== (no unrolled solvers) within a multi-stability-robust ==Receding-Horizon Continuation==; **19–127×** faster, 3–6 orders lower loss vs gradient-free baselines, real elastic-strip manipulation.
- **[[2604.25897|Variational-Belief-Grasping]]** — A robust dexterous-grasping method parameterizing multimodal pose/contact uncertainty with a ==differentiable Gaussian Mixture belief== (Gumbel-Softmax + reparameterized gradients) inside a risk-sensitive ==CVaR-MPC== hedging worst-case outcomes; **79%** robust success in sim, **100%** real YCB grasp-and-lift, ~10× faster than particle-filter MPC.
- **[[2604.10358|COSMIK-MPPI]]** — A constrained ==MPPI== collision-avoidance framework for manipulators in dynamic human workspaces pairing a ==Constraints-as-Terminations== mechanism (unsafe trajectories lose future utility, no additive penalties) with markerless human pose estimation; **100%** SR in non-convex scenes where gradient MPC fails, **5.28 cm** clearance, real-time at **21–22 ms**.
- **[[2602.21302|Task-Level ILC]]** — A ==Task-Level Iterative Learning Control== algorithm for deformable-object manipulation refining a feedforward trajectory via a ==critical point objective== against a coarse ==approximate physical rope model==; ties an overhand knot on **7** rope types within **10** trials from one demo each, **100%** repeat SR over 40 trials.
- **[[2602.16462|Particle-Reactive-Motion]]** — A reactive motion generator (SMART) pairing a ==particle-based continuous-occupancy map== (obstacle position/velocity/uncertainty) with a ==dynamics-aware MPPI planner== (D-STORM) using uncertainty-aware collision costs; **96%** avoidance of fast (0.2 m/s) obstacles (vs 73–87%), real parcel/human avoidance on a UR5.
- **[[2602.03406|Deep-Learning]]** — DESectBot, a decoupled two-segment continuum robot for endoscopic submucosal dissection, controlled by a ==GRU== network learning inverse kinematics for simultaneous position+orientation control; **1.11 mm / 4.62°** RMSE on a nested-rectangle path, beating Jacobian, MPC, FNN, and LSTM controllers, **100%** peg-transfer SR.
- **[[2512.22575|ParaMaP]]** — A GPU-parallelized reactive pipeline pairing voxel-centric ==Euclidean-Distance-Field mapping== (gather-then-transform + robot-masked update) with a ==sampling-based MPC== over SE(3) Lie-algebra pose error + distance-field collision costs; **5.322 ms** average planning (vs STORM/RRTConnect), **>150 Hz** replanning in clutter.
- **[[2511.21264|MPPI-Bimanual]]** — A ==GPU-MuJoCo (MJX) MPPI== planner with ==QP jerk-bounded projection== and phase-dependent costs; **~100%** ball-lift and **95%** handover in clutter at **<100 ms**/step, real bimanual sim-to-real via mocap pose sync; learning-free generalization.
- **[[2511.15532|Interception-NMPC]]** — An ==Adaptive-Terminal NMPC== motion planner for dynamic object interception with cooperative closed-chain dual manipulators, modulating terminal/stage cost weights by state error to mitigate ==actuator power-limit violations==; **37.1%** real catch rate over 35 throws with only **8.6%** aborts, **19.21 ms** average solve within the 40 ms interval.
- **[[2509.06953|Deep Reactive Policy]]** — IMPACT, a transformer pretrained on 10M cuRobo trajectories for globally-coherent point-cloud motion, refined by iterative ==student-teacher== distillation against Geometric Fabrics, plus a non-learned ==Dynamic Closest Point RMP== goal-modifier for fast dynamic obstacles; **75.5%** sim / **70.0%** real at **300Hz**, **3.48ms** cold-start.
- **[[2509.04645|SPOT]]** — A* search directly over continuous SE(3) object transformations from raw point clouds, guided by learned ==Object== and ==Placement Suggester== priors plus a ==Model Deviation Estimator== penalizing physically-implausible proposals in the search cost; **88%/63%** sim planning/execution on block-stacking, **100%/86%** real table bussing, beating 3D Diffusion Policy.
- **[[2509.01044|Hierarchical-Reactive-Grasping]]** — A two-layer control framework separating ==task-space velocity-field guidance== (3D collision-free fingertip paths) from a ==joint-space Quadratic Program== tracking velocities under collision, joint-limit, and obstacle constraints; **100%** concave-object grasp vs a linear baseline's 30.7%, reactive to pushes and moving obstacles, 15-DoF arm.
- **[[2509.00836|One-Step-MPPI-CDF]]** — A motion planner fusing ==MPPI== with ==Configuration-Space Distance Fields== via a one-step angle-based cost unifying obstacle avoidance + goal-seeking at horizon H=1; **99.6%** (2-DOF) to **100%** (7-DOF Franka) success escaping local minima, **>750 Hz** control vs QP's 222 Hz and original MPPI's 28–61 Hz.
- **[[2508.21375|Super-Nominal Payload Diffusion]]** — A 1D U-Net diffusion model generating dynamics-compliant joint-space trajectories conditioned on a payload-mass ==one-hot conditioning vector==, trained on cuRobo-planned + dynamics-validated data; constant **~10ms** generation, Franka Panda payloads up to **9kg (3×** nominal) at **67.6%** workspace retained, beating kinodynamic RRT/SLSQP.
- **[[2508.21001|DiTree]]** — Integrates a ==Diffusion Policy== as an informed action sampler inside a sampling-based planner (RRT), conditioned on local relative state + obstacles and MPC-style resampled with explicit collision checking, provably preserving probabilistic completeness; **+26–28%** SR over baselines in OOD CarMaze/AntMaze, **0/10** real collisions vs RRT's 8/10.
- **[[2504.00342|Constraint-Aligned-Diffusion]]** — A diffusion-loss formulation baking ==analytical constraint-violation penalties== into training with ==step-wise statistical re-weighting== for trajectory optimization; cuts constraint violations on tabletop manipulation and reach-avoid while preserving quality and accelerating SNOPT convergence over random init.

#### 6.5 Curriculum, Resets & Exploration

Real-robot RL dies on manual resets and sparse rewards — these methods automate the reset, schedule the curriculum, and shape exploration so a robot can train itself for hours without a human in the loop.

- **[[2608.13415|DP Skill Budgeting]]** — Predicts each skill's ==competence-vs-practice== curve, then reformulates the bilevel budget-allocation problem as an exact single-level ==bilinear program== via ==MDP LP duality==, solved globally by Gurobi to pick which skills to drill; on Cleanup it targets reward-**4** plans at **250** episodes where greedy baselines stay stuck at reward **1**.
- **[[2603.15789|OmniReset]]** — Automates RL-problem construction via ==diverse simulator resets== + ==large-scale on-policy training==, yielding emergent multi-phase behaviors with minimal engineering; a distilled visuomotor policy reaches **85.37%**/**56.36%**/**15.38%** zero-shot real SR on Peg-Insertion/Leg-Twisting/Drawer-Insertion vs a demo-trained diffusion baseline's **~2%** aggregated.
- **[[2502.15442|Privileged Actions]]** — A three-stage curriculum grants sim-only ==privileged actions== — relaxed robot-table collisions via a ==virtual table==, then a ==virtual force== on the object near the end-effector — phased out into normal physics under a parsimonious grasp-lift reward; emergent push-to-grasp/pivot-grasp, **33x** the reward of vanilla PPO, zero-shot real Franka.
- **[[2405.03379|RFCL]]** — A two-stage ==Reverse Forward Curriculum Learning== algorithm using SAC with a Q-ensemble: a per-demonstration ==reverse curriculum== resetting to progressively earlier expert states, then a ==forward curriculum== prioritizing high-learning-potential states; the only method solving all 21 Adroit/ManiSkill2/MetaWorld tasks, succeeding from as few as 1–5 demonstrations.
- **[[2204.02041|Example-based-Resets]]** — A framework concurrently training a ==forward agent== and a ==self-supervised reset agent== that returns the system to an initial-state distribution from examples only, with a ==preemptive reset trigger== from a classifier ensemble; matches a dense-reward oracle in sim and learns peg-insertion from scratch on a real UR3 at **85%** train efficiency.
- **[[2104.11203|Reset]]** — A ==Multi-Task learning for Reset-Free RL== method (MTRF) redefining resets as a collection of complementary tasks, with a human-designed ==task-graph meta-controller== switching policies so simpler tasks organically reset harder ones; **70%** real in-hand valve reorientation after ~60 h and **>60%** pipe insertion after ~25 h, fully autonomous.
- **[[1707.05300|Reverse-Curriculum-Generation]]** — An automatic curriculum for sparse-reward goal-reaching that expands the start-state distribution outward from the goal, training on ==good starts== (intermediate success probability) and generating feasible starts via short ==Brownian-motion rollouts==; solves previously-intractable manipulation like key insertion where standard RL fails.

**RL & Policy-Steering — Decision Matrix**

| Need | Recommendation |
|---|---|
| Scalable real-robot RL from scratch | [[1806.10293\|QT-Opt]] (**96%** grasp), [[2104.08212\|MT-Opt]] |
| Offline-to-online from sparse data | [[2605.11151\|RankQ]] (**+41.6pp** real), [[2505.08078\|Batch-Online-RL-Study]] |
| RL over chunked action spaces | [[2508.11143\|AC3]], [[2605.11009\|ACSAC]], [[2507.07969\|Q-chunking]] |
| RL for flow / diffusion policies | [[2507.21053\|FPO]], [[2505.22094\|ReinFlow]], [[2502.02316\|DIME]] (13 envs) |
| Long-horizon hierarchical IL+RL | [[1910.11956\|Relay-Policy-Learning]], [[2604.10165\|MoRI]] (**97.5%**) |
| GPU-parallel multi-task RL | [[2606.03335\|DGPO]] (**85.2%**, 26.8K SPS) |
| Dexterous high-DoF grasping | [[2605.03363\|Hierarchical-RL-QP-Grasp]] (**81.4%**) |
| Fine-tune a frozen backbone safely | [[2605.19919\|ZPRL]] (latent residual), [[2509.19301\|ResFiT]] (**200×** eff.) |
| Steer a frozen VLA at inference | [[2603.10052\|OmniGuide]] (**93.5%** safety) |
| Recover from failures at deployment | [[2406.15917\|BGR]] (**+50%** real), [[2409.19190\|RAIL]] (**0%** collision) |
| Contact-rich compliance / insertion | [[2509.19696\|Diffusion-Impedance-Learning]] (**100%**) |
| Spatial generalization without state | [[2509.18644\|State-Free-Visuomotor-Policy]] (**98%**) |

^dm-6

> [!star] Key Papers — Learning-Signal Landmarks
> - [[1806.10293|QT-Opt]] — the landmark that proved continuous-action Q-learning scales to hundreds of thousands of real grasps and produces emergent manipulation behavior.
> - [[2507.07969|Q-chunking]] — established that redefining the Q-function over action chunks gives unbiased n-step backups, reconciling RL with the chunked action spaces modern policies use.
> - [[2507.21053|FPO]] — the reference for bringing on-policy RL to flow-matching policies without abandoning their generative expressivity.
> - [[2605.19919|ZPRL]] — crystallized the "RL the correction, not the policy" paradigm via a latent-bottleneck residual that keeps the frozen backbone intact.
> - [[2509.18644|State-Free-Visuomotor-Policy]] — the counter-intuitive result that *removing* proprioception improves spatial generalization, reframing what a policy should condition on.

^key-papers-6

> [!tip] RL the Correction, Not the Policy — The Residual Turn
> The field is converging on a clear strategy: a pretrained imitation backbone is too valuable to destabilize with full RL, so freeze it and learn only a small residual. [[2605.19919|ZPRL]] residualizes the latent, [[2605.05925|DexSynRefine]] residualizes task-space motion, [[2603.10052|OmniGuide]] adds inference-time guidance, [[2509.19696|Diffusion-Impedance-Learning]] residualizes impedance. Each gets the backbone's broad competence *for free* and spends its sample budget only on the last-mile correction — which is exactly where demonstrations are weakest (contact, disturbance, OOD pose). The full-RL camp ([[2507.21053|FPO]], [[2502.02316|DIME]]) still matters when there's no good backbone to start from, but for *improving* a capable policy, residual steering is the sample-efficient default. For the sim-to-real transfer this RL ultimately has to survive, see [[15_Sim-to-Real-Transfer#3. Policy-Side: Robustness & Domain Randomization]]; for force-aware residual control, see [[11_Contact-Rich-and-Tactile-Control#3. Force-Conditioned VLA Architectures]].

^insight-6

---

### 7. Demonstration, Data-Generation & Cross-Embodiment Transfer

Every backbone, every memory module, every RL algorithm in this deep-dive is downstream of one bottleneck: *where does the training data come from?* Real-robot teleoperation is the gold standard and the most expensive — minutes of data cost hours of human time, and it doesn't transfer across robots. This final section is the field's attack on the data problem, and it is arguably the highest-leverage axis: a 10× cheaper data source moves every method above it.

Three strategies have emerged. Demonstration collection and skill extraction (7.1) makes human-to-robot data capture cheaper and richer — portable mocap, smart glasses, hindsight relabeling, and self-supervised skill libraries that amortize collected data. Generative and synthetic data pipelines (7.2) sidestep human collection entirely — billion-frame synthetic datasets, LLM-driven scene generation, and digital-twin simulation. Cross-embodiment and human-video transfer (7.3) is the dream of *learn once, deploy anywhere* — retargeting human hands to robot grippers, aligning heterogeneous action spaces, and transferring skills across robot morphologies. The unifying insight: the cheapest demonstration is the one you don't have to collect on the target robot — so the field is racing to push data collection off the robot and onto humans, simulators, and other embodiments.

#### 7.1 Demonstration Collection & Skill Extraction

Make human-to-robot data capture cheaper and richer — portable mocap, active-vision wearables, hindsight relabeling — and amortize collected data into reusable skill libraries.

- **[[2608.13362|NestDex]]** — A reversible single-DoF clutch drives a ==VLM==-selected ==proprioceptive inner hand policy== during teleoperation, then a ==hand-action VAE (H-VAE)== compresses 20-DoF hand commands to **10**-D latents for a ==DINOv3==-based outer visuomotor Transformer; **100%** collection SR vs AnyTeleop's **0–75%**, H-VAE lifting Tongs Transfer **65%→100%**.
- **[[2607.25397|DR-LfD]]** — Decomposes demos into ==atomic skills== — ==SO(3)-equivariant diffusion== primitives for free-space motion, visuomotor policies for contact-rich ones — exported as ==PDDLStream== functions a ==TAMP solver== reorganizes under a ==perceive-plan-act-verify loop==; **100%** peg-in-hole at **50** demos in- and out-of-distribution, **21-step** plans, **10×** fewer demos.
- **[[2607.20345|DEED]]** — A retail-humanoid deployment recipe: a ==Data-Efficient post-training== pass (frequency alignment, curated demos, action smoothing) turning a 0%-SR GR00T checkpoint functional, then an ==Experience-Driven RECAP adaptation== + a ==GMM/Mahalanobis latent-OOD monitor==; real Unitree G1 chip-restocking SR **0%→32%→42%**, then **22%** as a second RECAP pass drifts OOD.
- **[[2607.14047|Zero2Skill]]** — An autonomous ==collect-and-reset loop== gated by ==VLM verification== that halts for an operator's ==language-guided intervention==, persisted as rules in a ==Corrective Memory== reused without retraining; **6×** less human time (30.0→**4.8 min**/50 episodes), **10.42** trajectories/human-min, verifier accuracy **40→100%**, policies matching teleop at **80%**.
- **[[2607.13882|Forward-Reverse Skill LfD]]** — Learns forward *and* reverse skills from one unfinished demo: ==twist-direction segmentation== splits contact from free-space phases, each contact segment becoming a ==screw motion primitive== under ==6D admittance control==, reversed by inverting parameters plus time-reversed ==DMPs==; **20/20** both directions, **18/20** unfinished, F1 **0.98**.
- **[[2607.13455|Auto-E2H]]** — Exploits hard-forward/easy-reverse task asymmetry: an autonomous closed-loop collector alternates easy/hard policies for mutual resets, then ==kinematic prior filtering== + ==critic-guided advantage filtering== turn reversed easy-task demos into hard-task supervision; **96.3%** sim / **70.0%** real SR, cutting teleoperation time **50–69.7%** per demo.
- **[[2607.08341|AnyDexRT]]** — A calibration-free dexterous-hand retargeter learning ==self-supervised fingertip mapping== (partial Chamfer + distance/motion preservation losses) stabilized by ==few-shot human-guidance anchors==, refined by a ==binary contact classifier== for pinch poses; **90.2%** motion consistency across 7 hands (vs GeoRT's 59.8%), **62.0%** real pinch SR.
- **[[2607.07491|SBR]]** — A gradient-free ==sampling-based retargeter== for kinematic hand teleoperation using ==Model Predictive Optimized Path Integral== + ==Improved Cross-Entropy Method==, validated via a rigorous Balanced-Latin-Square user study; **54.1%** real task SR (vs DexPilot's 44.0%, GeoRT's 26.6%), **−36.4%** joint jitter at **3.664 ms** latency.
- **[[2607.05883|DexTele]]** — A dual-arm dexterous teleoperation system pairing a ==Spatial Attention Gated Graph Convolutional Network== for cross-platform motion retargeting with a ==VLM+MPC adaptive grasping module== inferring semantic target force for compliant grasping; **0.0882 m** MPJPE across RMC-DA/YuMi/Unitree-H1, grasp SR lifted **5.13→9.13/10** at **10 FPS**.
- **[[2607.02322|The Moving Eye]]** — A ==Hybrid Dynamic Data Collection== framework using a dual-arm rig (one arm as mobile camera) with ==Hierarchical Data Decoupling== to break VLA shortcut learning from Camera-Base/Object-Position couplings; **83.0%** OOD moving-view SR (vs **43.0%** fixed-view), **+8.5–26.8%** SR gains across Gr00t/Diffusion backbones.
- **[[1810.07121|MIME]]** — The ==MIME dataset==: 8,260 paired ==kinesthetic Baxter trajectories== + synchronized human videos + multi-camera RGBD over 20 tasks; joint-angle task recognition hits **79.7%** (DTW) / **61.2%** (LSTM), and ==behavioral cloning== mapping human video → robot trajectories beats average-trajectory + k-NN on MSE — an origin point for paired human-robot demo corpora.
- **[[2306.10007|RPT]]** — A ==self-supervised sensorimotor pretraining== method via masked prediction over vision/proprioception/action tokens; **2×** SR on block stacking, **68.8%** cross-lab and **50.0%** cross-robot transfer at 10 Hz; high masking ratio (0.7–0.9) critical.
- **[[2307.08927|Cable-Routing]]** — A ==hierarchical imitation learning== system for multi-stage cable routing pairing a low-level vision-based clip-insertion primitive (teleop + explicit ==recovery demonstrations==) with a high-level primitive-sequencing policy; **79%/58%/50%** for 1/2/3-clip routing (vs flat BC's 0% at 3 clips), recovery demos lifting insertion **18%→46%**.
- **[[2507.15833|Look,-Focus,-Act]]** — An active-vision framework (GIAVA) capturing ==synchronized human gaze== with robot demos and using ==foveated ViT tokenization== (dense patches near the gaze point, sparse periphery) with flow-matching policies; **~7×** training / **3×** inference speedup, **5×** less GPU memory, **92%** on ThreadNeedle/PourTestTube with added distractor robustness.
- **[[2507.01857|TypeTele]]** — A hierarchical ==Dexterous Manipulation Type Library== (30 grasp/non-grasp/bimanual types) retrieved by an ==MLLM-assisted== (GPT-4o) module from voice + vision, then human motion is mapped via ==interpolation== between the type's stretched/contracted postures; **95.2%** vs retargeting's **60.6%** collection SR, unlocks 0%-baseline tasks (scissors, kettle).
- **[[2410.18647|Data-Scaling-Laws-Imitation]]** — An empirical study over **40,000+** UMI human demos deriving a ==power-law== between policy generalization and the number of training environments/objects, yielding a recipe (many environments, one object each, ~50 demos per pair); validated to **~90%** SR in novel environments with unseen objects on two new tasks.
- **[[2602.23205|EmbodMocap]]** — A portable ==dual-RGB-D-iPhone 4D capture== system reconstructing in-the-wild human motion + scene geometry into a gravity-aligned world frame via point-tracking/Chamfer/bundle-adjustment + ==World-Space SMPLify==; **~5 cm** human-to-scene alignment (vs >30 cm monocular), lifting embodied 'Support' SR to **66.0%** (vs 20.6%) with sim-to-real transfer.
- **[[2606.27375|ABC]]** — An open end-to-end ==behavior-cloning stack== (hardware, data, training, eval) releasing ==ABC-130K== — 3,553 hours / 130K episodes / 195 tasks of low-cost bimanual teleoperation — plus an ABC-Sim VR-teleop proxy correlating with real eval; ABC-DiT/ABC-VLA solve long-horizon box-folding and card-extraction, with ABC-VLA inference cut to **17.5 ms** from 47.8 ms.
- **[[2606.24884|InSight]]** — A two-stage steerable VLA executing primitive-level commands (a VLM ==auto-segments primitives== from teleop demos), then a VLM-guided ==acquisition loop== that spots missing "primitive gaps," attempts them autonomously, and retrains on successes; **75%** sim block-flip (vs RL's 0%), real "twist-cap" **92%** / "pour-beans" **96%**, **80%** on a 14-primitive task.
- **[[2606.23689|AutoDex]]** — An automated real-world ==dexterous-grasp data-collection== system pairing a grasp-candidate generator with ==20-camera multi-view== perception, collision-monitored execution, post-hoc 6D-pose labeling, and an active ==stable-reorient reset==; **4.8×** higher throughput than teleoperation (500 trajectories in 10.3 h) and **76%** validated success vs sim-screened 34%.
- **[[2606.23371|TSD]]** — A training-free plug-and-play ==Trajectory Saliency Detector== identifying "precise" (fine-manipulation) vs "agile" (dynamic-maneuver) segments via physics-grounded ==spatial entropy + centripetal acceleration== for data compression and targeted expansion; comparable/superior SR with **68.0–74.5%** fewer frames in sim and using only **80.3–84.9%** of frames real.
- **[[2606.22907|ISR]]** — An ==Information-Standardized Trajectory Resampling== method defining an ==information-intensity field== (weighted velocity+acceleration norms) that warps a demo into a Riemannian manifold and resamples at equal information distance; **+24.0pp** π⁰·⁵ (47.8→71.8%) and **+17.5pp** VO-DP over time-uniform 3× downsampling at **28–40%** of points.
- **[[2605.07381|Anchor-Centric]]** — A budget-efficient VLA adaptation strategy escaping the "diversity trap" via a ==Coverage–Density Trade-off==, first ==stabilizing a policy skeleton== with dense demos at core anchors, then ==error-driven boundary mining== expanding to high-risk conditions; **+40.8%** mean SR over diversity-first baselines at N=100, matching N=150 with **3×** fewer demos.
- **[[2604.14089|UMI-3D]]** — A ==Universal Manipulation Interface== extension adding wrist-mounted ==LiDAR + LiDAR-inertial odometry== for drift-resistant metric 3D; curtain-pulling **0.88–0.96** normalized, generalizes to unseen object combos; back-compatible with UMI policies.
- **[[2508.01131|COLLAGE]]** — Retrieves demonstrations from a large prior dataset across four modalities (visual/motion/shape/language), adaptively weighting each subset by the log-likelihood a lightweight reference policy assigns to target demos, biasing few-shot ==BC== sampling; **50.5%** LIBERO-10 (**+11.2%** over best modality), **45.5%** real DROID-augmented (**+57.4%**).
- **[[2601.05844|DexterCap]]** — An affordable (<$6,000) ==13-camera optical mocap== for in-hand manipulation using character-coded checkerboard markers + a 3-stage CornerNet/EdgeNet/BlockNet detection pipeline and MANO reconstruction; **2.06 mm** marker error, **1.42 px** reprojection, rivaling commercial optical systems and yielding the open DexterHand dataset.
- **[[2403.07788|DexCap]]** — A portable ==EMF-glove + SLAM + LiDAR== mocap rig (**0.8 cm** drift, 3× teleop throughput) feeding a ==point-cloud diffusion policy==; **72%** from 30 min of human-only mocap, no on-robot data; HIL correction adds +10%.
- **[[2604.08534|ActiveGlasses]]** — A ==smart-glasses stereo + 6-DoF head capture== system turning bare-hand manipulation into ==object-centric 3D== policies predicting object + head trajectories; beats π0.5 on occluded pouring, zero-shot Flexiv→UR5 deployment via active vision.
- **[[2510.01607|ActiveUMI]]** — A portable ==robot-free human-demonstration== system using gripper-mounted Meta Quest 3 controllers for 6-DoF tracking plus HMD ==active egocentric perception==, learning head-movement gaze control for a third camera arm; **70%** average over 6 bimanual tasks (**+44%** over fixed-head), **56%** in novel environments at **4.0 mm** pose error.
- **[[2506.19121|CUPID]]** — Defines ==Performance Influence==, the derivative of closed-loop return w.r.t. a demo's training weight, decomposed into tractable ==Action Influences== estimated ==REINFORCE-style== from a handful of online rollouts, filtering detrimental or selecting beneficial demos; **+38%** avg real SR, turns a **100%**-failure task into **84–88%** by filtering brittle demos.
- **[[2506.15666|Vision-in-Action]]** — A bimanual system (ViA) learning ==task-relevant active perception== from human demos via a 6-DoF arm as a flexible ==robot neck== + iPhone head camera, with a VR teleop interface that renders an intermediate point cloud to cut motion sickness; **+45%** average task performance under occlusion over fixed chest/wrist cameras.
- **[[2506.05064|DemoSpeedup]]** — A self-supervised proxy policy estimates per-frame ==action entropy== to split precision-required from casual demo segments, then ==HDBSCAN clustering== + piecewise varying-speed downsampling with ==Replicate-Before-Downsample== accelerates demos pre-training; **1.7–3x** faster execution on ACT/DP across 16 tasks, often lifting SR (e.g. **72%→81%**).
- **[[2505.17389|HD-Space]]** — ==Atomic task segmentation== cuts long-horizon tasks into subtasks, then demonstrates from ==end-effector poses uniformly sampled== around each subtask's target point instead of one fixed trajectory, on a ==DINOv2==+==MAE-masking== ACT backbone; **25**+**25** hybrid demos beat **100** naive, **+18–44%** on mobile bowl/spoon grasping.
- **[[2503.24070|HACTS]]** — A low-cost (<$300) kinematically-equivalent leader-follower teleoperation system with ==real-time bilateral joint synchronization== + a foot-pedal copilot override, collecting ==action-correction data== during robot failures; HACTS-ACT **80%** OpenBox vs full-ACT's 60%, UprightMug failure recovery **10%→70%**, RLPD-HACTS **80%** CloseBin in 45 min.
- **[[2503.07771|RoboCopilot]]** — A human-in-the-loop ==interactive imitation== framework with a compliant bilateral-teleoperation device on a mobile bimanual manipulator and an adapted ==HG-DAgger== loop for on-the-fly corrections; **>90%** real industrial part-picking (from 60% offline), mastering a long-horizon kitchen task from 20 demos with 30–50% fewer demos than offline BC.
- **[[2503.07017|How-Train-Your-Robots]]** — An empirical study systematically comparing ==kinesthetic teaching vs VR vs spacemouse teleoperation== across data quality, user experience, and downstream IL SR, then validating a hybrid paradigm; kinesthetic wins low-contact tasks but underperforms on contact-heavy ones, and mixed kinesthetic+VR data lifts policy SR by **~20%** on average.
- **[[2503.03081|AirExo-2]]** — A $600 kinematically-isomorphic exoskeleton with ==demonstration adaptors== (==differentiable rendering== + ==ControlNet-guided Stable Diffusion==) converting in-the-wild human RGB-D into pseudo-robot data, learned by **RISE-2** fusing 3D geometry + ==DINOv2== semantics; **85.0%** zero-shot real SR from pseudo-robot data alone, **1.737mm** action error.
- **[[2411.00704|Look-Around-Enhancing-Teleoperation]]** — A mobile bimanual system adding a ==5-DOF actuated neck== teleoperated via an Apple Vision Pro so demonstrations carry human-like head movements, then training IL policies on the dynamic-viewpoint data; **95%** cup-transfer (vs 0% static camera), **90%** / **82%** on two more tasks, the policy implicitly reasoning when to move the neck.
- **[[2408.11805|ACE-dexterous]]** — A ~$150 cross-platform ==visual-exoskeleton teleoperation== system pairing a 3D-printed 6-DOF wrist exoskeleton with ==MediaPipe webcam hand tracking==, modular across robot platforms and end-effectors; **3 mm** end-effector accuracy, shorter task times and higher SR than GELLO, enabling diverse real manipulation policies.
- **[[2412.10631|ARMADA]]** — A ==robot-free AR data-acquisition== system overlaying a virtual robot on bare hands via Apple Vision Pro, translating tracked hand motion through IK with real-time cues (singularity/workspace/velocity) to keep demos robot-compatible; **+48–85%** replay SR over no-feedback, with implicit-learning gains even after feedback removal.
- **[[2210.06407|Language-Table]]** — An ==Event-Selectable Hindsight Relabeling== method turning raw teleop into a language-annotated corpus for the ==LAVA== policy; **93.5%** over 87,588 short-horizon commands, **85.0%** over 100K long-horizon goals, one operator driving four robots by speech.
- **[[2406.17768|EXTRACT]]** — A ==VLM-clustered offline skill extraction== into a VAE skill space for hierarchical ==SAC==; **10×** sample efficiency over SPiRL on Franka Kitchen, beating BC/SAC across LIBERO and real FurnitureBench; semantically meaningful skills.
- **[[2509.22149|DemoGrasp]]** — A method amortizing a ==single grasp demonstration== into universal dexterous grasping by editing EEF + delta-joint params as a ==single-step MDP RL== action; **86.5%** on 110 unseen real objects (71.1% small / 68.3% thin), cross-embodiment across multi-finger and parallel grippers.
- **[[2602.09973|RoboInter]]** — A holistic ==intermediate-representation== suite: a densely per-frame-annotated dataset (10+ IR categories), a ==RoboInter-VQA== embodied-reasoning benchmark, and a ==plan-then-execute VLA== (VLM planner + VLA executor); RoboInter-Qwen-3B **+49.1%** over RoboBrain2.0 on RoboRef-It, real closed-loop **77.3%** vs 65.0% with smaller OOD drop.
- **[[2410.22325|Robots-Pre-train-Robots]]** — A ==Manipulation-Centric Representation== pretraining framework on large-scale robot datasets (DROID) adding ==dynamics-alignment== + ==action-prediction== losses to temporal contrastive learning; **+14.8%** average over 20 sim tasks and **+76.9%** real over 3 UR5e tasks, with manipulation-centricity correlating R=0.93 with downstream SR.
- **[[2403.19622|RH20T-P]]** — A ==primitive-level== robotic dataset of ~38K clips annotated with primitive skills + ==spatial information== (destination/direction/trajectory), standardizing a ==plan-execute== paradigm with the RA-P CGA baseline; **80%** novel-scene / **70%** novel-object / **67.5%** novel-skill OOD execution vs VLM-only planners' 12.5–35%.
- **[[2312.02352|Working-Backwards-to]]** — A self-supervised data-collection pipeline (Placing via Picking) reversing successful pick trajectories into placement demonstrations, using ==Compliant Control for Grasping== + ==Tactile Regrasping== for uninterrupted collection and VLM-driven grasp planning; **87.78%** dish-rack SR, **+20%** over kinesthetic-teaching-trained policies.
- **[[2202.10448|Robotic Telekinesis]]** — A single uncalibrated-RGB-camera teleoperation pipeline retargeting ==FrankaMocap== hand pose to ==Allegro hand== joints via a differentiable keyvector-similarity ==MLP retargeter== trained on internet hand video, with adversarial ==self-collision== avoidance; beat a vision-based baseline on **7/10** tasks (**90% vs 70%** Pickup-Dice).
- **[[2109.13396|Bridge]]** — A cross-domain ==multi-task multi-domain dataset== (7,200 demos, 71 tasks, 11 kitchens) with ==task-conditioned behavioral cloning== + a data-rebalancing joint-training strategy; **66%** vs 28% on shared tasks, **44%** zero-shot skill import, and **2×** (50% vs 22%) on entirely new tasks; the cross-domain bridge-data origin.
- **[[2003.05212|Transteleop]]** — A mobile hand-arm ==teleoperation== system pairing an auto-encoder (==image-to-image translation== of human depth → robot joint angles) for dexterous hand control with IMU-based arm tracking and a wearable camera for unrestricted workspace; **+10.63%** joint-angle accuracy over TeachNet, **100%** pick-place and cup-insertion SR.

#### 7.2 Generative & Synthetic Data Pipelines

Sidestep human collection entirely — billion-frame synthetic datasets, LLM-driven scene and demonstration generation, and digital-twin simulation.

- **[[2608.01452|DynamicManip]]** — A ==static-to-dynamic data-augmentation== pipeline synthesizing dynamic-manipulation episodes via keyframe extraction + ==phase-operator== trajectory editing, paired with a dynamic-aware adaptive IL policy that adjusts inference frequency; matches policies trained on **50–200** demos, **−32.9%** latency and **+18.4pp** SR over a 3D-Diffusion-Policy baseline.
- **[[2606.30749|G2D-Pretrain]]** — A 355K-trajectory dataset augmenting the ==Dexonomy grasp synthesis dataset== for low-level policy pretraining, paired with a hierarchical policy (16-keypoint ==sub-goal predictor== + goal-conditioned ==3D Diffusion Policy== with ==Act3D-style cross-attention==) on the new **DexCraft** benchmark; **+33.3pp** real full-task SR over an end-to-end DP3 baseline.
- **[[2606.24078|MinInter]]** — A ==trajectory-selection== method for demo augmentation quantifying the interpolation needed to recompose an expert trajectory's segments for a new initial condition, picking the source needing minimal interpolation to keep expert fidelity; **+12.8%** data-generation SR over 26 variants and **+4.8%** (up to +20.7%) policy SR, beating SkillGen.
- **[[2606.19586|1001 DEMOS]]** — A ==fisheye 3D Gaussian Splatting== reconstruction (==KB8== ray sampler) paired with ==trajectory optimization== (funnel-convergence, ==TSDF== collision, render, smoothness losses) generates thousands of collision-free action-view episodes from one demo; real obstacle-avoidance **100%** vs **10%/5%** baselines.
- **[[2606.17040|R2RDreamer]]** — A real-to-real augmentation pairing ==lightweight 3D co-editing== of object point clouds + EEF poses with ==occlusion-aware projection== and a projection-controlled video-completion model that fills masked regions; lifts single-source-demo SR from **3.13%** to **21.9–40.6%** (DP) and **up to 46.9%** (π₀), matching policies trained on 5–15 human demos.
- **[[2606.13677|MANA]]** — A dexterous articulated-tool framework reinterpreting manipulation as a ==coarse-to-fine animation== data-generation problem (collision-aware grasp keyframes + GPU RRT-Connect + RL for in-hand actuation) training a point-cloud diffusion policy under heavy ==force randomization==; **~70%** real grasp/in-hand across tongs/pliers/syringes, beating teleoperation (<30%).
- **[[2604.15569|ShapeGen]]** — Trains ==spatial-warping== networks supervised by ==signed-distance-function== equality into a template-anchored ==Shape Library== of functional correspondences, then substitutes novel objects into a demo via per-keypoint ==SE(3)== alignment + ==gripper-action correction==; hard mug-hanging SR **1/20→10/20**.
- **[[2604.10579|AffordGen]]** — A method repurposing ==semantic affordance correspondence== as a generative prior to synthesize diverse 6D trajectories from minimal demos, establishing ==3D keypoint correspondence== via DINOv2 + canonicalization then keypoint-constrained replay; **+24.1%** over CPGen on unseen objects, the only method with non-zero cross-category SR (14/27 real mug pouring).
- **[[2603.26757|RoboNVS]]** — Fuses ==DepthCrafter== relative + ==DA3== metric depth into a watertight ==DW-Mesh==, then completes rendered occlusions with a ==LoRA==-tuned ==Wan2.1-14B== ==flow-matching== video model under ==Bi-directional Masking==; beats EX-4D on PSNR **14.07** vs **12.72**, real Lego pick-place **65%** vs monocular's **5%**.
- **[[2603.04338|ArtHOI]]** — Reconstructs ==4D human-object interaction== by treating a text-to-video diffusion clip as inverse-rendering supervision, ==flow-segmented part Gaussians== + decoupled object-articulation/SMPL-X stages; **6.71°** rotation error (73% below D3D-HOI), **75.64%** contact; zero-shot data source for manipulation.
- **[[2602.05233|MobileManiBench]]** — A simulation-first mobile-manipulation framework (Isaac Sim) auto-generating **150K** multi-modal trajectories from ==state-based RL policies== over 630 objects / 5 skills for two embodiments, for VLA training; MobileManiVLA reaches **56.7%/57.3%** on unseen objects/scenes, richer sensory inputs aiding generalization.
- **[[2601.05241|RoboVIP]]** — ==Action-guided segmentation== (gripper-state windows + ==SAM2==) masks robot/objects for a ==LoRA==-tuned ==Wan2.1-I2V== multi-view inpainting model conditioned on ==visual identity prompting== (curated exemplar crops); FID **39.97** vs Cosmos-Transfer2.5's **47.43**, real cluttered stacking **9/10** vs **0/10** unaugmented.
- **[[2511.16223|DynaMimicGen]]** — A data-generation framework (D-MG) parsing minimal demos into ==object-centric subtask segments==, generalizing each via ==Dynamic Movement Primitives== with continuous ==object-pose monitoring== and structured perturbations for dynamic (non-quasi-static) tasks; matches a 10-demo baseline from **1–2** demos, **97.33%** sim Square / **96.0%** real Lift.
- **[[2511.01294|Kinematify]]** — An open-vocabulary pipeline synthesizing high-DoF ==articulated-object URDFs== from static RGB/text via ==part-aware SDF meshes==, ==MCTS kinematic-topology== inference, and ==Distance-Weighted Contact-Aware Virtual Linkage== joint optimization; **2.92°** axis error on everyday objects, **1.32** Tree-Edit-Distance on robots, deployable for drawer/pouring real.
- **[[2510.20774|FieldGen]]** — A semi-automated real-world data engine decoupling a human-supervised fine-manipulation phase from an automated ==Pre-Manipulation Field== phase (cone position + spherical orientation fields from a few teleop demos) + reward annotations for sub-optimal behaviors; **+35.5–45.8%** SR over teleop-trained policies at equal time, **2.47×** higher active-collection ratio.
- **[[2510.18316|MoMaGen]]** — A framework formulating bimanual mobile-manipulation data generation as ==constrained optimization== with hard (reachability, object visibility during manipulation) and soft constraints, generating from a single demo via IK filtering + cuRobo planning; **63%** D0 generation SR with double the visibility ratio, **60%** real Pick-Cup after sim-pretrain.
- **[[2510.08547|R2RGen]]** — A simulator-free, rendering-free real-to-real pipeline: template tracking completes point clouds, ==group-wise backtracking== augmentation transforms object groups/environment/robot base preserving causal constraints, then ==camera-aware 3D post-processing== restores sensor realism; **40.3%** avg SR from one demo across 8 tasks (vs single-source's 3.4%).
- **[[2510.05684|D2E]]** — A ==vision-action pretraining== framework scaling on desktop/gaming data: an OWA Toolkit captures interactions and a ==Generalist-IDM== pseudo-labels YouTube gameplay to pretrain a 1B VAPT model for embodied transfer; **96.6%** LIBERO long-horizon matching **7×**-larger models, **80%** real pick-place, **+8pp** CANVAS navigation.
- **[[2509.05314|ManipDreamer3D]]** — A pipeline reconstructing a ==64³ 3D occupancy map== from one image, planning ==collision-free 3D trajectories== (A* + CHOMP-inspired optimization), then a parameter-free ==trajectory-guided latent-editing== video synthesizer; **93.98** FVD and low EEF/object trajectory error, generating physically-plausible manipulation video for policy training.
- **[[2509.19454|ROPA]]** — An offline RGB-D bimanual augmentation method using a ==ControlNet pose-guided diffusion model== conditioned on rendered ==skeleton poses== with IK-derived targets + a ==constrained-optimization action labeler== for contact-rich feasibility; **80%** Coordinated-Lift-Ball with 4 cameras, improving real Lift/Push SR, skeleton cues beating raw joints.
- **[[2505.09601|R2R2R]]** — A ==Real2Render2Real== pipeline generating robot training data from smartphone scans + demo videos via ==Gaussian Splatting== assets, trajectory interpolation, and parallelized rendering, with no dynamics sim or robot hardware; **51 vs 1.7** demos/minute (30× over teleop) while matching teleoperation-trained policy SR.
- **[[2505.04860|D-CODA]]** — An offline eye-in-hand bimanual augmentation method (D-CODA) synthesizing ==viewpoint-consistent wrist-camera images== for both arms via a conditional diffusion model + ==camera-perturbation sampling== with SAM2 contact decomposition and IK action labels; wins 4/5 sim tasks (**77.3%** Lift-Ball), 14/20 real Lift-Drawer vs 0/20.
- **[[2505.03233|SynGrasp-1B]]** — The first ==billion-frame photorealistic synthetic grasping dataset== with heavy DR, training ==GraspVLA== via Progressive Action Generation co-trained on web semantics; **~90%** zero-shot real grasp across 5 test sets, **93.3%** language-conditioned matching AnyGrasp.
- **[[2507.00833|HumanoidGen]]** — An ==LLM code-form planning== pipeline with ==MCTS + Segment-Truncate-Combine-Resume== for bimanual dexterous data generation; **>50%** over 20 tasks, MCTS lifts reasoning success up to **55%**, diffusion policies trained on HGen-Bench show few-shot scaling.
- **[[2508.17547|LodeStar]]** — VLM-driven automatic ==skill segmentation== (DIFT+Co-Tracker keypoints) feeding ==real-to-sim== state augmentation + ==residual RL== atop BC skill policies, composed by a ==Skill Routing Transformer== for hand-off-free long-horizon chaining; **+25%** real SR over the best data-generation baseline, **70%** fewer demos than real-only.
- **[[2506.22756|RoboPearls]]** — An editable video-simulation framework reconstructing demos as ==4D Gaussian Splatting==, exposing ==editable simulation operators== (insert/remove/recolor + physics) driven by LLM agents and a ==VLM failure-analysis loop== for self-improvement; **+17.5%** over RVT on COLOSSEUM, **+5.6%** on RLBench, 14/20 vs RDT's 4/20 real pick-unseen.
- **[[2506.17198|Dex1B]]** — A ==billion-demonstration dexterous-manipulation dataset== built by an iterative pipeline pairing optimization-seeded data with a ==geometry-constrained CVAE (DexSimple)== + debiased sampling; **86.0%** DexGraspNet SR (new SOTA), **96%** direct sim-to-real on unseen objects across 6,000+ objects and 3 hands.
- **[[2410.18907|SkillMimicGen]]** — An automated demo-generation system segmenting human demos into contact-rich ==skill== vs free-space ==motion== segments, geometrically adapting skills to new scenes and ==motion-planning== collision-free connectors, sim-validated; **65%** vs MimicGen's 14% generation SR on Coffee, up to **95%** policy SR at 5K demos, zero-shot sim-to-real.
- **[[2408.14368|GR-MG]]** — A method leveraging partial annotations via ==progress-guided goal-image generation== (InstructPix2Pix) + a multimodal goal-conditioned policy; CALVIN 5-task SR **41.2%→64.4%**, real **44.4%→60.6%**, **17.5%** few-shot novel skills from 10 demos.
- **[[2406.18915|Manipulate-Anything]]** — A modular VLM/LLM framework auto-generating robot demonstrations via multi-viewpoint action generation + sub-task verification with error-recovery re-planning, no privileged state; **+22%** zero-shot SR over baselines on all 14 RLBench tasks, BC policies within **0.27%** of human data.
- **[[2504.13175|Novel-Demonstration-Generation-Gaussian]]** — A one-shot augmentation reconstructing a manipulation scene as ==3D Gaussian Splatting==, then generating novel demonstrations via ==Gaussian manipulation + frame alignment== for behavior cloning; **87.8%** one-shot SR vs 57.2% for traditional methods, robust to object pose/type/view/lighting at **29×** faster than manual collection.
- **[[2503.18738|RoboEngine]]** — A plug-and-play ==data-augmentation toolkit== pairing wire-level ==Robo-SAM== robot segmentation with a fine-tuned ==Background-Diffusion== generating physically plausible backgrounds; **0.9037** OOD GIoU and a **210%** average behavior-score/SR gain (60.9% avg) across six novel real scenes, beating other augmentations by 20%.
- **[[2503.05887|MatchMaker]]** — An automated assembly-asset generator casting mating-part creation as ==shape completion==: a VLM extracts contact surfaces/assembly axes, a ==3D generative latent-diffusion model (BrepGen)== synthesizes the counterpart under fixed geometric constraints + clearance control; **0.43** diversity (vs AutoMate 0.19), <1 min/asset, **82%** real assembly across 5 tasks.
- **[[2503.03045|ArticuBot]]** — A universal articulated-object policy trained on **42.3K** auto-generated sim trajectories over 322 objects via a ==hierarchical weighted-displacement high-level + 3D-diffusion low-level== pair on point clouds; **0.63** Franka / **0.54** X-Arm zero-shot on 20 unseen objects where OpenVLA opened none.
- **[[2504.06961|Two-by-Two]]** — A multi-task pairwise-assembly dataset (2BY2) of annotated 3D object pairs over ==18 fine-grained daily-assembly tasks== with semantic/functional relationships, plus a two-step ==SE(3)-equivariant== network predicting base + fitting-object poses; SOTA across all 18 tasks with strong cross-scenario generalization, validated on a real UR5.
- **[[2406.20095|LLaRA]]** — A recipe casting robot policy as ==visuo-textual conversations== using image pixel coordinates, auto-generating ==Instruct-BC== conversational datasets from existing trajectories plus six self-supervised auxiliary tasks; **90.0%** VIMA-Bench L1 (beating VIMA) at only **12%** of its data, **75%** real distractor tasks where RT-2-style baselines fail.
- **[[2502.16932|DemoGen]]** — A synthetic demo-generation method amplifying a single example into hundreds by decomposing the trajectory into ==skill segments (transformed)== and ==motion segments (replanned)== with matching 3D point-cloud observations for new configurations; **>80%** SR across spatial configs from only 3 demos vs ~30 for baselines, across bimanual/insertion/assembly platforms.
- **[[2605.26638|HyperSim]]** — A ==constraint-aware scene synthesis + 3DGS backgrounds + adversarial trajectory generation== pipeline with sim-and-real co-training; **75%** zero-shot and **95%** with 35 real demos for π₀ on deep-bin transfer, **+35%** robustness from adversarial trajectories.
- **[[2512.09297|BiDemoSyn]]** — A one-shot synthesizer turning a single real bimanual example into thousands of contact-rich demos via ==invariant-primitive/variable-block decomposition==, ==initial-frame alignment==, and ==collision-aware validation==; **~5 s** per sample, **86.7%/66.7%** in-distribution/OOD with EquiBot over 6 tasks, zero-shot cross-embodiment.
- **[[2512.02729|RoboWheel]]** — A ==data engine== reconstructing human HOI video into robot-executable supervision via physics optimization + ==cross-embodiment retargeting== + a sim-augmented flywheel; **0.92 cm/s²** hand jitter, **91.7%** real UR5 retarget, HOI-trained policies matching teleop (47.5% vs 35.0% hard).
- **[[2509.23829|DexFlyWheel]]** — A self-improving ==data flywheel== seeded by one teleop demo, iterating ==IL + residual RL== with progressive augmentation; expands one seed to ~2,040 configs and lifts generalization **16.5→81.9%**, beating DexMimicGen (45.2% with 10× more human data).
- **[[2509.22578|EgoDemoGen]]** — An ego viewpoint-generalization framework that ==EgoTrajTransfer==-projects robot action trajectories to novel ego viewpoints (IK-filtered) and ==EgoViewTransfer== synthesizes matching RGB via ==conditional video diffusion== with ==double reprojection==; **+16.9%** sim and **+23.0%** real SR on novel viewpoints — aligns observation *and* action frame together.
- **[[2310.17596|MimicGen]]** — A data-generation engine using ==object-centric subtask parsing== of ~**200** human demos, transformed and replayed across new object poses to auto-synthesize **50,000+** demonstrations over **18** tasks (**250×** multiplication); agents trained on it match or beat human-only data — the engine [[2406.02523|RoboCasa]] builds on.

#### 7.3 Cross-Embodiment & Human-Video Transfer

Learn once, deploy anywhere — retarget human hands to robot grippers, align heterogeneous action spaces, and transfer skills across robot morphologies.

- **[[2608.04196|SiMDex]]** — A ==unified morphology-agnostic== wrist-local-fingertip representation feeding a three-stage ==similarity-based data mining== pipeline (Recall/Ranking/Re-ranking) that retrieves task-relevant human demonstrations for ==masked-supervision== co-training; **47.7%→61.1%** SR (**+13.4pp**), **~4×** fewer robot-demo hours (25h→6h).
- **[[2606.29028|Keypose Exploration]]** — An automatic pipeline fusing ==VLM semantic event detection== with trajectory analysis to extract keyposes (via a ==Consistency Model==) conditioning a ==Diffusion Policy==, filtered by a ==kinematic reachability map== for zero-shot cross-embodiment transfer; Franka→UR5e Square SR **73.8%→82.9%**, variance cut 10.3%→4.4%.
- **[[2606.28813|Human2Any]]** — A human-to-robot transfer framework learning embodiment-agnostic ==object-object interaction priors== from human video via ==conditional diffusion==, composed at test time with robot-specific ==agent-object grasping priors== through ==constraint-aware particle filtering==; **80%** real Franka SR with zero robot demos, transferring to an RBY-1 humanoid.
- **[[2606.26095|Action-Priors]]** — A two-stage cross-embodiment VLA giving the action head an explicit ==motion prior==: a decoupled stage trains a flow-matching encoder-decoder on unconditioned robot trajectories, then reuses the decoder as the action head with a latent ==alignment-distillation== loss; **55.3→68.0%** over 13 tasks (beating GR00T 48.6% / π0.5 53.8%), **35.0→66.3%** real Franka.
- **[[2606.24049|SPACE-Cross-Robot]]** — A cross-robot generalist predicting controller-independent ==Cartesian state deltas== instead of robot-specific commands, paired with a lightweight ==Action Adapter== (offline-calibrated, online LMS-updated) translating deltas to per-robot commands; **+30%** co-training SR and **92%** zero-shot UR5→FR3 transfer, holding **84%** on unseen units (vs 18%).
- **[[2606.22836|Cloak]]** — A zero-shot cross-embodiment method dynamically ==masking the end-effector== in wrist-camera inputs via geometric rendering from proprioception, with shape-varying mask augmentations and a ==masked-attention== vision encoder plus tip-pose retargeting; **81.8%** on an unseen 5-finger Sharpa hand (vs baselines' 54.4–60.2%), **85.1%/86.3%** on UMI/YAM near 88.0% source.
- **[[2606.21672|GLAM]]** — A Grounded Latent-Action World Model unifying heterogeneous demos (handheld, sim, human video) into a ==shared source-invariant latent action space== via coupled generative models + an ==asymmetric KL alignment== so a frozen IDM relabels all data for BC; **+35%** avg real (up to **+69%** bimanual stacking), matching baselines with **5×** less target-robot data.
- **[[2606.16436|V2P-Manip]]** — A framework learning dexterous manipulation from unconstrained ==monocular human videos== via a ==perception-refinement-synthesis== pipeline (geometric alignment then physics consistency via Task-Wrench-Space optimization) feeding ==residual RL==; **0.483** OakInk SR, **79.3%** with both refinement stages vs 0% without geometry, transferring to 5 robot hands.
- **[[2606.16272|TopoRetarget]]** — An interaction-preserving retargeting casting human hand-object demos to robot references via ==topology-aware Laplacian optimization== over a reused ==Delaunay interaction-edge set==, enforcing kinematic feasibility and penetration handling; **7.71 mm** position displacement, **87.5%** downstream RL SR on pen-spinning (+40pp), zero-shot to a real Wuji Hand.
