- **[[2606.10614|Dexterous-Point-Policy]]** — A point-based dexterous policy learned solely from human video via a unified ==3D keypoint== (wrist + 5 fingertips) abstraction, an ==autoregressive transformer== pretrained on internet-scale VITRA, plus ==contact-point prediction== injecting closing forces; **75.0%** over 8 real tasks vs baselines' 1.0–3.7%, **76.7%** novel objects.
- **[[2606.10025|GHOST]]** — A hierarchical policy factoring control into a ==high-level 3D end-effector sub-goal== predictor over an embodiment-agnostic keypoint space (trainable on human video) + a ==2D-heatmap-conditioned low-level Diffusion Policy==; **80.0%** fold-onesie vs flat DP's 10.0%, **63.3%** novel-object mug-on-table vs 13.3%, with cross-embodiment transfer.
- **[[2606.06627|TriHands]]** — An everyday-human-video cotraining study contributing the ==TriHands== dataset (28 h multi-view triangulated 3D hand poses) with ==conditional flow-matching== cotraining + ==embodiment-specific visual/action experts==; **+29.7%** average absolute SR over robot-only training in low-robot-data settings, resolving 100% of local errors on 5/6 tasks.
- **[[2508.20085|HERMES]]** — Unified RL converting heterogeneous human motion (teleop/mocap/video) into mobile bimanual dexterous behaviors via one-shot augmentation + residual actions, DAgger-distilled into a depth-image sim2real policy, plus a navigation model augmented with closed-loop ==PnP localization==; **67.8%** real SR (**+54.5%**), PnP cutting translation error **18cm→2.4cm**.
- **[[2603.27012|UMI-Underwater]]** — Pairs an autonomous ==self-supervised underwater data-collection== pipeline with a zero-shot ==depth-based affordance predictor== pretrained on land demos via a portable **UMI-Aquatic** interface, guiding an ==affordance-conditioned diffusion policy==; **85%** underwater grasping (vs RGB-only's 65%), **80%** OOD backgrounds where RGB collapses to **0%**.
- **[[2603.22264|UniDex]]** — A ==kinematic+visual retargeting== of egocentric human video into a 3D VLA over a ==Function Actuator Aligned Space==; **81%** real tool-use progress, **60%/40%** zero-shot to unseen Oymotion/Wuji hands, **5.2×** cheaper data via human-robot co-training.
- **[[2603.00182|Morphology-Aware-Transformer]]** — A cross-robot policy embedding morphology via ==Kinematic Tokens== (per-joint action factorization), a ==Mix-Mask topology-aware attention bias== (1-hop kinematic masking alternating with full attention), and ==FiLM== per-joint attributes; **47.4%** DROID SR (vs π0.5's 19.7%), consistent multi-embodiment Macro-SR gains over DROID+SO101.
- **[[2602.10105|DexImit]]** — A four-stage pipeline generating ==bimanual dexterous manipulation== data from monocular human videos: ==4D hand-object trajectory reconstruction==, an ==action-centric scheduling algorithm==, ==force-closure-based grasp synthesis==, and scale/pose/camera augmentation; **82%** reconstruction SR, beats RigVid/DexMan on 6 sim tasks and zero-shot to real robots.
- **[[2602.13764|MOTIF]]** — A three-stage framework decoupling embodiment-agnostic ==spatiotemporal action motifs== (VQ-VAE over canonicalized EEF trajectories with progress-aware + adversarial losses) from a ==motif-conditioned flow-matching DiT== generating robot-specific actions; **+6.5%** sim few-shot transfer and **+43.7%** real 5-shot transfer on ARX5/Piper over baselines.
- **[[2601.12993|Being-H0.5]]** — A foundational VLA using ==human-centric learning== and a ==Unified Action Space== with a Mixture-of-Flow architecture, trained on the 35K-hour UniHand-2.0 for cross-embodiment generalization; **98.9%** LIBERO and **53.9%** RoboCasa from low-res RGB, deployed across five real platforms with embodiment-level zero-shot transfer.
- **[[2511.09484|SPIDER]]** — A ==physics-informed dexterous retargeting== casting kinematic human demos into dynamically feasible robot trajectories via a sampling optimizer with ==virtual contact guidance==; **2.4M** frames over 5 hands + 4 humanoids, up to **100%** task SR at order-of-magnitude faster generation than RL.
- **[[2506.20668|DemoDiffusion]]** — A one-shot human-imitation method ==kinematically retargeting== a single human-hand demonstration into an open-loop end-effector trajectory, then refining it via a pre-trained generalist ==diffusion policy== from an intermediate noise level; **83.8%** over 8 real tasks vs direct deployment's 13.8% and open-loop retargeting's 52.5%.
- **[[2502.11744|FUNCTO]]** — A ==Function-Centric One-Shot Imitation== method establishing tool correspondence via ==3D functional keypoints== (grasp/center/function points) extracted from a single human video with Grounding-SAM + VLMs, transferred to novel tools via coarse VLM proposals + dense correspondence; **79.5%** novel-tool SR vs DINOBOT's 57.5%, beating ACT/DP trained on 50 demos.
- **[[2604.15215|HiST-AT]]** — A ==hierarchical spatiotemporal action tokenizer== with two VQ codebooks and ==Lipschitz-regularized== latents for in-context IL; **59%** RoboCasa (+6% over prior tokenizer), **62.5%** cross-dataset transfer and **11.4%** zero-shot vs LipVQ-VAE's 5.2%.
- **[[2605.05756|MaMi-HOI]]** — A ==dual-adapter diffusion== (geometry-aware proximity + kinematic-harmony) reconciling global motion fluidity with contact precision for human-object-interaction generation; **−50%** trajectory endpoint error and **+6.02pp** downstream task SR.
- **[[2604.10836|HO-Flow]]** — A generalizable ==hand-object interaction generation== model with an ==Interaction-aware VAE== over hand-centric object point clouds + a ==masked autoregressive transformer== with ==flow-matching==; **98.25%** physical plausibility + **~3×** semantic diversity on GRAB, **89.76%** plausibility OOD on OakInk — kinematic-aware HOI priors for human-to-robot transfer.
- **[[2604.06778|RichMap]]** — A GPU-accelerated ==grid-based reachability map== over SO(3) with geodesic capacity bounds; **−25–35%** false-positive rate, microsecond queries, and **+10.97%** absolute cross-embodiment diffusion-policy transfer via similarity-guided energy landscapes.
- **[[2506.14608|Latent-Action-Diffusion]]** — A method building a ==semantically-aligned latent action space== via contrastive learning over retargeting-paired human/robot actions, then a ==diffusion policy in the frozen latent space== controlling heterogeneous end-effectors; **+25.3%** (avg **+13.4%**) SR from cross-embodiment co-training, one policy driving dexterous hands and grippers.
- **[[2503.21860|ManipTrans]]** — A two-stage dexterous bimanual transfer pairing ==pre-trained trajectory imitation== with ==residual learning== under a physical-constraint curriculum, transferring human hand-object motions to multiple robot hands; generates the **3.3K**-episode (1.34M-frame) DexManipNet, transferring bottle-unscrewing across hand types at higher SR and motion fidelity.
- **[[2503.00774|SHADOW]]** — A zero-shot ==cross-embodiment policy transfer== method masking the active robot's pixels and overlaying the *other* robot's ==segmentation mask== at the matching end-effector pose, aligning visual input distributions without in-painting artifacts; matches source-robot SR on 5/6 sim tasks and statistically beats Mirage in sim and real.
- **[[2502.15613|GADP]]** — A ==gripper-aware diffusion policy== (GADP) for zero-shot transfer across grippers, using a ==gripper-invariant grasp-probability map== (GG-CNN) + ==differentiable gripper mapping== of TCP offsets and a QP safety-projection in DDIM denoising; **93.3%** seen / **70.0%** unseen-object pick-place across 6 grippers, **+60pp** over DP/DP3.
- **[[2407.15002|GET]]** — A ==Graph Embodiment Transformer== (GET-Zero) encoding the robot's kinematic graph into self-attention, distilled from per-embodiment experts with a ==forward-kinematics self-modeling loss==; **+20%** zero-shot generalization to new graph and link-length variations on dexterous tasks, transferring to a physical LEAP hand at **0.82 mm** FK error.
- **[[2411.04005|Object-Centric]]** — A hierarchical dexterous framework from ==human motion data==: a Transformer planner generates 6-DoF wrist poses, an ==RL low-level controller== learns fingers + residual wrist, refined by a ==Data Augmentation Loop==; **>50%** completion gain over direct human-motion mapping, **+28%** unseen objects, sim-to-real on a bimanual Shadow Hand.
- **[[2403.15203|DITTO (Trajectory Transformation)]]** — A two-stage pipeline extracting an ==object-pose-centric== trajectory from a single human RGB-D video offline, then warping it via online ==In-Situ Trajectory Generation== (RAFT/LoFTR correspondence + SVD/RANSAC pose estimation) for one-shot skill transfer; **79%** real Franka SR across 10 tasks from a single human demo.
- **[[2402.19249|Mirage-XPolicy]]** — A zero-shot policy-transfer method visually adapting observations by ==cross-painting== (mask target robot, inpaint background, overlay rendered source robot at the target EEF pose) + a ==blocking controller== aligning action spaces; **30–90%** real SR transferring Franka→unseen UR5, beating generalist Octo zero-shot.
- **[[2511.04671|X-Diffusion]]** — A framework unifying human/robot action spaces via retargeting + a ==noised human-robot action classifier== finding each demo's ==minimum indistinguishability step (k*)==, applying the ==Diffusion Policy== denoising loss to human data only beyond k*; **+16%** avg SR over cross-embodiment baselines across 5 real tasks, beating manual filtering.
- **[[2307.09955|XSkill]]** — A ==Self-supervised skill-prototype discovery== from unlabeled, unaligned human and robot videos that builds a ==cross-embodiment skill space==, then a ==skill-conditioned diffusion policy== translates human prompts to robot execution; **70.2%** sim / **60%** real on unseen four-subtask compositions — skills aligned across embodiments without paired data.
- **[[1910.11215|RoboNet]]** — A **162K**-trajectory, 7-platform multi-robot dataset with ==Visual Foresight== video-prediction MPC, probing cross-robot transfer; pretraining + 300–400 trajectories lifts new-robot SR (Kuka **10→40%**), and diverse viewpoints improve zero-shot generalization — the multi-robot pretraining-data origin.

#### 7.4 Cross-Embodiment Dexterous Grasp & Hand Generalization

The transfer problem specialized to grasping: one grasp model that generalizes across radically different multi-fingered hands. These encode hand morphology explicitly — eigengrasps, canonical URDFs, kinematic graphs, interaction-centric distance fields — so a single model controls heterogeneous and even unseen hands.

- **[[2607.20207|SeededGrasp]]** — Decouples ==VLM== semantic seed-point prediction from a ==flow-matching== multi-embodiment grasp generator (==GCN== point-cloud encoding, learnable gripper queries, ==DiT== sampling) trained on **2.56M** synthetic grasps; **+35%** over Geomatch in clutter, **72.97%** with Gemini-predicted seeds, **78%** real on an unseen Delto gripper.
- **[[2607.11031|GraspGraphNet]]** — Represents hands as ==topology-aware graphs== read from ==URDF==, generating palm pose + joint configuration by ==conditional flow matching== with ==dynamic world-edge message passing== rebuilt each step; **83.48%** across Barrett/Allegro/Shadow, **40 ms** vs DRO-Grasp's **569 ms**, **72.70%** on finger-ablated hands.
- **[[2606.18092|EAGG]]** — A time-conditioned generative model predicting a compact grasp state, aligning embodiment via an ==embodiment-aligned PCA control basis== + a ==URDF topology-aware graph== + ==Iterative Geometry Injection==; **56.17%** unified average over 6 hands (within 1.10pp of specialists), **33.25%** zero-shot to unseen end effectors, 89–95% real on 3 platforms.
- **[[2603.16806|DexGrasp-Zero]]** — A zero-shot cross-embodiment grasping policy over a ==morphology-aligned graph== (MAGCN with ==layer-wise URDF-property injection==) in a ==hand-agnostic motion-primitive space==; **85.0%** sim zero-shot on 2 unseen hands (**+59.5pp** over CrossDex), **82%** real across 3 platforms on 10 unseen objects.
- **[[2603.14522|One-Policy-Fits-All]]** — A single visuomotor policy over a ==Geometry-Aware Latent Representation== (KPConv multi-scale + geometric transformer encoding of the end-effector point cloud) feeding a ==unified universal-hand-model decoder==; **+19.8%** spatial-generalization SR over naive co-training across 9 end effectors, XHand surpassing **80%** with only 8 new trajectories.
- **[[2602.16712|Canonical-Hand]]** — A ==parameterized canonical representation== unifying diverse hands into a ==shared parameter space and canonical URDF== (≤5 fingers, 22 DoF, capsule primitives) with automated bidirectional conversion; a single grasp model reaches up to **71%** real zero-shot on unseen LEAP-Hand variants, a VAE learning a smooth 16-D morphology latent.
- **[[2602.08278|DexFormer]]** — A ==history-conditioned Transformer== for cross-embodied dexterous manipulation implicitly inferring hand morphology from past observation-action pairs over a ==shared canonical finger action space==, trained with morphology randomization; **77.5%** grasping across canonical + 32 unseen hands vs LSTM's 58.72%, transfers to a real LEAP hand with removed joints.
- **[[2602.00915|UniMorphGrasp]]** — A diffusion grasp model conditioning on a ==Graphormer morphology encoder== of the hand's kinematic graph with a ==canonical human-like pose mapping== and a kinematic-influence-weighted hierarchical loss; **94.0%** SR on MultiDex (**+4.9%**), **87.4%/91.3%** zero-shot on unseen datasets, **91%** real on a Leap Hand.
- **[[2510.06068|MachaGrasp]]** — An end-to-end model predicting articulation coefficients in a low-dimensional ==eigengrasp space== via a ==URDF Morphology Encoder== and a ==Kinematic-Aware Articulation Loss== weighting joints by fingertip influence; **91.9%** sim over 28 unseen objects across 3 hands at **0.353 s**/grasp, **87%** real on an unseen Robotiq 3-finger hand after few-shot.
- **[[2509.24661|CEDex]]** — A cross-embodiment dexterous grasp generator pairing a CVAE over object-centric ==human contact representations== with ==Kinematic Human Contact Alignment== (SDF optimization adapting contacts to diverse hands) + physics-aware penetration-repulsion constraints; builds the largest such dataset (**20M** grasps, 4 grippers), **88.7%** SR, lifting DRO-Grasp **+3.5%**.
- **[[2506.02489|Grasp2Grasp]]** — A grasp-translation method framing cross-morphology transfer as a ==Schrödinger Bridge== over unpaired grasp distributions, learned in a VAE latent with ==physics-informed Optimal Transport costs== (contact-map, grasp-wrench-hull); **67.45%** SR and **14.63%** 6D wrench-hull IoU preserving functional intent at **0.8 s**/grasp, no paired data.
- **[[2502.16420|AnyDexGrasp]]** — A two-stage framework separating a ==universal contact-centric grasp representation== (hand-agnostic) from a ==hand-specific decision model== learned by focused real trial-and-error; **75–95%** SR across 3-, 4-, 5-finger hands from only hundreds of attempts on 40 objects, generalizing to 150 novel objects with human-infant-level efficiency.
- **[[2411.16755|FunGrasp]]** — An end-to-end system for ==one-shot functional grasping== across diverse dexterous hands, pairing a ==human-to-robot grasp-retargeting== module (posture + contact from one RGBD image) with an RL controller and ==privileged-learning== sim-to-real; **74%** real on 20 unseen objects, **75–85%** sim across Shadow/Faive/Allegro hands.
- **[[2410.02479|Cross-Embodiment-DexGrasp]]** — A multi-task RL method (CrossDex) with a universal ==human eigengrasp action space== + a geometry-only ==unified observation space== (fingertip/palm positions), co-trained across hands then distilled to vision; **80%** SR across 4 trained hands, **35.2%** zero-shot to unseen LEAP/Inspire hands, beating all baselines.
- **[[2410.01702|DR,O-Grasp]]** — An ==interaction-centric== representation as a robot-to-object point-distance matrix with ==configuration-invariant contrastive pretraining== of the hand encoder, decoded to joints via multilateration + SVD; **87.53%** sim / **89%** real on novel objects in **<1 s**, robust to 50% occlusion across 3 dexterous hands.

#### 7.5 Benchmarks & Diagnostic Evaluation

Every method above is only as trustworthy as the benchmark that measures it — these suites stress-test compositional generalization, low-level physical reasoning, long-horizon control, dexterity, soft-grasping, and continual learning, exposing where today's policies actually break.

- **[[2608.11769|HandPriorScore]]** — Diagnoses a ==policy-induced hand prior== in GR00T-based humanoid dual-arm manipulation via ==HandPriorScore== + ==bidirectional joint-swap counterfactuals== localizing bias to two right-arm joints; pose diversification lifts SR **5.8%→63.3%**.
- **[[2608.04425|SSC]]** — A ==Structured Subtask Chain== representation modeling demonstrations as alternating scene-graph states + actions, verified by a three-stage labelling pipeline including a ==state-resolution cascade==; top VL models hit **86.23%** accuracy on a bimanual "trimmed-hard" subset, the checker flagging **31** anomalies (**1.3%**) across 2,357 BEHAVIOR-1K action cells.
- **[[2607.23108|Curse of Precision]]** — Establishes two ==data scaling laws== for high-precision manipulation: a ==power-law== failure-rate law and a ==super-exponential== precision law bounded by a ==limit precision c==, measured on ==ManiSkill3== Diffusion Policies; **R²>0.968** across tasks, dropping the wrist camera moves c **2.35 → 3.85 mm** while cleaner experts reach **1.27 mm**.
- **[[2606.31494|Manipulation Robustness Survey]]** — Survey: a task-centered four-dimensional definition of manipulation robustness (goal/challenge/mechanism/evaluation) formalized via a ==unified partially-observable stochastic control model==, taxonomizing mechanisms across perception/planning/control/policy-learning/hardware and existing evaluation protocols.
- **[[2606.23686|LIBERO-Safety]]** — A benchmark for physical + semantic VLA safety pairing a parametric UBDDL environment + safety-centric taxonomy with a ==keypose-driven data generator== of ~20,000 collision-free demos for zero-shot/OOD eval; OpenVLA near-collapses on OOD physical suites, while scaling demos jointly raises success and cuts collisions, exposing OOD spatial gaps.
- **[[2606.16826|ATOM-Bench]]** — A real-world benchmark factorizing tabletop manipulation into 15 ==motor atoms== + instruction atoms with **3,000** demos and Atomic-Score / Compositional-Failure-Share metrics; π0.5 hits **83.3%** atomic but only **15.8%** compositional SR, exposing that policies ground instructions yet fail to compose learned atomic skills.
- **[[2606.11901|DuoBench]]** — A reproducible ==bimanual manipulation benchmark== (11 sim + 4 real FR3-Duo tasks) over a four-type coordination taxonomy with ==stage-based failure analysis== and 750 teleop episodes; ACT/π0.5/X-VLA reach near-**0%** on hard tasks (Hinge-Chest **0%**, Bin-Sort **0–2%**), failing mostly at initial grasp, with a persistent sim-to-real gap.
- **[[2606.04206|DLO-Lab]]** — A ==differentiable deformable-linear-object simulator== (Discrete Elastic Rods) with bending plasticity + two-way rigid/soft coupling and a 10-task benchmark, plus a ==VLM-grasp-proposal DLO agent==; CMA-ES beats model-free/first-order RL on non-smooth DLO landscapes, **~58%** zero-shot sim-to-real Wiring-ring, **60.0%** fine-tuned multi-task VLA.
- **[[2605.18617|ManiSoft]]** — A ==vision-language manipulation benchmark for soft continuum robots== pairing a hybrid ==Cosserat-rod + MuJoCo + Blender simulator== with four language-conditioned tasks and a planner/RL-executor demo generator; Diffusion Policy and OpenVLA-OFT reach only **~30%** SR, exposing visual-proprioceptive ambiguity and unexploited soft-arm compliance.
- **[[2604.09294|POMDAR]]** — A performance-based dexterity benchmark of **18 tasks** from ==human grasp taxonomies== with 3D-printable scaffolded components + MuJoCo and a throughput score (80% correctness / 20% speed vs human); across 4 ORCA-hand embodiments dexterity scores rise with DoF count, largest gaps on coordinated multi-finger tasks.
- **[[2604.04974|Video-to-Control-Survey]]** — Survey: an ==interface-centric taxonomy== classifying non-action-annotated-video methods by where structure enters the control stack — ==Direct Video-Action Policies==, ==Latent-Action Methods==, ==Explicit Visual Interfaces== — analyzing ==control-loop closure== and grounding failures; names the "integration layer" a critical bottleneck.
- **[[2603.06749|RFM Industrial Readiness Survey]]** — Survey: derives an 11-implication, 47-attribute, ==149-criterion industrial-readiness framework== and evaluates **324** manipulation-capable Robotic Foundation Models via a conservative ==LLM-based (GPT-5.1) scoring pipeline==; even the top model scores only **0.11-0.12/1.0**, with Safety & Compliance and Real-Time Performance most neglected.
- **[[2511.02097|WM-Manipulation-Survey]]** — Survey: a multi-dimensional taxonomy of world models for robotic manipulation across paradigm (==Implicit/Latent-Dynamics/Video-Generation==), architecture, and function (decision support vs training facilitation), proposing 13 core capabilities (imagination, physics-awareness, counterfactual/causal reasoning) as the field's aspirational blueprint.
- **[[2510.10903|Manipulation Survey 2025]]** — Survey: aggregates the field into unified taxonomies for ==high-level planning== (language, code, motion, affordance, 3D representations), a novel ==low-level learning-based control== breakdown (learning strategy, input, latent learning, policy architecture), and the field's first ==data & generalization bottleneck== taxonomy, over 1200 references.
- **[[2509.19524|StepEval]]** — A modular cost-aware framework using off-the-shelf ==VLMs as black-box subgoal judges== that infer per-subgoal outcomes zero/few-shot from recorded trajectories, outputting a ==trajectory-level vector of per-subgoal success rates== instead of a single binary task SR; a plug-and-play four-step workflow giving granular diagnostic resolution.
- **[[2505.14986|AnyBody]]** — A ==cross-embodiment manipulation benchmark== in Isaac-Sim with 18 procedural + real robot variations on reach/push and explicit ==interpolation / extrapolation / composition== axes; multi-embodiment policies match single-embodiment on seen robots and interpolation but collapse to **0%** on extrapolation (Arms-push), exposing the morphology-generalization wall.
- **[[2505.14030|AutoBio]]** — A ==MuJoCo-based biology-lab simulation benchmark== with custom physics plugins (threaded caps, detent, liquid handling) + 3DGS/PBR photorealistic transparent-material rendering over 16 biologically-grounded tasks; π0 and RDT VLAs drop sharply on medium/hard tasks, failing at precision, transparent-liquid sensing, and dynamic-display grounding.
- **[[2505.11032|DexGarmentLab]]** — A ==dexterous bimanual garment-manipulation environment== (15 tasks) with an automated single-example data pipeline exploiting garment structural correspondence + the ==HALO== policy (Garment Affordance Model + Structure-Aware Diffusion Policy); **0.82** Fling-Dress SR (vs Diffusion Policy's 0.59), real 13/15 Fold-Tops, generalizing across garment shapes.
- **[[2505.09698|ManipBench]]** — A **12,617**-MCQ benchmark probing VLMs' ==low-level physical reasoning== (keypoint/trajectory selection) across pick-place, articulated, deformable, and dynamic tasks with CoT prompting; Gemini-2.5-pro leads, and MCQ accuracy correlates **0.889** with real-robot SR (0.950 on fabric), validating it as a diagnostic.
- **[[2504.08438|Diffusion-for-Manipulation-Survey]]** — Survey: the first comprehensive taxonomy of ==Diffusion Models== in robotic manipulation, classifying by network architecture (U-Net/Transformer/MLP), learning framework (IL/offline-RL), and application (trajectory generation, grasp synthesis, data augmentation), naming sampling speed and OOD generalization as open problems.
- **[[2602.23408|Action-Space-Design]]** — A large-scale empirical study formalizing ==spatial (joint vs task-space)== and ==temporal (absolute vs delta, chunking)== abstraction axes over **13,000+** real rollouts and **500+** models; chunk-wise delta beats step-wise by **>10%**, joint-space wins overall but task-space dominates cross-embodiment transfer.
- **[[2412.18194|VLABench]]** — A large-scale ==language-conditioned manipulation benchmark== of 100 task categories / 2000+ objects probing world-knowledge transfer, implicit-goal understanding, and ==long-horizon reasoning==; pre-trained VLAs score **<20%** on most generalization tests and workflow approaches only 30–40%, exposing how far multi-step reasoning lags in current policies.
- **[[2412.13211|MS-HAB]]** — A ==GPU-accelerated== ManiSkill3 port of Habitat's Home-Assistant Benchmark replacing magical grasp with realistic low-level control over 3 long-horizon rearrangement tasks + rule-based trajectory filtering; **4299 SPS** (3.08× over Habitat 2.0) at 32% less memory, with long-horizon SR still <20% exposing the control gap.
- **[[2503.03464|GenAI-in-Manipulation-Survey]]** — Survey: organizes generative AI's role in manipulation into a three-layered ==Foundation / Intermediate / Policy== hierarchy — data + reward generation, language/code/visual generation, then generative ==grasp and trajectory== policies (VAE, ==diffusion==, pretrained VLA) — synthesizing how each model family attacks data scarcity and planning.
- **[[2503.02834|MuBlE]]** — A ==MuJoCo + Blender== simulation environment pairing accurate physics with photorealistic rendering, plus the SHOP-VRB2 benchmark (12,000 scenes) for closed-loop reasoning over visual and non-visual attributes; baseline CLIER hits **43.9%** sim, and Blender-fidelity training is critical for **64.4%** real Franka transfer.
- **[[2411.19408|SoGraB]]** — A visual soft-grasping benchmark scoring grasp success, holding time, and ==Density-Aware Chamfer Distance== object deformation into one quality scalar from ungrasped/grasped point clouds; over **900** grasps it spans **0.517–0.940** quality, categorizing three stiffness regimes where soft grippers reduce deformation.
- **[[2408.11537|Object-Centric-Manipulation-Survey]]** — Survey: categorizes embodied learning for object-centric manipulation into ==Embodied Perceptual Learning== (representation, pose, affordance), ==Embodied Policy Learning== (representation, RL/IL), and ==Embodied Task-Oriented Learning== (grasping, manipulation), tracking post-2021 LLM/NeRF/==Diffusion== influence across each branch.
- **[[2402.17767|MOSART]]** — A modular mobile-manipulation pipeline for zero-shot opening of articulated structures, pairing an ==Articulation-parameter Prediction Module== (Mask R-CNN + depth lifting) with ==SeqIK== trajectory optimization and ==contact-based correction==; **61%** SR on 31 novel cabinets, **13/20 vs 6/20** beating a 1000-demo IL baseline, **59%** of failures from perception.
- **[[2402.08191|THE-COLOSSEUM]]** — A simulation benchmark (extending RLBench) stress-testing manipulation policies against ==14 perturbation factors== (object/background/physical) across 20 tasks; individual perturbations cause **30-50%** degradation, combined **≥75%**, with sim-real degradation correlating at **R²=0.614**; 3D models prove most robust, exposing the generalization wall.
- **[[2402.02500|Point-Cloud-Matters]]** — An observation-space diagnostic (OBSBench) comparing RGB / RGB-D / point-cloud inputs across ==125 contact-rich tasks== over two simulators with standardized encoders + PVRs; point clouds win mean SR by up to **+76.92%** with Diffusion Policy and generalize best zero-shot to view/lighting shifts, establishing observation space as a first-order bottleneck.
- **[[2311.18044|Robotics Transfer Learning Survey]]** — Survey: unifies transfer learning in robotics under one definition and the first taxonomy over three modes — robot, environment, task — yielding single-, dual-, and triple-mode categories plus an abstraction-level stack from mimicry to verbal instruction; a **44,067**-paper ICRA/IROS sweep flags negative transfer as under-studied.
- **[[2304.04321|ARNOLD]]** — A language-grounded benchmark for ==continuous object states== (e.g. "75% full") over 8 manipulation tasks in photorealistic Isaac-Sim/PhysX with 10K expert demos + systematic generalization splits; PerAct's 3D voxel grid beats 2D 6D-CLIPort, but all models collapse on the Novel-State split, exposing poor continuous-goal generalization.
- **[[2303.16958|PartManip]]** — A large-scale cross-category part-manipulation benchmark (494 objects, 11 categories, 1432 tasks) over sparse-view point clouds, with a two-stage ==part-canonicalized== expert distilled via ==Behavior Cloning + DAgger== and ==domain adversarial learning== into a vision-based student; **49.1%** OpenDoor vs baseline's **26.9%**.
- **[[2210.13066|DaXBench]]** — A ==differentiable JAX simulator== + benchmark for deformable manipulation (liquid, rope, cloth, elasto-plastic) wrapping planning/IL/RL tasks with Lazy-Dynamic-Update + checkpointing; differentiable RL (APG) and ILD beat PPO/Transporter on long-horizon low-level control with fewer demos, validated by a real Kinova rope-push, exposing where diff-physics helps.
- **[[2206.08522|VLMbench]]** — A ==compositional vision-language manipulation benchmark== auto-generating diverse 6-DoF tasks + instructions via AMSolver (rule-based unit tasks), with a keypoint-based 6D-CLIPort baseline; precise 6-DoF orientation tasks (Pour) score near-zero and ground-truth-position ablations show object localization is the dominant bottleneck for multimodal policies.
- **[[2105.10919|Continual-World]]** — A ==continual-RL benchmark== of 10/20 Meta-World manipulation tasks (CW10/CW20/Triplets) ordered to probe forward and negative transfer, with **seven** CL methods over SAC; PackNet leads at **0.80** CW20 SR, while regularization methods curb forgetting but suppress forward transfer below the **0.46** reference.
- **[[2001.03070|In-Hand-Manipulation-Benchmark]]** — A ==platform-agnostic benchmarking protocol== for in-hand manipulation defining three difficulty levels (hand pose / contact region / both) with quantitative pose + contact-region error metrics (Euclidean/geodesic) and timing; baselines span **0% drop / 1.32 cm** position error to 9% drop, standardizing dexterous-vs-gripper comparison.

**Demonstration & Transfer — Decision Matrix**

| Need | Recommendation |
|---|---|
| Cheap human-only data collection | [[2403.07788\|DexCap]] (**0.8 cm** drift), [[2604.08534\|ActiveGlasses]] |
| Self-supervised sensorimotor pretraining | [[2306.10007\|RPT]] (**68.8%** cross-lab) |
| Language-annotated corpus at scale | [[2210.06407\|Language-Table]] (**93.5%**) |
| Skill extraction from offline data | [[2406.17768\|EXTRACT]] (**10×** efficiency), [[2509.22149\|DemoGrasp]] (1 demo) |
| Massive synthetic grasp data | [[2505.03233\|SynGrasp-1B]] (**~90%** zero-shot) |
| LLM-generated demonstration data | [[2507.00833\|HumanoidGen]] (**>50%**) |
| Self-improving data flywheel | [[2509.23829\|DexFlyWheel]] (**16.5→81.9%**), [[2512.02729\|RoboWheel]] |
| Sim+real co-trained transfer | [[2605.26638\|HyperSim]] (**95%** at 35 demos) |
| Human-video → dexterous-hand transfer | [[2603.22264\|UniDex]] (**81%**, 5.2× cheaper), [[2511.09484\|SPIDER]] |
| Add new sensor modalities to a generalist | [[2501.04693\|FuSe]] (**>60%**) |
| Cross-embodiment reachability transfer | [[2604.06778\|RichMap]] (**+10.97%**) |

^dm-7

> [!star] Key Papers — Data & Transfer Landmarks
> - [[1810.07121|MIME]] — an early large-scale paired human-video + robot-demonstration dataset that seeded the visual-imitation-from-human-video line.
> - [[2403.07788|DexCap]] — the portable-mocap landmark proving high-quality dexterous policies can be learned from human-only data with zero on-robot collection.
> - [[2505.03233|SynGrasp-1B]] — demonstrated that a billion-frame synthetic dataset plus a VLA yields ~90% zero-shot real grasping, validating synthetic-data-at-scale.
> - [[2603.22264|UniDex]] — the cross-embodiment landmark: a function-aligned action space lets one human-video-trained policy transfer zero-shot across dexterous hands.
> - [[2306.10007|RPT]] — established masked sensorimotor pretraining as a transferable representation that scales with data and encoder size.

^key-papers-7

> [!tip] The Cheapest Demonstration Is the One You Don't Collect on the Robot
> Data is the highest-leverage axis in this deep-dive — a 10× cheaper source uplifts every method above it — and the field is racing to push collection *off the target robot*. Onto humans ([[2403.07788|DexCap]], [[2604.08534|ActiveGlasses]], [[2603.22264|UniDex]]), onto simulators ([[2505.03233|SynGrasp-1B]], [[2507.00833|HumanoidGen]], [[2605.26638|HyperSim]]), and onto other embodiments ([[2604.06778|RichMap]], [[2604.15215|HiST-AT]]). Each strategy trades a fidelity gap (human-robot embodiment mismatch, sim-real gap, cross-morphology gap) for a massive cost reduction, then spends a thin layer of real data closing the residual. The strategic read: your data strategy *is* your manipulation strategy — pick the off-robot source whose fidelity gap your downstream method can absorb. For the sim-side of synthetic data, see [[15_Sim-to-Real-Transfer#2. Sim-Side: Learned & Procedural Simulators]]; for the egocentric-human-video pretraining substrate, see [[14_Egocentric-Pretraining-and-Human-Video#5. Transfer Mechanisms — Hand → Gripper]].

^insight-7

## Quick-Reference Matrix

| Question | Answer |
|---|---|
| Why is naive regression a bad policy backbone? | It averages multimodal actions into motion that satisfies no mode — diffusion/flow fix this (§1). |
| What's the real constraint on generative policies now? | Inference latency, not expressivity — every §1 paper fights the iteration loop ([[2602.07322\|A2A]] at **0.56 ms**). |
| Two routes to 3D understanding? | Bake geometry into the policy ([[2403.03954\|DP3]]) or query a spatial-reasoning VLM ([[2511.05491\|VST]]); the VLM route now feeds the policy route (§2). |
| What should a world model predict? | The *task-relevant* future (masks, keyframes, flow), not every pixel — predict-less generalizes better OOD (§3). |
| Biggest enemy in long-horizon control? | Visual aliasing — structure (episodic anchors, object beliefs) beats window-length (§4). |
| Why borrow LLM/VLM reasoning instead of training it? | Semantic generalization is expensive from demos, cheap to borrow — but needs a grounding harness (§5). |
| The dominant RL strategy for manipulation? | RL the *correction* (latent/residual/impedance), not the whole policy — keep the frozen backbone (§6). |
| Highest-leverage axis in the whole stack? | Data — push collection off the robot onto humans, sims, and other embodiments (§7). |
| Cheapest path to a dexterous policy? | Human-only mocap ([[2403.07788\|DexCap]], **72%** from 30 min) or billion-frame synthetic ([[2505.03233\|SynGrasp-1B]], **~90%** zero-shot). |
| How do the three axes relate? | Backbone (Part A) × memory/reasoning (Part B) × learning signal (Part C) are independent design axes; the frontier is their interaction. |

## Cross-References

- [[04_VLA]] — The VLA umbrella: manipulation skill learning is the substrate beneath the VLA headline; §2/§3/§6 here detail the spatial, world-model, and RL components VLAs compose. *See [[04_VLA#1. Design-Space Principles]].*
- [[06_WAM]] — World-action-model design space; §3's manipulation world models draw their architectures from the WAM lineage. *See [[06_WAM#6. Efficient & Action-Centered WAMs]].*
- [[07_Latent-World-Models]] — The JEPA/latent-prediction theory behind §3's "predict less" bet. *See [[07_Latent-World-Models#1. The JEPA Principle]].*
- [[05_VLA-Reasoning-and-CoT]] — Reasoning/CoT trained *into* VLAs, complementing §5's borrowed-foundation-model reasoning. *See [[05_VLA-Reasoning-and-CoT#5. Reasoning-Traced Training]].*
- [[14_Egocentric-Pretraining-and-Human-Video]] — The human-video pretraining substrate behind §7.3's cross-embodiment transfer. *See [[14_Egocentric-Pretraining-and-Human-Video#5. Transfer Mechanisms — Hand → Gripper]].*
- [[11_Contact-Rich-and-Tactile-Control]] — Force/tactile conditioning that the §1 backbones and §6 contact-rich control absorb. *See [[11_Contact-Rich-and-Tactile-Control#3. Force-Conditioned VLA Architectures]].*
- [[15_Sim-to-Real-Transfer]] — The sim-to-real gap that §6's RL and §7's synthetic data must survive. *See [[15_Sim-to-Real-Transfer#3. Policy-Side: Robustness & Domain Randomization]].*
